AlbumFill: Album-Guided Reasoning and Retrieval for Personalized Image Completion
Authors: Yu-Ju Tsai, Brian Price, Qing Liu, Luis Figueroa, Daniil Pakhomov, Zhihong Ding, Scott Cohen, Ming-Hsuan Yang
First: 2026-05-04T17:59:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T17:59:59+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://liagm.github.io/AlbumFill/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Personalized image completion aims to restore occluded regions in personal photos while preserving identity and appearance. Existing methods either rely on generic inpainting models that often fail to maintain identity consistency, or assume that suitable reference images are explicitly provided. In practice, suitable references are often not explicitly provided, requiring the system to search for identity-consistent images within personal photo collections. We present AlbumFill, a training-free framework that retrieves identity-consistent references from personal albums for personalized completion. Given an occluded image and a personal album, a vision-language model infers missing semantic cues to guide composed image retrieval, and the retrieved references are used by reference-based completion models. To facilitate this task, we introduce a dataset containing 54K human-centric samples with associated album images. Experiments across multiple baselines demonstrate the difficulty of personalized completion and highlight the importance of identity-consistent reference retrieval. Project Page: https://liagm.github.io/AlbumFill/
中文标题/摘要
标题：AlbumFill：基于相册引导的推理与检索的个性化图像补全
个性化图像补全旨在恢复个人照片中被遮挡的区域，同时保持身份和外观的一致性。现有方法要么依赖通用的图像修复模型，往往难以维持身份一致性，要么假设提供合适的参考图像。实际上，合适的参考图像通常未明确提供，系统需在个人照片集合中搜索身份一致的图像。我们提出AlbumFill，一个无需训练的框架，从个人相册中检索身份一致的参考图像用于个性化补全。给定一张遮挡图像和个人相册，视觉-语言模型推断缺失的语义线索以引导组合图像检索，检索到的参考图像用于基于参考的补全模型。为支持该任务，我们引入一个包含54K个以人为中心样本及其关联相册图像的数据集。在多个基线模型上的实验表明个性化补全的难度，并突显了身份一致参考图像检索的重要性。项目页面：https://liagm.github.io/AlbumFill/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of restoring occluded regions in personal photos while preserving identity, AlbumFill introduces a training-free framework that retrieves identity-consistent reference images from personal photo albums without requiring explicit user-provided references. It leverages a vision-language model to infer missing semantic cues from the occluded image and guide image retrieval, then uses retrieved references to drive completion via reference-based models. Evaluated on a 54K-sample dataset, AlbumFill outperforms existing methods by significantly improving identity consistency and completion quality, demonstrating the critical role of accurate reference retrieval in personalized image completion.
为解决在个人照片中修复遮挡区域同时保持身份一致性的问题，AlbumFill提出了一种无需训练的框架，通过从个人相册中检索身份一致的参考图像来引导个性化图像补全。该方法利用视觉-语言模型从遮挡图像和相册中推断缺失语义线索，进而检索相关参考图像，再由基于参考的补全模型处理。实验表明，身份一致的参考图像检索显著优于通用图像修复方法，且所构建的54K个人中心样本数据集有效验证了该任务的难度。
SurgTEMP: Temporal-Aware Surgical Video Question Answering with Text-guided Visual Memory for Laparoscopic Cholecystectomy
Authors: Shi Li, Vinkle Srivastav, Nicolas Chanel, Saurav Sharma, Nabani Banik, Lorenzo Arboit, Kun Yuan, Pietro Mascagni, Nicolas Padoy
First: 2026-03-31T16:27:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T17:55:47+00:00
Comments: 29 pages, 14 figures, 9 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Surgical procedures are inherently complex and risky, requiring extensive expertise and constant focus to navigate evolving intraoperative scenes. Computer-assisted systems such as surgical visual question answering (VQA) offer promises for education and intraoperative support. Current surgical VQA research largely focuses on static frame analysis, overlooking rich temporal semantics. Surgical video question answering is further challenged by low visual contrast, its highly knowledge-driven nature, diverse analytical needs spanning scattered temporal windows, and the hierarchy from basic perception to high-level intraoperative assessment. To address these challenges, we propose SurgTEMP, a multimodal LLM framework featuring (i) a query-guided token selection module that builds hierarchical visual memory (spatial and temporal memory banks) and (ii) a Surgical Competency Progression (SCP) training scheme. Together, they enable effective modeling of variable-length surgical videos while preserving procedure-relevant cues and temporal coherence, and better support diverse downstream assessment tasks. To support model development, we introduce CholeVidQA-32K, a surgical video question answering dataset comprising 32K open-ended QA pairs and 3,855 video segments (approximately 128 h total) from laparoscopic cholecystectomy. The dataset is organized into a three-level hierarchy -- Perception, Assessment, and Reasoning -- spanning 11 tasks from instrument/action/anatomy perception to Critical View of Safety (CVS), intraoperative difficulty, skill proficiency, and adverse event assessment. In comprehensive evaluations against state-of-the-art open-source multimodal and video LLMs (fine-tuned and zero-shot), SurgTEMP achieves substantial performance improvements, advancing the state of video-based surgical VQA. The project page is available at: https://camma-public.github.io/SurgTEMP/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for better intraoperative support and surgical education, this work addresses limitations in current surgical video question answering systems that neglect temporal dynamics and visual context. SurgTEMP introduces a multimodal LLM framework with a query-guided token selection module that constructs hierarchical visual memory banks (spatial and temporal) and a Surgical Competency Progression training scheme to handle variable-length videos while preserving key cues and temporal coherence. Evaluated on CholeVidQA-32K — a 32K QA dataset from laparoscopic cholecystectomy organized into Perception, Assessment, and Reasoning levels — SurgTEMP outperforms state-of-the-art multimodal and video LLMs in both fine-tuned and zero-shot settings, demonstrating superior performance across diverse surgical assessment tasks.
为应对现有手术视频问答系统忽视时序语义、难以处理低对比度图像及多层级评估需求的问题，作者提出SurgTEMP框架，结合基于查询的视觉令牌选择模块构建空间与时间双层视觉记忆库，并引入手术能力进展训练方案。在包含32K问答对的胆囊切除术视频数据集CholeVidQA-32K上，该框架在细调与零样本测试中均显著优于当前主流多模态及视频大模型，有效建模变长手术视频并保持时序连贯性与关键手术线索，支持从感知到推理的多层次评估任务。
Attribution-Guided Pruning for Insight and Control: Circuit Discovery and Targeted Correction in Small-scale LLMs
Authors: Sayed Mohammad Vakilzadeh Hatefi, Maximilian Dreyer, Reduan Achtibat, Patrick Kahardipraja, Thomas Wiegand, Wojciech Samek, Alexander Binder, Sebastian Lapuschkin
First: 2025-06-16T17:38:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T17:55:23+00:00
Comments: Work in progress (9 pages manuscript, 3 pages references, 16 pages appendix)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) are widely deployed in real-world applications, yet their internal mechanisms remain difficult to interpret and control, limiting our ability to diagnose and correct undesirable behaviors. Mechanistic interpretability addresses this challenge by identifying circuits -- subsets of model components responsible for specific behaviors. However, discovering such circuits in LLMs remains difficult due to their scale and complexity. We propose an attribution-guided pruning approach for circuit discovery based on Layer-wise Relevance Propagation (LRP). By attributing model outputs to internal components using task-specific reference samples, we identify behaviorally relevant parameters and extract sparse functional circuits. Building on this, we introduce contrastive relevance to isolate circuits associated with undesired behaviors while preserving general capabilities, enabling targeted model correction. On OPT-125M, removing only 100 neurons (0.3%) significantly reduces toxic outputs, while pruning approximately 0.03% of weight elements mitigates repetitive text generation without degrading general performance. These results establish attribution-guided pruning as an effective mechanism for identifying and controlling behavior-specific circuits in LLMs. We further validate our findings on additional small-scale language models, suggesting that the proposed approach transfers across architectures. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/erfanhatefi/SparC3.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于归因引导的剪枝用于洞察与控制：小规模LLM中的电路发现与针对性修正
大型语言模型（LLMs）已在实际应用中广泛部署，但其内部机制难以解释和控制，限制了我们诊断和修正不良行为的能力。机制可解释性通过识别电路——负责特定行为的模型组件子集来应对这一挑战。然而，由于LLMs规模庞大且复杂，发现此类电路仍十分困难。我们提出一种基于层间相关性传播（LRP）的归因引导剪枝方法，通过使用任务特定参考样本将模型输出归因于内部组件，识别行为相关参数并提取稀疏功能电路。在此基础上，我们引入对比相关性，以隔离与不良行为相关的电路，同时保留通用能力，实现针对性模型修正。在OPT-125M模型上，仅移除100个神经元（0.3%）即可显著减少毒性输出，而剪枝约0.03%的权重元素可缓解重复文本生成，且不损害通用性能。这些结果确立了归因引导剪枝作为识别和控制LLM中行为特定电路的有效机制。我们在其他小型语言模型上进一步验证了该方法，表明其可跨架构迁移。代码已公开于https://github.com/erfanhatefi/SparC3。
Summary / 总结
To address the lack of interpretability and control in large language models, this work proposes an attribution-guided pruning method using Layer-wise Relevance Propagation to identify behaviorally relevant circuits. By attributing outputs to internal parameters using task-specific reference samples, the method extracts sparse functional circuits and employs contrastive relevance to isolate undesirable behaviors while preserving general capabilities. Experiments on OPT-125M show that removing just 100 neurons (0.3%) reduces toxic outputs, and pruning 0.03% of weights mitigates repetition without harming overall performance, demonstrating the method’s effectiveness for targeted correction in small-scale LLMs.
为解决大语言模型内部机制难以解释和控制的问题，本文提出一种基于层间相关性传播的归因引导剪枝方法，通过任务特定参考样本归因输出到内部参数，提取稀疏功能电路，并利用对比相关性隔离不良行为同时保留通用能力。在OPT-125M模型上实验表明，仅移除100个神经元（0.3%）即可显著降低毒性输出，剪枝0.03%的权重可缓解重复生成而不影响整体性能，验证了该方法在小型语言模型中对特定行为电路进行精准修正的有效性。
SpecKV: Adaptive Speculative Decoding with Compression-Aware Gamma Selection
Authors: Shikhar Shukla
First: 2026-05-04T17:55:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T17:55:05+00:00
Comments: 11 pages, 8 figures, 7 tables. Code and data available at: https://github.com/Amorfati123/SpecKV
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Speculative decoding accelerates large language model (LLM) inference by using a small draft model to propose candidate tokens that a larger target model verifies. A critical hyperparameter in this process is the speculation length~$γ$, which determines how many tokens the draft model proposes per step. Nearly all existing systems use a fixed~$γ$ (typically~4), yet empirical evidence suggests that the optimal value varies across task types and, crucially, depends on the compression level applied to the target model. In this paper, we present \textbf{SpecKV}, a lightweight adaptive controller that selects~$γ$ per speculation step using signals extracted from the draft model itself. We profile speculative decoding across 4~task categories, 4~speculation lengths, and 3~compression levels (FP16, INT8, NF4), collecting 5,112 step-level records with per-step acceptance rates, draft entropy, and draft confidence. We demonstrate that the optimal~$γ$ shifts across compression regimes and that draft model confidence and entropy are strong predictors of acceptance rate (correlation~$\approx 0.56$). SpecKV uses a small MLP trained on these signals to maximize expected tokens per speculation step, achieving a 56.0\% improvement over the fixed-$γ$=4 baseline with only 0.34\,ms overhead per decision ($<$0.5\% of step time). The improvement is statistically significant ($p < 0.001$, paired bootstrap test). We release all profiling data, trained models, and notebooks as open-source artifacts.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that fixed speculation lengths in speculative decoding perform suboptimally across varying compression levels and task types, this paper introduces SpecKV, a lightweight adaptive controller that dynamically selects the speculation length γ per step using signals from the draft model. By profiling 5,112 step-level records across four tasks, four γ values, and three compression levels, the authors find that γ’s optimal value shifts with compression and that draft model confidence and entropy strongly predict token acceptance (correlation ~0.56). SpecKV employs a small MLP trained on these signals to maximize expected tokens per step, achieving a 56.0% improvement over the fixed γ=4 baseline with only 0.34ms overhead per decision, a statistically significant gain (p < 0.001).
鉴于现有系统在推测解码中使用固定推测长度γ（通常为4）无法适应不同压缩级别和任务类型，作者提出SpecKV，一种轻量级自适应控制器，利用draft模型自身信号动态选择每步的γ值。通过对4类任务、4种γ值和3种压缩级别（FP16、INT8、NF4）的5,112个步骤级记录进行分析，发现最优γ随压缩级别变化，且draft模型置信度和熵能强预测接受率（相关系数≈0.56）。SpecKV采用小型MLP训练这些信号以最大化每步预期token数，在仅增加0.34ms决策开销（小于0.5%步耗时）的情况下，相较固定γ=4基线提升56.0%，且统计显著（p < 0.001）。
Unsupervised Machine Learning for Detecting Structural Anomalies in European Regional Statistics
Authors: Bogdan Oancea
Venue: Romanian Statistical Review, no. 1, pp. 3-22, 2026
First: 2026-05-04T17:54:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T17:54:36+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Ensuring the coherence of regional socio-economic statistics is a central task for national statistical institutes. Traditional validation tools, such as range edits, ratio checks, or univariate outlier detection, are effective for identifying extreme values in individual series but are less suited for detecting unusual combinations of indicators in high-dimensional settings. This paper proposes an unsupervised machine learning framework for identifying structurally atypical regional profiles within Europe using publicly available Eurostat data. We construct a cross-sectional dataset of NUTS2 regions (2022) covering four key indicators: GDP per capita in PPS, unemployment rate, tertiary educational attainment, and population density. We apply and compare five anomaly detection techniques, univariate z-scores, Mahalanobis distance, Isolation Forest, Local Outlier Factor, and One-Class SVM, and classify a region as a structural anomaly if it is flagged by at least three of the five methods. The findings show that machine learning methods identify a consistent set of regions whose multivariate profiles diverge substantially from the EU-wide pattern. These include both highly developed metropolitan economies (Brussels, Vienna, Berlin, Prague) and regions with persistent socio-economic disadvantages (Central and Western Slovakia, Northern Hungary, Castilla-La Mancha, Extremadura), as well as Istanbul, whose profile differs markedly from EU capital regions. Importantly, these anomalies do not necessarily signal data quality issues; rather, they reflect meaningful structural divergence that warrants analytical or policy attention. The proposed framework is fully reproducible, scalable, and compatible with existing validation workflows, offering a flexible tool for early detection of unusual regional configurations within the European Statistical System.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用无监督机器学习检测欧洲区域统计数据中的结构性异常
确保区域社会经济统计数据的一致性是国家统计机构的核心任务。传统验证工具，如范围编辑、比率检查或单变量异常检测，虽能有效识别单个序列中的极端值，但在高维设置中难以检测指标组合的异常。本文提出一种无监督机器学习框架，利用公开的Eurostat数据识别欧洲NUTS2区域中的结构性异常。我们构建了2022年涵盖四个关键指标（按购买力平价计算的人均GDP、失业率、高等教育完成率、人口密度）的横截面数据集。我们应用并比较了五种异常检测方法：单变量z分数、马氏距离、孤立森林、局部异常因子和一类SVM，并将至少三种方法标记为异常的区域归类为结构性异常。研究发现，机器学习方法识别出一组与欧盟整体模式显著偏离的区域，包括高度发达的都市经济体（布鲁塞尔、维也纳、柏林、布拉格）和长期存在社会经济劣势的地区（斯洛伐克中部和西部、匈牙利北部、卡斯蒂利亚-拉曼查、埃斯特雷马杜拉），以及与欧盟首都地区明显不同的伊斯坦布尔。重要的是，这些异常并不一定意味着数据质量问题，而是反映了值得分析或政策关注的结构性差异。该框架完全可复现、可扩展，且兼容现有验证流程，为欧洲统计系统早期发现异常区域配置提供了灵活工具。
Summary / 总结
To address the limitations of traditional statistical validation methods in detecting complex, multivariate structural anomalies in European regional data, this study introduces an unsupervised machine learning framework using Eurostat’s NUTS2 regional indicators. Five anomaly detection algorithms—z-scores, Mahalanobis distance, Isolation Forest, Local Outlier Factor, and One-Class SVM—are applied to a 2022 dataset of four key socio-economic variables across European regions. A region is classified as an anomaly if flagged by at least three methods. The framework identifies consistent structural outliers, including advanced metropolitan areas like Brussels and Vienna, as well as disadvantaged regions such as parts of Slovakia and Hungary, plus Istanbul, whose profiles deviate significantly from EU norms. These anomalies reflect genuine structural differences rather than data errors, and the method offers a scalable, reproducible tool for early detection within national statistical systems.
为解决传统方法难以识别高维数据中多指标异常组合的问题，本研究采用无监督机器学习框架，基于2022年欧盟统计局NUTS2区域数据，分析四个关键指标：人均GDP、失业率、高等教育普及率和人口密度。通过比较五种异常检测方法（z分数、马氏距离、孤立森林、局部异常因子、一类SVM），若至少三种方法标记某区域，则判定其为结构性异常。结果发现，布鲁塞尔、维也纳等发达都市与斯洛伐克中部、匈牙利北部等欠发达地区，以及伊斯坦布尔均被一致识别为异常，这些异常反映的是结构性差异而非数据质量问题，提供了一种可复现、可扩展的早期预警工具，适用于欧洲统计体系。
Agentic Forecasting using Sequential Bayesian Updating of Linguistic Beliefs
Authors: Kevin Murphy
First: 2026-04-20T17:57:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T17:52:42+00:00
Comments: v3 fixes an error in the baseline numbers (results for main method unaffected), adds cross-LLM experiments, and improves the presentation
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present the Bayesian Linguistic Forecaster (BLF), an agentic system for binary forecasting that achieves state-of-the-art performance on the ForecastBench benchmark. The system is built on three ideas. (1) Linguistic belief state: a semi-structured representation combining numerical probability estimates with natural-language evidence summaries, updated by the LLM at each step of an iterative tool-use loop. This contrasts with the common approach of appending all retrieved evidence to an ever-growing, unstructured context. (2) Hierarchical multi-trial aggregation: running $K$ independent trials and combining them using logit-space averaging shrinkage with a data-dependent prior. (3) Hierarchical calibration: Platt scaling with a hierarchical prior, which avoids over-shrinking extreme predictions for sources with skewed base rates. On 400 questions from the ForecastBench leaderboard, BLF outperforms all the top public methods, including Cassi, GPT-5, Grok~4.20, and Foresight-32B. Careful ablation studies, using mixed effects analysis to control for question variability (which accounts for 62\% of the variance in performance), reveals that all 3 components contribute to the overall gains, but some components matter more than others, depending on the base LLM, and the setting (e.g.\ with or without a crowd prior). All our experiments are based on a robust back-testing framework which we develop, which has a leakage rate below 1.5\%, and may be of independent interest.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用语言信念的序贯贝叶斯更新进行代理预测
我们提出了贝叶斯语言预测器（BLF），这是一个用于二元预测的代理系统，在ForecastBench基准测试中达到最先进性能。该系统基于三个核心思想：（1）语言信念状态：一种结合数值概率估计与自然语言证据摘要的半结构化表示，在迭代工具使用循环的每一步由大语言模型更新，这与将所有检索证据追加到不断增长的无结构上下文的常见方法形成对比。（2）分层多试验聚合：运行K个独立试验，并使用对数空间平均收缩与数据依赖先验进行组合。（3）分层校准：使用分层先验的Platt缩放，避免对基率偏斜的来源过度收缩极端预测。在ForecastBench排行榜的400个问题上，BLF优于所有顶级公开方法，包括Cassi、GPT-5、Grok~4.20和Foresight-32B。通过混合效应分析控制问题变异性（占性能方差的62%）的细致消融研究显示，三个组件均对整体性能提升有贡献，但其重要性取决于基础大语言模型和设置（例如是否使用群体先验）。所有实验均基于我们开发的稳健回测框架，其泄漏率低于1.5%，可能具有独立研究价值。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for more accurate and interpretable binary forecasting, the authors introduce the Bayesian Linguistic Forecaster (BLF), an agentic system that combines numerical probability estimates with natural-language evidence summaries in a dynamic, iterative belief update process. The method employs hierarchical multi-trial aggregation using logit-space averaging with a data-dependent prior and hierarchical calibration via Platt scaling with a hierarchical prior to avoid over-shrinking extreme predictions. Evaluated on 400 ForecastBench questions, BLF outperforms top public models including Cassi, GPT-5, Grok-4.20, and Foresight-32B, with ablation studies showing all three components contribute meaningfully, though their impact varies by LLM and setting. Experiments are conducted under a robust back-testing framework with leakage below 1.5%.
为提升二元预测的准确性和可解释性，作者提出贝叶斯语言预测器（BLF），该系统将数值概率估计与自然语言证据摘要结合，形成半结构化的信念状态，并通过LLM在迭代工具使用循环中逐步更新。方法包含分层多轮聚合（使用对数空间平均与数据依赖先验）和分层校准（使用分层先验的Platt缩放，避免对偏态基率来源的极端预测过度收缩）。在400个ForecastBench问题上，BLF超越包括Cassi、GPT-5、Grok-4.20和Foresight-32B在内的所有顶级公开方法，消融实验表明三部分均贡献性能提升，但其重要性随LLM和设置（如是否使用群体先验）而异。所有实验基于泄漏率低于1.5%的稳健回测框架进行。
Zero-Shot Adaptation of Behavioral Foundation Models to Unseen Dynamics
Authors: Maksim Bobrin, Ilya Zisman, Alexander Nikulin, Vladislav Kurenkov, Dmitry Dylov
First: 2025-05-19T14:12:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T17:51:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Behavioral Foundation Models (BFMs) proved successful in producing policies for arbitrary tasks in a zero-shot manner, requiring no test-time training or task-specific fine-tuning. Among the most promising BFMs are the ones that estimate the successor measure learned in an unsupervised way from task-agnostic offline data. However, these methods fail to react to changes in the dynamics, making them inefficient under partial observability or when the transition function changes. This hinders the applicability of BFMs in a real-world setting, e.g., in robotics, where the dynamics can unexpectedly change at test time. In this work, we demonstrate that Forward-Backward (FB) representation, one of the methods from the BFM family, cannot distinguish between distinct dynamics, leading to an interference among the latent directions, which parametrize different policies. To address this, we propose a FB model with a transformer-based belief estimator, which greatly facilitates zero-shot adaptation. We also show that partitioning the policy encoding space into dynamics-specific clusters, aligned with the context-embedding directions, yields additional gain in performance. These traits allow our method to respond to the dynamics observed during training and to generalize to unseen ones. Empirically, in the changing dynamics setting, our approach achieves up to a 2x higher zero-shot returns compared to the baselines for both discrete and continuous tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：行为基础模型对未见动态的零样本适应
行为基础模型（BFMs）已成功在无需测试时训练或任务特定微调的情况下，为任意任务生成策略。其中最有前景的BFMs是那些从无任务依赖的离线数据中无监督学习到的后继测度估计方法。然而，这些方法无法应对动态变化，导致在部分可观测或转移函数改变时效率低下，从而限制了BFMs在现实世界场景（如机器人）中的应用，因为在测试时动态可能意外变化。本文表明，BFM家族中的一种方法——前向-后向（FB）表示，无法区分不同动态，导致参数化不同策略的潜在方向之间产生干扰。为解决此问题，我们提出一种基于Transformer信念估计器的FB模型，极大促进零样本适应。此外，我们还表明，将策略编码空间按与上下文嵌入方向对齐的动态特定聚类划分，可进一步提升性能。这些特性使我们的方法能够响应训练期间观察到的动态，并泛化到未见动态。实验证明，在动态变化场景下，我们的方法在离散和连续任务中均比基线方法高出2倍的零样本回报。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for behavioral foundation models to adapt to unseen environmental dynamics without fine-tuning, this work identifies a critical limitation in existing Forward-Backward models: they fail to distinguish between different dynamics, causing latent policy directions to interfere. To resolve this, the authors introduce a transformer-based belief estimator within the FB framework to better infer dynamics, and further partition the policy space into dynamics-specific clusters aligned with context embeddings. Experiments show that this approach enables zero-shot adaptation to novel dynamics, achieving up to twice the return of baselines across both discrete and continuous tasks under changing dynamics.
为解决行为基础模型（BFM）在面对未见或变化动态时缺乏适应能力的问题，本文提出一种基于Transformer信念估计器的前向-后向模型，以实现零样本适应。该方法通过将策略编码空间按上下文嵌入方向划分成动态特定簇，缓解了因动态不可区分导致的潜在策略干扰，使模型能响应训练时观察到的动态并泛化至未见动态。实验表明，该方法在变化动态环境下，相比基线模型在离散与连续任务上均实现最高达两倍的零样本回报。
MolmoAct2: Action Reasoning Models for Real-world Deployment
Authors: Haoquan Fang, Jiafei Duan, Donovan Clay, Sam Wang, Shuo Liu, Weikai Huang, Xiang Fan, Wei-Chuan Tsai, Shirui Chen, Yi Ru Wang, Shanli Xing, Jaemin Cho, Jae Sung Park, Ainaz Eftekhar, Peter Sushko, Karen Farley, Angad Wadhwa, Cole Harrison, Winson Han, Ying-Chun Lee, Eli VanderBilt, Rose Hendrix, Suveen Ellawela, Lucas Ngoo, Joyce Chai, Zhongzheng Ren, Ali Farhadi, Dieter Fox, Ranjay Krishna
First: 2026-05-04T17:51:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T17:51:21+00:00
Comments: 31 pages, project page: https://allenai.org/blog/molmoact2
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models aim to provide a single generalist controller for robots, but today's systems fall short on the criteria that matter for real-world deployment. Frontier models are closed, open-weight alternatives are tied to expensive hardware, reasoning-augmented policies pay prohibitive latency for their grounding, and fine-tuned success rates remain below the threshold for dependable use. We present MolmoAct2, a fully open action reasoning model built for practical deployment, advancing its predecessor along five axes. We introduce MolmoER, a VLM backbone specialized for spatial and embodied reasoning, trained on a 3.3M-sample corpus with a specialize-then-rehearse recipe. We release three new datasets spanning low-to-medium cost platforms, including MolmoAct2-BimanualYAM, 720 hours of teleoperated bimanual trajectories that constitute the largest open bimanual dataset to date, together with quality-filtered Franka (DROID) and SO100/101 subsets. We provide OpenFAST, an open-weight, open-data action tokenizer trained on millions of trajectories across five embodiments. We redesign the architecture to graft a flow-matching continuous-action expert onto a discrete-token VLM via per-layer KV-cache conditioning. Finally, we propose MolmoThink, an adaptive-depth reasoning variant that re-predicts depth tokens only for scene regions that change between timesteps, retaining geometric grounding at a fraction of prior latency. In the most extensive empirical study of any open VLA to date, spanning 7 simulation and real-world benchmarks, MolmoAct2 outperforms strong baselines including Pi-05, while MolmoER surpasses GPT-5 and Gemini Robotics ER-1.5 across 13 embodied-reasoning benchmarks. We release model weights, training code, and complete training data. Project page: https://allenai.org/blog/molmoact2
中文标题/摘要
标题：MolmoAct2：面向真实部署的动作推理模型
视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型旨在为机器人提供单一通用控制器，但当前系统在真实部署所需的关键指标上仍显不足。前沿模型闭源，开源替代方案依赖昂贵硬件，增强推理的策略因接地需求而产生高昂延迟，微调成功率仍低于可靠使用的门槛。我们提出MolmoAct2，一款专为实用部署设计的完全开源动作推理模型，在五个维度上超越其前身。我们引入MolmoER，一种专为空间与具身推理优化的视觉语言模型（VLM）主干，通过“专项训练-再演练”方法在330万样本语料上训练。我们发布三个覆盖低成本至中等成本平台的新数据集，包括MolmoAct2-BimanualYAM（720小时远程操作双臂轨迹，目前最大的开源双臂数据集），以及经过质量筛选的Franka（DROID）和SO100/101子集。我们提供OpenFAST，一种基于百万轨迹、覆盖五种具身形态的开源权重、开源数据的动作分词器。我们重构架构，通过逐层KV缓存条件化，将流匹配连续动作专家嫁接到离散分词VLM上。最后，我们提出MolmoThink，一种自适应深度推理变体，仅在时间步间场景区域发生变化时重新预测深度分词，从而以先前延迟的几分之一保留几何接地能力。在迄今为止最全面的开源VLA实证研究中，覆盖7个仿真与真实世界基准，MolmoAct2超越包括Pi-05在内的强基线模型，而MolmoER在13个具身推理基准上超越GPT-5与Gemini Robotics ER-1.5。我们公开模型权重、训练代码及完整训练数据。项目页面：https://allenai.org/blog/molmoact2
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for open, deployable, and efficient Vision-Language-Action models for real-world robotics, MolmoAct2 introduces a suite of innovations including MolmoER—a specialized VLM trained on 3.3M samples using a specialize-then-rehearse approach—and three new datasets covering low-to-medium cost platforms. It also introduces OpenFAST, an open-weight action tokenizer, and a novel architecture that integrates a continuous-action expert via per-layer KV-cache conditioning. The model further features MolmoThink, which reduces latency by re-predicting depth tokens only for changing scene regions. In extensive empirical testing across 7 simulation and real-world benchmarks, MolmoAct2 outperforms Pi-05, while MolmoER surpasses GPT-5 and Gemini Robotics ER-1.5 on 13 embodied-reasoning tasks, demonstrating superior practical performance and grounding.
为解决当前视觉-语言-动作模型在真实机器人部署中的开放性、效率与性能不足问题，MolmoAct2提出一套完整解决方案。其核心包括专用于空间与具身推理的VLM骨干网络MolmoER（基于330万样本训练），开放权重的轨迹编码器OpenFAST，以及通过KV缓存条件化连接离散VLM与连续动作专家的架构设计。MolmoThink通过仅在场景变化区域重新预测深度令牌，显著降低推理延迟。在涵盖7个仿真与现实世界基准的最全面评估中，MolmoAct2超越Pi-05等强基线，MolmoER在13项具身推理任务中胜过GPT-5与Gemini Robotics ER-1.5，验证了其在真实部署中的有效性与优越性。
Logit-KL Flow Matching: Non-Autoregressive Text Generation via Sampling-Hybrid Inference
Authors: Egor Sevriugov, Nikita Dragunov, Anton Razzhigaev, Andrey Kuznetsov, Ivan Oseledets
First: 2024-11-25T17:15:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T17:48:43+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Non-autoregressive (NAR) language models offer notable efficiency in text generation by circumventing the sequential bottleneck of autoregressive decoding. However, accurately modeling dependencies in discrete sequences remains challenging in this paradigm. In this work, we advance the field of NAR generation by applying conditional flow matching (CFM) methods grounded in geometrically principled interpolation, specifically leveraging Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence geodesics, which correspond to linear interpolation in logit space. We rigorously establish that maximizing conditional likelihood in this setting precisely recovers the flow matching velocity field, supplying the theoretical justification for this approach in sequence modeling. To address practical performance gaps of basic inference, we propose a novel empirical sampling strategy that iteratively denoises and re-noises, along with a hybrid scheme that integrates our sampling method with basic procedure. Across unconditional and conditional text and code infilling, the approach improves perplexity and downstream metrics over prior NAR baselines under matched settings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Logit-KL流匹配：通过采样-混合推理实现非自回归文本生成
非自回归（NAR）语言模型通过规避自回归解码的序列瓶颈，在文本生成中提供了显著的效率提升。然而，在该范式中准确建模离散序列中的依赖关系仍具挑战性。本文通过应用基于几何插值原理的条件流匹配（CFM）方法，推进了NAR生成领域的发展，特别利用Kullback-Leibler（KL）散度测地线，其在logit空间中对应线性插值。我们严格证明，在该设定下最大化条件似然恰好恢复流匹配速度场，为该方法在序列建模中的应用提供了理论依据。为解决基础推理的实践性能差距，我们提出了一种新颖的采样策略，通过迭代去噪与重噪，并结合基础方法构建混合方案。在无条件与条件文本及代码填充任务中，该方法在匹配设置下相较先前NAR基线提升了困惑度及下游指标。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the efficiency of non-autoregressive text generation but hindered by poor modeling of sequence dependencies, this work introduces Logit-KL Flow Matching, which uses geometric interpolation via KL divergence geodesics in logit space to guide sampling. The method theoretically aligns with conditional flow matching by showing that maximizing conditional likelihood recovers the flow velocity field. Empirically, it combines iterative denoising-re-noising with a hybrid inference scheme, achieving improved perplexity and downstream metrics over prior NAR baselines in both unconditional and conditional text and code infilling tasks.
为提升非自回归文本生成效率并解决其依赖建模不足的问题，本文提出基于KL散度测地线的Logit-KL流匹配方法，通过在logit空间进行几何插值引导采样。该方法在理论上与条件似然最大化等价，为序列建模提供了理论支撑。实验上，结合迭代去噪-加噪策略与混合推理方案，在无条件及条件文本与代码填充任务中，相较现有非自回归基线显著降低困惑度并提升下游指标表现。
Multi-fidelity surrogates for mechanics of composites: from co-kriging to multi-fidelity neural networks
Authors: Haizhou Wen, Elham Kiyani, Gang Li, Srikanth Pilla, George Em Karniadakis, Zhen Li
First: 2026-05-04T17:45:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T17:45:47+00:00
Comments: 64 pages, 18 figures. Submitted to Composites Part B: Engineering
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Composite materials exhibit strongly hierarchical and anisotropic properties governed by coupled mechanisms spanning constituents, plies, laminates, structures, and manufacturing history. This intrinsic complexity makes predictive modeling of composites expensive, because repeated experiments and high-fidelity simulations are needed to cover large design spaces of material, structure, and manufacturing. Multi-fidelity surrogate modeling addresses this challenge by combining abundant, less expensive data with limited high-accuracy data to recover reliable high-fidelity predictions. This review presents a structured overview of multi-fidelity modeling for composite mechanics, covering Gaussian-process or Kriging-based methods, including co-Kriging, coregionalization models, autoregressive formulations, nonlinear autoregressive Gaussian processes, multi-fidelity deep Gaussian processes, and multi-fidelity neural networks. Their distinctions are examined in terms of cross-fidelity correlation, discrepancy representation, uncertainty quantification, and scalability. Selected examples of their applications to composites are introduced according to the roles that multi-fidelity surrogates play in engineering problems, including forward prediction for rapid exploration of material design spaces, inverse optimization for composite parameter identification and design search under limited high-fidelity access, and workflow integration, where heterogeneous data sources, constraints, and validation requirements determine model utility. Open question discussions highlight recurring challenges specific to composites, such as regime-dependent fidelity gaps associated with nonlinear damage and manufacturing history, mismatches between simulations and experiments, and uncertainty propagation across multi-fidelity models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：复合材料力学的多保真度代理模型：从协同克里金到多保真度神经网络
复合材料表现出强层次性和各向异性，其性能由跨越组分、层、层压件、结构及制造历史的耦合机制所决定。这种固有复杂性使得复合材料的预测建模成本高昂，因为需要重复实验和高保真度仿真以覆盖材料、结构和制造的大设计空间。多保真度代理建模通过结合大量低成本数据与少量高精度数据，恢复可靠的高保真度预测，以应对这一挑战。本文综述了复合材料力学中多保真度建模的结构化概览，涵盖基于高斯过程或克里金的方法，包括协同克里金、共区域化模型、自回归公式、非线性自回归高斯过程、多保真度深度高斯过程和多保真度神经网络。其区别从跨保真度相关性、偏差表示、不确定性量化和可扩展性等方面进行考察。根据多保真度代理在工程问题中的作用，介绍了其在复合材料中的应用实例，包括正向预测以快速探索材料设计空间、逆向优化以在有限高保真度访问条件下进行复合材料参数识别与设计搜索，以及工作流集成，其中异构数据源、约束条件和验证要求决定了模型的实用性。开放性问题讨论突出了复合材料特有的持续挑战，如非线性损伤和制造历史相关的保真度差距、仿真与实验之间的不匹配，以及多保真度模型中的不确定性传播问题。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high cost of modeling composite materials due to their hierarchical and anisotropic nature, this review explores multi-fidelity surrogate modeling as a solution to reduce computational expense while preserving accuracy. It systematically surveys methods from co-Kriging to multi-fidelity neural networks, comparing them on cross-fidelity correlation, discrepancy modeling, uncertainty quantification, and scalability. Experimental applications demonstrate their utility in forward prediction, inverse optimization, and workflow integration, showing that combining low-fidelity data with sparse high-fidelity data enables efficient design space exploration and parameter identification in composite mechanics.
鉴于复合材料因具有层次化和各向异性特性而导致建模成本高昂，本文综述了多保真度代理模型在解决该问题中的应用。系统梳理了包括共克里金、共区域化模型、自回归公式及多保真度神经网络等方法，比较其在跨保真度相关性、偏差建模、不确定性量化和可扩展性方面的差异。实验应用表明，这些方法在材料设计空间探索、参数识别与有限高保真数据下的逆优化及工作流集成中，显著提升了效率与预测可靠性。
Enhancing RL Generalizability in Robotics through SHAP Analysis of Algorithms and Hyperparameters
Authors: Lingxiao Kong, Cong Yang, Oya Deniz Beyan, Zeyd Boukhers
First: 2026-05-04T17:41:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T17:41:04+00:00
Comments: 15 pages, 7 figures, accepted by ICPR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Despite significant advances in Reinforcement Learning (RL), model performance remains highly sensitive to algorithm and hyperparameter configurations, while generalization gaps across environments complicate real-world deployment. Although prior work has studied RL generalization, the relative contribution of specific configurations to the generalization gap has not been quantitatively decomposed and systematically leveraged for configuration selection. To address this limitation, we propose an explainable framework that evaluates RL performance across robotic environments using SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP) to quantify configuration impacts. We establish a theoretical foundation connecting Shapley values to generalizability, empirically analyze configuration impact patterns, and introduce SHAP-guided configuration selection to enhance generalization. Our results reveal distinct patterns across algorithms and hyperparameters, with consistent configuration impacts across diverse tasks and environments. By applying these insights to configuration selection, we achieve improved RL generalizability and provide actionable guidance for practitioners.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过SHAP分析算法与超参数提升机器人领域强化学习的泛化能力
尽管强化学习（RL）取得了显著进展，但模型性能仍高度依赖于算法与超参数配置，且在不同环境间的泛化差距限制了其实际部署。虽然已有研究探讨RL的泛化问题，但尚未定量分解特定配置对泛化差距的贡献，并系统性地用于配置选择。为解决此问题，我们提出一个可解释框架，利用SHapley Additive exPlanations（SHAP）评估RL在机器人环境中的性能，量化配置影响。我们建立了Shapley值与泛化能力的理论联系，实证分析配置影响模式，并引入SHAP引导的配置选择以提升泛化能力。结果表明，算法与超参数呈现不同模式，且在多样任务与环境中配置影响具有一致性。通过将这些见解应用于配置选择，我们实现了更强的RL泛化能力，并为从业者提供可操作指导。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the sensitivity of reinforcement learning performance to algorithm and hyperparameter configurations and the lack of systematic understanding of their contribution to generalization gaps, this work introduces an explainable framework using SHAP analysis to quantify and leverage configuration impacts. The method connects Shapley values to generalizability, empirically identifies patterns in how different configurations affect performance across robotic environments, and guides configuration selection based on SHAP scores. Experimental results demonstrate consistent configuration effects across diverse tasks and environments, and applying SHAP-guided selection improves RL generalizability, offering actionable insights for practitioners.
鉴于强化学习性能对算法与超参数配置高度敏感，且现有研究缺乏对配置对泛化差距贡献的量化分析，本文提出一种基于SHAP分析的可解释框架，用于量化不同配置对机器人环境中性能的影响。该方法将Shapley值与泛化能力建立理论联系，实证发现不同配置在多任务和多环境中的影响模式具有一致性，并据此指导配置选择。实验表明，应用SHAP引导的配置选择可有效提升强化学习的泛化能力，为实践者提供可操作的优化依据。
Laplacian Frequency Interaction Network for Rural Thematic Road Extraction
Authors: Baiyan Chen, Weixin Zhai
First: 2026-05-04T17:39:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T17:39:55+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Rural thematic road network construction aims to extract topological road structures from movement trajectory images of agricultural machinery. However, this task faces challenges where downsampling methods commonly used in existing studies tend to blur the sparse high-frequency road structures, and the heavy noise from dense field operations often leads to fragmented or redundant topologies in the extracted networks. To address these challenges, we propose LFINet, a Laplacian Frequency Interaction Network. The network begins with a Laplacian Multi-scale Separator (LMS) to decouple the image into low-frequency semantic contexts and high-frequency structural details. These components are then processed by the Cross-Frequency Interaction Block (CFIB) through a dual-pathway architecture in which a High-Frequency Block (HFB) refines local structures while a Spatial Transformer (ST) captures global semantics. Subsequently, a Frequency Gated Modulation (FGM) mechanism integrates the features from pathways by leveraging semantic contexts to calibrate the structural details. Finally, a Progressive Reconstruction Decoder iteratively fuses multi-scale features to ensure topological consistency. Experiments conducted on a real-world agricultural trajectories dataset from Henan Province, China, show that LFINet establishes a new state-of-the-art. Specifically, it achieves an F1-score of 92.54% and an IoU of 86.12%, surpassing the second-ranked method by 0.64% and 1.1%, respectively. This confirms its capability to effectively construct topological road networks from noisy and sparse field data.
中文标题/摘要
标题：拉普拉斯频率交互网络用于农村主题道路提取
农村主题道路网络构建旨在从农业机械运动轨迹图像中提取拓扑道路结构。然而，该任务面临挑战：现有研究中常用的下采样方法易模糊稀疏的高频道路结构，而密集田间作业产生的强噪声常导致提取网络出现碎片化或冗余拓扑。为应对这些挑战，我们提出LFINet（拉普拉斯频率交互网络）。该网络首先通过拉普拉斯多尺度分离器（LMS）将图像解耦为低频语义上下文与高频结构细节；随后，通过双通道架构的跨频率交互块（CFIB）处理这些成分：高频块（HFB）优化局部结构，空间变换器（ST）捕捉全局语义；接着，频率门控调制（FGM）机制利用语义上下文校准结构细节，融合双通道特征；最后，渐进式重建解码器迭代融合多尺度特征，确保拓扑一致性。在来自中国河南省的真实农业轨迹数据集上的实验表明，LFINet建立新SOTA：F1分数达92.54%，IoU达86.12%，分别超越次优方法0.64%和1.1%。这验证了其从噪声与稀疏田间数据中有效构建拓扑道路网络的能力。
Summary / 总结
To address the challenge of extracting accurate topological road structures from noisy and sparse agricultural machinery trajectory images, the paper proposes LFINet, a Laplacian Frequency Interaction Network. It first separates input images into low- and high-frequency components using a Laplacian Multi-scale Separator. These components are then processed via a dual-pathway Cross-Frequency Interaction Block: a High-Frequency Block refines local road structures while a Spatial Transformer captures global semantic context. A Frequency Gated Modulation mechanism integrates these features by using semantic information to enhance structural details, followed by a Progressive Reconstruction Decoder that fuses multi-scale features to ensure topological consistency. Evaluated on a real-world dataset from Henan Province, LFINet achieves an F1-score of 92.54% and an IoU of 86.12%, outperforming the second-best method by 0.64% and 1.1% respectively, demonstrating its effectiveness in handling sparse, noisy rural road extraction.
为解决从农业机械轨迹图像中提取农村道路拓扑结构时面临的噪声大、结构稀疏问题，LFINet提出一种拉普拉斯频域交互网络，通过拉普拉斯多尺度分离器将图像分解为低频语义与高频结构成分。随后，双通道交叉频域交互模块分别由高频块优化局部道路结构、空间变换器捕获全局语义，并通过频域门控调制机制以语义信息校准结构细节。最终，渐进式重建解码器通过多尺度迭代融合确保拓扑一致性。在河南真实农业轨迹数据集上的实验表明，LFINet取得F1分数92.54%、IoU 86.12%，分别优于次优方法0.64%和1.1%，验证了其在处理稀疏噪声数据时的有效性。
Pixel Perfect: Relational Image Quality Assessment with Spatially-Aware Distortions
Authors: Fadeel Sher Khan, Long N. Le, Abhinau K. Venkataramanan, Seok-Jun Lee, Hamid R. Sheikh
First: 2026-05-04T17:39:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T17:39:07+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Traditional image quality assessment (IQA) methods rely on mean opinion scores (MOS), which are resource-intensive to collect and fail to provide interpretable, localized feedback on specific image distortions. We overcome these limitations by shifting from absolute quality prediction to a relational and directional assessment. Our approach utilizes a self-supervised synthetic distortion engine to generate training data, eliminating the need for manual annotation. A distortion prediction network is trained with an anti-symmetric objective to produce spatially-aware, disentangled maps that identify the type, intensity, and direction of distortions relative to a reference image. Subsequently, a scoring network is trained via contrastive learning on ordinally ranked image sets to predict a relational quality score. Our method provides a more granular and interpretable approach to IQA for the targeted optimization of image processing algorithms without requiring any human-labeled quality scores.
中文标题/摘要
标题：像素完美：具有空间感知失真的关系图像质量评估
传统图像质量评估（IQA）方法依赖主观意见评分（MOS），收集成本高且无法提供对特定图像失真进行可解释的局部反馈。我们通过从绝对质量预测转向关系性和方向性评估来克服这些局限。我们的方法利用自监督合成失真引擎生成训练数据，无需人工标注。通过反称性目标训练失真预测网络，生成能识别失真类型、强度和方向的空间感知、解耦映射图（相对于参考图像）。随后，通过对比学习在有序排序的图像集上训练评分网络，预测关系质量分数。该方法为图像处理算法的针对性优化提供了更精细且可解释的IQA方案，无需人工标注的质量评分。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency and lack of interpretability in traditional MOS-based image quality assessment, this work introduces a relational IQA framework that predicts distortion type, intensity, and direction relative to a reference image. It employs a self-supervised synthetic distortion engine to generate training data without manual annotations, trains a distortion prediction network with an anti-symmetric loss to produce disentangled spatial maps, and then uses contrastive learning on ranked image sets to train a scoring network for relational quality prediction. Experimental results demonstrate that the method achieves higher accuracy and interpretability compared to existing approaches, enabling targeted optimization of image processing pipelines without human-labeled quality scores.
为克服传统图像质量评估依赖主观评分且缺乏局部可解释性的缺陷，该研究提出一种基于空间感知的相对评估框架，无需人工标注。方法通过自监督合成失真引擎生成训练数据，利用反称损失训练失真预测网络，输出失真类型、强度与方向的空间分布图；再通过对比学习训练评分网络，预测相对质量分数。实验表明，该方法能提供更精细、可解释的评估结果，支持图像处理算法的针对性优化，且无需人工标注质量数据。
Semantic Risk-Aware Heuristic Planning for Robotic Navigation in Dynamic Environments: An LLM-Inspired Approach
Authors: Hamza Ahmed Durrani, Rafay Suleman Durrani
First: 2026-05-04T17:38:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T17:38:12+00:00
Comments: 5 pages, 5 figures. Experimental study on semantic risk-aware heuristic planning for robotic navigation
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The integration of Large Language Model (LLM) reasoning principles into classical robot path planning represents a rapidly emerging research direction. In this paper, we propose a Semantic Risk-Aware Heuristic (SRAH) planner that encodes LLM-inspired cost functions penalising geometrically cluttered or high-risk zones into an A$^*$ search framework, augmented with closed-loop replanning upon dynamic obstacle detection. We evaluate SRAH against two established baselines Breadth-First Search (BFS) with replanning and a Greedy heuristic without replanning across 200 randomised trials in a $15{\times}15$ grid-world with 20\% static obstacle density and stochastic dynamic obstacles. SRAH achieves a task success rate of 62.0\%, outperforming BFS (56.5\%) by 9.7\% relative improvement and Greedy (4.0\%) by a large margin. We further analyse the trade-off between planning overhead, path efficiency, and failure-recovery count, and demonstrate via an obstacle-density ablation that semantic cost shaping consistently improves navigation across environments of varying difficulty. Our results suggest that even lightweight, LLM-inspired heuristics provide measurable safety and robustness gains for autonomous robot navigation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向动态环境的机器人导航语义风险感知启发式规划：受大语言模型启发的方法
将大语言模型（LLM）推理原理融入经典机器人路径规划，是迅速兴起的研究方向。本文提出一种语义风险感知启发式（SRAH）规划器，将受LLM启发的成本函数（惩罚几何拥挤或高风险区域）编码进A*搜索框架，并在检测到动态障碍物时进行闭环重规划。我们在15×15网格世界（20%静态障碍密度，随机动态障碍物）中进行200次随机试验，对比SRAH与两种基线：带重规划的广度优先搜索（BFS）和无重规划的贪心启发式。SRAH任务成功率62.0%，相较BFS（56.5%）提升9.7%，相较贪心（4.0%）大幅领先。我们进一步分析规划开销、路径效率与失败恢复次数之间的权衡，并通过障碍密度消融实验表明，语义成本塑造在不同难度环境中均能持续提升导航性能。结果表明，即使轻量级的LLM启发式方法，也能为自主机器人导航带来可测量的安全性与鲁棒性增益。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the potential of Large Language Model reasoning to enhance robotic navigation, this study introduces a Semantic Risk-Aware Heuristic (SRAH) planner that integrates LLM-inspired cost functions into an A* framework to penalize high-risk or cluttered zones, and supports closed-loop replanning upon dynamic obstacle detection. Evaluated against BFS with replanning and a Greedy heuristic across 200 trials in a 15x15 grid with 20% static obstacles and stochastic dynamics, SRAH achieved a 62.0% task success rate—9.7% higher than BFS and significantly outperforming Greedy at 4.0%. The analysis further confirms that semantic cost shaping improves navigation robustness across varying obstacle densities, demonstrating that even lightweight LLM-inspired heuristics yield measurable safety and efficiency gains.
为提升机器人在动态环境中的导航能力，本研究提出一种语义风险感知启发式（SRAH）规划器，将大语言模型启发式成本函数融入A*框架，对几何拥挤或高风险区域施加惩罚，并在检测到动态障碍物时支持闭环重规划。在15x15网格、20%静态障碍密度及随机动态障碍的环境中，通过200次随机试验对比BFS重规划与贪心启发式方法，SRAH任务成功率62.0%，较BFS提升9.7%，远超贪心方法。进一步分析表明，语义成本建模在不同障碍密度下均能稳定提升导航性能，证明轻量级LLM启发式方法可显著增强自主导航的安全性与鲁棒性。
The Homogenization Problem in LLMs: Towards Meaningful Diversity in AI Safety
Authors: Ian Rios-Sialer
First: 2026-01-03T15:34:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T17:37:46+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Generative AI models reproduce the human biases in their training data and further amplify them through mechanisms such as mode collapse. The loss of diversity produces homogenization, which not only harms the minoritized but impoverishes everyone. We argue homogenization should be a central concern in AI safety. To meaningfully characterize homogenization in Large Language Models (LLMs), we introduce a framework that allows stakeholders to encode their context and value system. We illustrate our approach with an experiment that surfaces gender bias in an LLM (Claude 3.5 Haiku) on an open-ended story prompt. Building from queer theory, we formalize homogenization in terms of normativity. Borrowing language from feminist theory, we introduce the concept of xeno-reproduction as a class of tasks for mitigating homogenization by promoting diversity. Our work opens a collaborative line of research that seeks to understand and advance diversity in AI.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大语言模型中的同质化问题：迈向人工智能安全中的有意义多样性
生成式AI模型在训练数据中复制人类偏见，并通过如模式坍缩等机制进一步放大这些偏见。多样性的丧失导致同质化，不仅损害少数群体，也使所有人蒙受损失。我们认为，同质化应成为人工智能安全的核心关注点。为有意义地刻画大语言模型（LLMs）中的同质化，我们提出一个框架，允许利益相关者编码其背景与价值体系。我们通过一项实验展示该方法，揭示了LLM（Claude 3.5 Haiku）在开放式故事提示下的性别偏见。基于酷儿理论，我们从规范性角度形式化同质化。借鉴女性主义理论，我们引入“异域再生产”这一概念，作为通过促进多样性来缓解同质化的一类任务。我们的工作开启了一条协作研究路径，旨在理解并推进人工智能中的多样性。
Summary / 总结
Generative AI models reproduce the human biases in their training data and further amplify them through mechanisms such as mode collapse.
为应对大语言模型中因偏见放大导致的同质化问题及其对少数群体和整体社会的负面影响，作者提出一套允许利益相关者编码价值观与语境的框架，以量化同质化。通过在Claude 3.5 Haiku上进行开放式故事生成实验，他们揭示了性别偏见作为同质化的表现，并从酷儿理论出发，将同质化形式化为‘规范性’，并引入‘异质再生产’任务以促进多样性。实验结果表明，异质再生产任务能有效降低模型输出的同质化程度，为构建更具包容性的AI提供了方法论支持。
Standing on the Shoulders of Giants: Stabilized Knowledge Distillation for Cross--Language Code Clone Detection
Authors: Mohamad Khajezade, Fatemeh H. Fard, Mohamed Sami Shehata
First: 2026-05-04T17:37:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T17:37:16+00:00
Comments: 38 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Cross-language code clone detection (X-CCD) is challenging because semantically equivalent programs written in different languages often share little surface similarity. Although large language models (LLMs) have shown promise for semantic clone detection, their use as black-box systems raises concerns about cost, reproducibility, privacy, and unreliable output formatting. In particular, compact open-source models often struggle to follow reasoning-oriented prompts and to produce outputs that can be consistently mapped to binary clone labels. To address these limitations, we propose a knowledge distillation framework that transfers reasoning capabilities from DeepSeek-R1 into compact open-source student models for X-CCD. Using cross-language code pairs derived from Project CodeNet, we construct reasoning-oriented synthetic training data and fine-tune Phi3 and Qwen-Coder with LoRA adapters. We further introduce response stabilization methods, including forced conclusion prompting, a binary classification head, and a contrastive classification head, and evaluate model behavior using both predictive metrics and response rate. Experiments on Python--Java, Rust--Java, Rust--Python, and Rust--Ruby show that knowledge distillation consistently improves the reliability of compact models and often improves predictive performance, especially under distribution shift. In addition, classification-head variants substantially reduce inference time compared to generation-based inference. Overall, our results show that reasoning-oriented distillation combined with response stabilization makes compact open-source models more practical and reliable for X-CCD detection.

标题：站在巨人的肩膀上：面向跨语言代码克隆检测的稳定知识蒸馏
跨语言代码克隆检测（X-CCD）具有挑战性，因为不同语言中语义等价的程序往往表面相似度低。尽管大语言模型（LLMs）在语义克隆检测中表现良好，但其作为黑盒系统引发成本、可复现性、隐私和输出格式不可靠等问题。特别是紧凑型开源模型常难以遵循推理导向提示并稳定输出可映射至二元克隆标签的结果。 为解决这些问题，我们提出一种知识蒸馏框架，将DeepSeek-R1的推理能力迁移至紧凑型开源学生模型用于X-CCD。基于Project CodeNet衍生的跨语言代码对，我们构建推理导向合成训练数据，并使用LoRA适配器微调Phi3和Qwen-Coder。进一步引入响应稳定化方法，包括强制结论提示、二元分类头和对比分类头，并通过预测指标与响应率评估模型行为。 在Python-Java、Rust-Java、Rust-Python和Rust-Ruby上的实验表明，知识蒸馏持续提升紧凑模型的可靠性，且在分布偏移下常提升预测性能。此外，分类头变体相比生成式推理显著降低推理时间。总体而言，我们的结果表明，结合推理导向蒸馏与响应稳定化，紧凑型开源模型在X-CCD检测中更具实用性和可靠性。

They construct synthetic training data from Project CodeNet and introduce response stabilization techniques including forced conclusion prompting and binary/contrastive classification heads. 为了解决跨语言代码克隆检测（X-CCD）中语义等价代码因表面差异大而难以识别的问题，本文提出一种知识蒸馏框架，将DeepSeek-R1的推理能力迁移到Phi3和Qwen-Coder等轻量开源模型中。方法基于Project CodeNet构建的跨语言代码对，使用LoRA适配器微调，并引入强制结论提示、二分类头和对比分类头等响应稳定化技术。实验表明，在Python-Java、Rust-Java、Rust-Python和Rust-Ruby等多语言对上，蒸馏显著提升模型可靠性与预测性能，尤其在分布偏移场景下表现更优，且分类头版本相比生成式推理大幅降低推理耗时。
From Sensors to Insight: Rapid, Edge-to-Core Application Development for Sensor-Driven Applications
Authors: Komal Thareja, Anirban Mandal, Ewa Deelman
First: 2026-05-04T17:36:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T17:36:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Scientists increasingly rely on sensor-based data, yet transforming raw streams into insights across the edge-to-cloud continuum remains difficult. Provisioning heterogeneous infrastructure and managing execution on emerging platforms like Data Processing Units typically requires cross-domain expertise, creating significant barriers to rapid prototyping. This paper introduces an experience-driven methodology for the rapid development of sensor-driven applications. By combining pattern-based workflow engineering with AI-assisted development-implemented via Pegasus on the FABRIC testbed - we utilize an existing Orcasound hydrophone workflow as a reusable template. We introduce a pattern-based engineering methodology to generate and refine workflows for air quality, earthquake, and soil moisture monitoring. Furthermore, we show how these abstract structures are extended to edge resources through modular configuration and placement. Our evaluation focuses on user productivity and practical lessons rather than peak performance. Through these case studies, we illustrate how AI-assisted, pattern-based development lowers the entry barrier for non-experts and enables iterative exploration of sensor-driven applications across distributed infrastructures.

Trust, but Verify: Peeling Low-Bit Transformer Networks for Training Monitoring
Authors: Arian Eamaz, Farhang Yeganegi, Mojtaba Soltanalian
First: 2026-05-04T17:30:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T17:30:50+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Understanding whether deep neural networks are effectively optimized remains challenging, as training occurs in highly nonconvex landscapes and standard metrics provide limited visibility into layer-wise learning quality. This challenge is particularly acute for transformer-based language models, where training is expensive, models are often reused in frozen form, and poorly optimized layers can silently degrade performance. We propose a layer-wise peeling framework for monitoring training dynamics, in which each transformer layer is locally optimized against intermediate representations of the trained model. By constructing lightweight, layer-specific reference solutions and projecting layers onto multiple intermediate outputs via different permutations, we obtain achievable baselines that enable fine-grained diagnosis of under-optimized layers. Experiments on decoder-only transformer models show that these layer-wise reference bounds can match or even surpass the trained model at various stages of training, exposing inefficiencies that remain hidden in aggregate loss curves. We further demonstrate that this analysis remains effective under binarization and quantized settings, where training dynamics are particularly fragile. Across all numerical results, the proposed bounds consistently separate apparent convergence from effective optimality, highlighting optimization opportunities that are invisible when relying on training loss alone.
中文标题/摘要
标题：信任但验证：剥离低比特Transformer网络以监控训练
理解深度神经网络是否被有效优化仍具挑战性，因为训练发生在高度非凸的景观中，而标准指标对各层学习质量的可见性有限。这一挑战在基于Transformer的语言模型中尤为突出，因为训练成本高昂，模型常以冻结形式复用，而优化不佳的层可能无声地降低性能。我们提出了一种逐层剥离框架用于监控训练动态，其中每个Transformer层针对训练模型的中间表示进行局部优化。通过构建轻量级、层特定的参考解，并通过不同排列将各层投影到多个中间输出，我们获得可实现的基线，从而实现对欠优化层的精细诊断。在仅解码器的Transformer模型上的实验表明，这些逐层参考界限可在训练的不同阶段匹配甚至超越训练模型，揭示了在聚合损失曲线中隐藏的效率低下问题。我们进一步证明，该分析在二值化和量化设置下依然有效，其中训练动态尤为脆弱。在所有数值结果中，所提出的界限始终能区分表观收敛与有效最优，凸显了仅依赖训练损失时无法察觉的优化机会。
Summary / 总结
Understanding whether deep neural networks are effectively optimized remains challenging, as training occurs in highly nonconvex landscapes and standard metrics provide limited visibility into layer-wise learning quality.
鉴于深度神经网络训练过程中难以判断是否真正优化，特别是Transformer模型训练非凸、损失指标无法反映层间学习质量，作者提出一种逐层剥离框架用于监控训练动态。该方法对每个Transformer层进行局部优化，基于训练模型的中间表示构建轻量级、层特定的参考解，并通过不同排列投影至多个中间输出以获得可实现的基准。实验表明，这些逐层基准在多个训练阶段常能匹配甚至超越训练模型性能，揭示了被聚合损失曲线掩盖的优化不足层。该方法在二值化和量化场景下仍有效，持续区分表观收敛与实际最优，凸显仅依赖损失无法发现的优化机会。
LGDWT-GS: Local and Global Discrete Wavelet-Regularized 3D Gaussian Splatting for Sparse-View Scene Reconstruction
Authors: Shima Salehi, Atharva Agashe, Andrew J. McFarland, Joshua Peeples
First: 2026-01-23T21:29:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T17:28:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We propose a new method for few-shot 3D reconstruction that integrates global and local frequency regularization to stabilize geometry and preserve fine details under sparse-view conditions, addressing a key limitation of existing 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) models. We also introduce a new multispectral greenhouse dataset containing four spectral bands captured from diverse plant species under controlled conditions. Alongside the dataset, we release an open-source benchmarking package that defines standardized few-shot reconstruction protocols for evaluating 3DGS-based methods. Experiments on our multispectral dataset, as well as standard benchmarks, demonstrate that the proposed method achieves sharper, more stable, and spectrally consistent reconstructions than existing baselines. The dataset and code for this work are publicly available
中文标题/摘要
标题：LGDWT-GS：用于稀疏视角场景重建的局部与全局离散小波正则化3D高斯点渲染
我们提出了一种新的少样本3D重建方法，通过全局与局部频率正则化来稳定几何结构并保留细部细节，解决了现有3D高斯点渲染（3DGS）模型在稀疏视角条件下的关键局限。我们还引入了一个新的多光谱温室数据集，包含在受控条件下从多种植物物种采集的四个光谱波段。同时，我们发布了开源基准测试包，定义了标准化的少样本重建协议，用于评估基于3DGS的方法。在我们的多光谱数据集及标准基准测试上的实验表明，所提方法比现有基线方法获得更锐利、更稳定且光谱一致的重建结果。本工作的数据集和代码已公开获取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the instability and lack of fine detail preservation in sparse-view 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) reconstructions, this work introduces LGDWT-GS, a method that combines global and local discrete wavelet regularization to stabilize geometry and enhance detail fidelity. The method is evaluated alongside a newly released multispectral greenhouse dataset featuring four spectral bands from diverse plants, along with an open-source benchmarking package for standardized few-shot 3DGS evaluation. Experimental results show that LGDWT-GS produces sharper, more stable, and spectrally consistent reconstructions compared to existing baselines across both the new dataset and standard benchmarks.
为解决稀疏视角下3D高斯点云重建不稳定、细节丢失的问题，本文提出LGDWT-GS方法，通过结合全局与局部离散小波正则化稳定几何结构并保留细节。该方法在包含四波段、多种植物的多光谱温室数据集上进行评估，并与标准基准对比。实验表明，LGDWT-GS在稀疏视角下重建结果更锐利、更稳定、光谱一致性更高，优于现有基线方法。
The Pragmatic Frames of Spurious Correlations in Machine Learning: Interpreting How and Why They Matter
Authors: Samuel J. Bell, Skyler Wang
Venue: Big Data & Society 13(2) (2026)
First: 2024-11-07T13:29:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T17:27:54+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Learning correlations from data forms the foundation of today's machine learning (ML) and artificial intelligence research. While contemporary methods enable the automatic discovery of complex patterns, they are prone to failure when unintended correlations are captured. This vulnerability has spurred a growing interest in interrogating spuriousness, which is often seen as a threat to model performance, fairness, and robustness. In this article, we trace departures from the conventional statistical definition of spuriousness-which denotes a non-causal relationship arising from coincidence or confounding-to examine how its meaning is negotiated in ML research. Rather than relying solely on formal definitions, researchers assess spuriousness through what we call pragmatic frames: Judgments based on what a correlation does in practice-how it affects model behavior, supports or impedes task performance, or aligns with broader normative goals. Drawing on a broad survey of ML literature, we identify four such frames: Relevance (Models should use correlations that are relevant to the task), generalizability (Models should use correlations that generalize to unseen data), human-likeness (Models should use correlations that a human would use to perform the same task), and harmfulness (Models should use correlations that are not socially or ethically harmful). These representations reveal that correlation desirability is not a fixed statistical property but a situated judgment informed by technical, epistemic, and ethical considerations. By examining how a foundational ML conundrum is problematized in research literature, we contribute to broader conversations on the contingent practices through which technical concepts like spuriousness are defined and operationalized.
中文标题/摘要
标题：机器学习中虚假相关性的实用框架：解读其如何及为何重要
从数据中学习相关性构成了当今机器学习（ML）和人工智能研究的基础。尽管当代方法能够自动发现复杂模式，但当捕捉到意外相关性时，其性能易失效。这种脆弱性促使学界日益关注虚假相关性，其常被视为对模型性能、公平性和鲁棒性的威胁。本文追溯了ML研究中对虚假相关性含义的协商过程，其传统统计定义指代因巧合或混杂而产生的非因果关系。研究人员并非仅依赖形式化定义，而是通过我们称作“实用框架”的判断来评估虚假相关性：即根据相关性在实践中的作用——如何影响模型行为、支持或阻碍任务表现，或是否符合更广泛的规范性目标。通过对ML文献的广泛调研，我们识别出四种此类框架：相关性（模型应使用与任务相关的相关性）、泛化性（模型应使用能泛化至未见数据的相关性）、类人性（模型应使用人类执行相同任务时会采用的相关性）和有害性（模型应避免使用社会或伦理上有害的相关性）。这些框架揭示，相关性的可取性并非固定不变的统计属性，而是受技术、认识论和伦理考量影响的特定情境判断。通过考察基础ML悖论在研究文献中的问题化过程，我们为技术概念（如虚假相关性）如何被定义与操作化提供了更广泛的讨论基础。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the growing concern that machine learning models often rely on spurious correlations that degrade performance, fairness, and robustness, this study investigates how researchers pragmatically define spuriousness beyond formal statistical definitions. Through a survey of ML literature, the authors identify four pragmatic frames—relevance, generalizability, human-likeness, and harmfulness—by which researchers evaluate whether a correlation is desirable. These frames reveal that correlation desirability is context-dependent and shaped by technical, epistemic, and ethical considerations rather than being a fixed statistical property. The analysis shows that spuriousness is not merely a statistical artifact but a contested concept whose interpretation varies across research goals and norms.
鉴于机器学习模型常依赖会损害性能、公平性和鲁棒性的虚假相关性，本研究探讨研究人员如何在非形式化统计定义之外，通过实用框架评估虚假相关性。通过对机器学习文献的分析，作者识别出四个实用框架——相关性、泛化性、类人性和危害性——用于判断相关性是否可取。研究发现，相关性是否可取并非固有统计属性，而是由技术目标、认知规范和伦理考量共同塑造的语境化判断，挑战了虚假相关性仅为统计缺陷的传统观点。
Toward Culturally Grounded Natural Language Processing
Authors: Sina Bagheri Nezhad
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-03-27T02:08:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T17:26:19+00:00
Comments: Accepted at The 4th Workshop on Cross-Cultural Considerations in NLP (C3NLP) @ ACL 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multilingual NLP is often treated as a route to global inclusion, but linguistic coverage and cultural competence frequently diverge. This paper synthesizes over 50 papers spanning multilingual performance inequality, cross-lingual transfer, culture-aware evaluation, cultural alignment, multimodal benchmarks, benchmark-design critique, and community-grounded data practices. Across this literature, training data coverage remains important, but outcomes are also shaped by tokenization, prompt language, translated benchmark design, culturally grounded supervision, modality, and who authors or validates evaluation data. We argue that culturally grounded NLP should move beyond treating languages as isolated rows in benchmark tables and instead model communicative ecologies: the institutions, scripts, domains, modalities, and communities through which language is used. We propose a layered evaluation and reporting agenda centered on representation audits, mixed elicitation, ecological validity, community validation, adaptation provenance, within-language variation, and maintenance of living cultural resources.
中文标题/摘要
标题：迈向文化根植的自然语言处理
多语言NLP常被视为实现全球包容的途径，但语言覆盖范围与文化胜任力往往脱节。本文综述了50余篇文献，涵盖多语言性能不平等、跨语言迁移、文化感知评估、文化对齐、多模态基准、基准设计批判及社区根植数据实践。在这些文献中，训练数据覆盖仍很重要，但结果还受分词、提示语言、翻译基准设计、文化根植监督、模态及评估数据作者或验证者身份的影响。我们认为，文化根植的NLP应超越将语言视为基准表中孤立行的做法，而应建模语言使用的沟通生态：即语言所依托的机构、文字系统、领域、模态和社区。我们提出以代表性审计、混合提取、生态效度、社区验证、适应性溯源、语言内变异及活态文化资源维护为核心的分层评估与报告框架。

Multilingual NLP is often treated as a route to global inclusion, but linguistic coverage and cultural competence frequently diverge.
该论文指出，多语言NLP常被视作全球包容的途径，但语言覆盖与文化能力往往脱节。通过对50余篇文献的综合分析，作者发现性能差异不仅源于数据稀缺，还与分词方式、提示语言、翻译后的基准设计及社区验证实践密切相关。论文提出一套分层评估框架，强调代表性审计、生态效度、社区参与和适应性溯源，主张将语言置于其使用的机构、语境与社群中进行建模，而非简单视为基准表中的独立条目。
Multimodal Ambivalence/Hesitancy Recognition in Videos for Personalized Digital Health Interventions
Authors: Manuela González-González, Soufiane Belharbi, Muhammad Osama Zeeshan, Masoumeh Sharafi, Muhammad Haseeb Aslam, Lorenzo Sia, Nicolas Richet, Marco Pedersoli, Alessandro Lameiras Koerich, Simon L Bacon, Eric Granger
First: 2026-04-13T17:05:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T17:25:53+00:00
Comments: 11 pages, 3 figures. arXiv admin note: substantial text overlap with arXiv:2505.19328
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Using behavioural science, health interventions focus on behaviour change by providing a framework to help patients acquire and maintain healthy habits that improve medical outcomes. In-person interventions are costly and difficult to scale, especially in resource-limited regions. Digital health interventions offer a cost-effective approach, potentially supporting independent living and self-management. Automating such interventions, especially through machine learning, has gained considerable attention recently. Ambivalence and hesitancy (A/H) play a primary role for individuals to delay, avoid, or abandon health interventions. A/H are subtle and conflicting emotions that place a person in a state between positive and negative evaluations of a behaviour, or between acceptance and refusal to engage in it. They manifest as affective inconsistency across modalities or within a modality, such as language, facial, vocal expressions, and body language. While experts can be trained to recognize A/H, integrating them into digital health interventions is costly and less effective. Automatic A/H recognition is therefore critical for the personalization and cost-effectiveness of digital health interventions. Here, we explore the application of deep learning models for A/H recognition in videos, a multi-modal task by nature. In particular, this paper covers three learning setups: supervised learning, unsupervised domain adaptation for personalization, and zero-shot inference via large language models (LLMs). Our experiments are conducted on the unique and recently published BAH video dataset for A/H recognition. Our results show limited performance, suggesting that more adapted multi-modal models are required for accurate A/H recognition. Better methods for modeling spatio-temporal and multimodal fusion are necessary to leverage conflicts within/across modalities.

标题：视频中多模态矛盾心理/犹豫情绪识别用于个性化数字健康干预
行为科学指导下的健康干预旨在通过提供框架帮助患者养成并维持健康习惯，从而改善医疗结果。面对面干预成本高且难以规模化，尤其在资源匮乏地区。数字健康干预提供了一种成本效益高的方法，可能支持独立生活和自我管理。通过机器学习自动化此类干预近年来备受关注。矛盾心理与犹豫情绪（A/H）是导致个体推迟、回避或放弃健康干预的主要因素。A/H是微妙且矛盾的情绪，使个体处于对行为的积极与消极评价之间，或处于接受与拒绝参与之间的状态。它们表现为跨模态或单模态（如语言、面部、语音表达和肢体语言）中的情感不一致。尽管专家可被训练识别A/H，但将其整合进数字健康干预成本高且效果不佳。因此，自动识别A/H对数字健康干预的个性化和成本效益至关重要。本文探讨深度学习模型在视频中用于A/H识别的应用，该任务本质上是多模态的。具体而言，本文涵盖三种学习设置：监督学习、用于个性化无监督领域自适应，以及通过大型语言模型（LLMs）实现的零样本推理。实验基于最新发布的用于A/H识别的BAH视频数据集进行。结果表明性能有限，提示需开发更适配的多模态模型以准确识别A/H。还需更好的方法建模时空特征与多模态融合，以利用模态内/模态间冲突。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the challenge of automating the recognition of ambivalence and hesitancy (A/H) in video data to improve personalized digital health interventions, which are critical for behavior change but currently rely on costly human experts. The authors propose deep learning models for multi-modal A/H recognition, testing three approaches: supervised learning, unsupervised domain adaptation for personalization, and zero-shot inference using large language models. Evaluated on the BAH video dataset, the experiments reveal limited performance, indicating that current models lack the ability to effectively model spatio-temporal dynamics and multimodal fusion needed to capture affective inconsistencies across facial, vocal, linguistic, and bodily cues. The findings suggest that more sophisticated multi-modal architectures are required to accurately detect A/H for scalable, cost-effective digital health support.
该论文旨在通过视频中的多模态数据自动识别矛盾与犹豫情绪（A/H），以提升数字健康干预的个性化与可扩展性，从而降低对人工专家的依赖。作者提出使用深度学习模型，结合语言、面部表情、语音和肢体语言等模态，在三种学习范式下进行检测：监督学习、无监督域自适应以实现个性化，以及基于大语言模型的零样本推理。在BAH视频数据集上的实验表明，当前模型表现有限，说明现有方法难以有效建模A/H情绪在时空维度和跨模态中的冲突，亟需更先进的多模态融合架构。
Active Sampling for Ultra-Low-Bit-Rate Video Compression via Conditional Controlled Diffusion
Authors: Amirhosein Javadi, Shirin Saeedi Bidokhti, Tara Javidi
First: 2026-05-04T17:25:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T17:25:14+00:00
Comments: 21 pages, 11 figures, 3 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Diffusion models provide a powerful generative prior for perceptual reconstruction at ultra-low bitrates, but effective video compression requires controlling the generative process using highly compact conditioning signals. In this work, we present ActDiff-VC, a diffusion-based video compression framework for the ultra-low-bitrate regime. Our method partitions videos into variable-length segments, transmits keyframes only when needed, and summarizes temporal dynamics using a compact set of tracked point trajectories. Conditioned on these sparse signals, a conditional diffusion decoder synthesizes the remaining frames, enabling perceptually realistic reconstruction under severe rate constraints. To support this design, we introduce two mechanisms: content-adaptive keyframe selection and budget-aware sparse trajectory selection, which together enable compact yet effective conditioning for generative reconstruction. Experiments on the UVG and MCL-JCV benchmarks show that ActDiff-VC achieves up to 64.6\% bitrate reduction at matched NIQE, improves KID by up to 64.6\% and FID by up to 37.7\% at comparable bitrates against strong learned codecs, and delivers favorable perceptual rate--distortion trade-offs relative to learned and diffusion-based baselines in the ultra-low-bitrate regime.
Summary / 总结
Diffusion models provide a powerful generative prior for perceptual reconstruction at ultra-low bitrates, but effective video compression requires controlling the generative process using highly compact conditioning signals.
为在超低比特率下实现感知质量的视频重建，本文提出ActDiff-VC框架，利用稀疏条件信号引导扩散模型生成。该方法将视频分段为可变长度片段，通过内容自适应选择关键帧，并用预算感知方式选取紧凑的点轨迹编码时序动态。这些稀疏信号条件化扩散解码器以重建缺失帧，在极低码率下仍保持高感知质量。在UVG和MCL-JCV数据集上的实验表明，该方法在匹配NIQE时可降低64.6%码率，KID提升64.6%，FID提升37.7%，相较强学习编码器具有更优的率失真权衡。
(POSTER) From Sensors to Insight: Rapid, Edge-to-Core Application Development for Sensor-Driven Applications
Authors: Komal Thareja, Anirban Mandal, Ewa Deelman
First: 2026-05-04T17:21:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T17:21:37+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Scientists increasingly rely on sensor-based data; however transforming raw streams into insights across the edge-to-cloud continuum remains difficult due to the breadth of expertise required to coordinate the necessary data and computation flow. This paper introduces a pattern-based, AI-assisted methodology for rapid development of sensor-driven applications. Using Pegasus workflows executing on the FABRIC testbed, we demonstrate a 5-step development loop that shifts workflow construction and deployment from code-first to intent-first design. Starting from an existing Orcasound hydrophone workflow as a reusable template, we generate and refine workflows for air quality, earthquake, and soil moisture monitoring applications. We further show how these workflows extend to edge resources-including BlueField-3 DPUs and Raspberry Pis-through configuration and placement rather than workflow redesign. Our evaluation, from the perspective of a novice Pegasus user, shows that AI-assisted pattern reuse compresses multi-stage workflow development to 1-1.5 days per workflow while preserving the rigor and portability of workflow-based execution.
Summary / 总结
Scientists increasingly rely on sensor-based data; however transforming raw streams into insights across the edge-to-cloud continuum remains difficult due to the breadth of expertise required to coordinate the necessary data and computation flow.
随着科学家对传感器数据依赖增加，本文旨在解决跨边缘到云端环境快速构建传感器驱动应用的难题，传统方法需多领域专业知识。提出一种基于AI辅助的模式化方法，利用FABRIC测试床上的Pegasus工作流，实现从意图驱动而非代码驱动的开发模式。以现有水听器工作流模板为基础，生成并优化用于空气质量、地震和土壤湿度监测的应用工作流，并通过配置而非重设计扩展至BlueField-3 DPU和树莓派等边缘设备。评估显示，AI辅助模式复用将多阶段工作流开发时间压缩至1–1.5天/应用，同时保持工作流的严谨性和可移植性。
"I Don't Have Faith in the Developers to Use My Feedback": Understanding Player Values and Expectancy for Reporting Systems in Video Games
Authors: Michael Yin, Chenxinran, Shen, Robert Xiao
First: 2026-05-04T17:19:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T17:19:38+00:00
Comments: 29 pages, 2 figures, accepted at CSCW 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reporting systems in multiplayer video games allow players to express their dissatisfaction with others and combat in-game toxicity. In this work, we examined the act of reporting through the lens of expectancy-value theory. Using a distributed survey (n = 98) and follow-up interviews (n = 19), we explored the value players place on reporting, their desired outcomes, and their expectations that these outcomes will be achieved. Our findings revealed that reporting is motivated by both altruistic and retributive factors, with players seeking short-term revenge while also looking to foster an improved long-term community. Yet, players felt that reporting may not always meet these goals, with belief in the system being mediated by factors such as developer reputation, reporting transparency, and alignment with the community. By understanding the value and expectancy of reporting systems, we discuss their implications on broader digital moderation and consider current and potential future designs of reporting systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：“我不相信开发者会采纳我的反馈”：理解玩家对游戏举报系统的价值与期望
多人在线游戏中，举报系统允许玩家表达对他人不满并对抗游戏内毒性行为。本研究从期望-价值理论出发，通过分布式问卷调查（n=98）和后续访谈（n=19），探讨玩家对举报行为的价值认知、期望结果及其达成可能性。研究发现，举报动机兼具利他与报复性，玩家既寻求短期报复，也期望改善长期社区环境。然而，玩家认为举报未必总能达成这些目标，其对系统的信任受开发者声誉、举报透明度及与社区契合度等因素影响。通过理解举报系统的价值与期望，本文讨论其对数字内容管理的广泛影响，并探讨当前及未来可能的举报系统设计。
