Reporting systems in multiplayer video games allow players to express their dissatisfaction with others and combat in-game toxicity.
TRACE: Temporal Reasoning over Context and Evidence for Activity Recognition in Smart Homes
Authors: Yingtian Shi, Abivishaq Balasubramanian, Jessica Herring, Jiachen Li, Juan Macias Romero, Rosemarie Santa Gonzalez, Varun Mishra, Agata Rozga, Xiang Zhi Tan, Thomas Plötz
First: 2026-05-04T17:19:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T17:19:24+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Human activity recognition (HAR) in smart homes remains challenging because many daily activities exhibit similar local sensor patterns, while minimally intrusive sensing provides sparse and ambiguous observations. As a result, methods based on short temporal or event windows often fail to capture the broader temporal and behavioral context needed for reliable activity understanding. We present TRACE (Temporal Reasoning over Context and Evidence), a contextual activity recognition framework for smart homes that integrates multi-source sensor evidence with user-specific contextual priors to improve activity interpretation. Rather than treating recognition as a local classification problem, TRACE leverages contextual reasoning to resolve ambiguities, reduce fragmented predictions, and infer more semantically specific activities. We evaluate TRACE on public benchmarks and in a deployment study conducted in our smart-home environment. Results show that TRACE improves recognition accuracy for semantically complex activities, produces more temporally coherent predictions that better align with user-specific routines, and maintains robust performance under cross-domain transfer and missing-modality conditions. These findings demonstrate the value of contextual reasoning for advancing smart-home HAR.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TRACE：面向智能家居活动识别的上下文与证据时序推理
智能家居中的人类活动识别（HAR）仍具挑战性，因许多日常活动具有相似的局部传感器模式，而最小侵入式传感提供稀疏且模糊的观测。因此，基于短时序或事件窗口的方法常无法捕捉可靠活动理解所需的更广泛时序与行为上下文。我们提出TRACE（上下文与证据时序推理），一种整合多源传感器证据与用户特定上下文先验的智能家居活动识别框架，以提升活动解释能力。TRACE不将识别视为局部分类问题，而是通过上下文推理解决模糊性、减少碎片化预测，并推断更语义化的活动。我们在公开基准及本智能家庭环境中部署的实验中评估TRACE，结果表明：TRACE提升了语义复杂活动的识别准确率，生成更时序连贯的预测，更贴合用户特定习惯，并在跨域迁移与缺失模态条件下保持稳健性能。这些发现证明了上下文推理对推进智能家居HAR的价值。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of distinguishing similar daily activities using sparse and ambiguous sensor data in smart homes, TRACE introduces a contextual framework that combines multi-source sensor evidence with user-specific behavioral priors to enhance activity recognition. Instead of relying on short-term local patterns, TRACE employs temporal reasoning to resolve ambiguities and generate more semantically precise and temporally coherent predictions. Experimental results on public benchmarks and real-world deployments show improved accuracy for complex activities, better alignment with user routines, and robustness under cross-domain and missing-sensor conditions.
为了解决智能家居中因传感器数据稀疏且模糊而导致的相似日常活动难以区分的问题，TRACE框架结合多源传感器证据与用户特定上下文先验，通过时序推理提升活动识别的准确性。它不依赖局部短时模式，而是整合更广泛的用户行为上下文，从而减少碎片化预测并推断更语义化的活动。实验结果表明，TRACE在复杂活动识别上表现更优，预测结果更符合用户习惯，并在跨域和缺失模态条件下仍保持稳定性能。
A second-order method on the Stiefel manifold via Newton$\unicode{x2013}$Schulz
Authors: Xinhui Xiong, Bin Gao, P. -A. Absil
First: 2026-05-04T17:18:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T17:18:11+00:00
Comments: 25 pages, 4 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Retraction-free approaches offer attractive low-cost alternatives to Riemannian methods on the Stiefel manifold, but they are often first-order, which may limit the efficiency under high-accuracy requirements. To this end, we propose a second-order method landing on the Stiefel manifold without invoking retractions, which is proved to enjoy local quadratic (or superlinear for its inexact variant) convergence. The update consists of the sum of (i) a component tangent to the level set of the constraint-defining function that aims to reduce the objective and (ii) a component normal to the same level set that reduces the infeasibility. Specifically, we construct the normal component via Newton$\unicode{x2013}$Schulz, a fixed-point iteration for orthogonalization. Moreover, we establish a geometric connection between the Newton$\unicode{x2013}$Schulz iteration and Stiefel manifolds, in which Newton$\unicode{x2013}$Schulz moves along the normal space. For the tangent component, we formulate a modified Newton equation that incorporates Newton$\unicode{x2013}$Schulz. Numerical experiments on the orthogonal Procrustes problem, principal component analysis, and real-data independent component analysis illustrate that the proposed method performs better than the existing methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过牛顿-舒尔兹方法在施蒂费尔流形上的二阶方法
无退化方法为施蒂费尔流形上的黎曼方法提供了成本较低的替代方案，但它们通常是低阶的，可能在高精度要求下效率受限。为此，我们提出了一种无需退化映射的施蒂费尔流形上的二阶方法，该方法被证明具有局部二次（或其不精确变体的超线性）收敛性。更新由两部分组成：(i) 与约束定义函数的等值面相切、旨在降低目标函数的部分；(ii) 与同一等值面正交、旨在降低不可行性的部分。具体而言，我们通过牛顿-舒尔兹方法（一种正交化固定点迭代）构造正交分量。此外，我们建立了牛顿-舒尔兹迭代与施蒂费尔流形之间的几何联系，其中牛顿-舒尔兹沿法空间移动。对于切向分量，我们构建了一个结合牛顿-舒尔兹的修正牛顿方程。在正交普罗克斯特问题、主成分分析和真实数据独立成分分析上的数值实验表明，所提方法优于现有方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient second-order optimization on the Stiefel manifold without retractions, this paper introduces a Newton-Schulz based method that achieves local quadratic convergence. The update decomposes into a tangent component reducing the objective and a normal component reducing infeasibility, with the normal part constructed via Newton-Schulz iteration for orthogonalization. The tangent component is derived from a modified Newton equation incorporating Newton-Schulz. Experiments on orthogonal Procrustes, PCA, and real-data ICA show the method outperforms existing first-order approaches in accuracy and efficiency.
为满足高精度需求下更高的计算效率，本文提出一种无需重缩放的Stiefel流形上的二阶优化方法。该方法将更新分解为切向与法向分量：切向分量通过结合Newton-Schulz的修正牛顿方程降低目标值，法向分量则利用Newton-Schulz作为正交化固定点迭代降低约束不满足度。理论上，该方法具有局部二次收敛性（其不精确变体为超线性），实验上在正交Procrustes、主成分分析及真实数据独立成分分析问题中均优于现有方法。
A Closed-Form Persistence-Landmark Pipeline for Certified Point-Cloud and Graph Classification
Authors: Sushovan Majhi, Atish Mitra, Žiga Virk, Pramita Bagchi
First: 2026-05-04T17:15:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T17:15:01+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce PLACE (Persistence-Landmark Analytic Classification Engine), a closed-form pipeline for classifying point clouds and graphs through their persistent-homology signatures. Three quantitative guarantees -- a margin-based excess-risk rate, a closed-form descriptor-selection rule, and a per-prediction certificate -- are derived from training labels alone, with no learned weights or held-out calibration. The embedding sums Mitra-Virk single-point coordinate functions over a sparse landmark grid; closed-form weights maximize a structural distortion constant $λ(ν)$ (a Lipschitz lower bound on $\mathcal{D}_n$ under non-interference). (i) An $O(kR/(Δ\sqrt{m_{\min}}))$ margin bound, driven by class-mean separation $Δ$ and embedding radius $R$, matched by a sample-starved minimax lower bound. (ii) The Mahalanobis margin under Ledoit-Wolf-shrunk covariance is the strongest closed-form descriptor selector on a heterogeneous 64-descriptor chemical-graph pool (mean Spearman $ρ\approx +0.54$ across 10 benchmarks, positive on 9 of 10); the isotropic surrogate $Δ/\sqrt\ell$ admits a closed-form selection-consistency rate on homogeneous (14-15 descriptor) protein/social pools. (iii) A training-time-decided certificate with no per-prediction overhead, in non-asymptotic Pinelis and asymptotic Gaussian plug-in forms. Empirically, PLACE is the strongest diagram-based method on Orbit5k and matches the strongest topology-based baseline within statistical noise on MUTAG and COX2. The remaining gaps fall into two diagnosable regimes: descriptor blindness on NCI1/NCI109, and pool-coverage limits elsewhere. Both radii exceed the firing threshold $\hatΔ/2$ on every benchmark at our training-set sizes, dominated by the $\sqrt\ell$ scaling of the multivariate-norm bound; the per-prediction certificate is constructive but not yet operational at these sizes.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于认证点云和图分类的闭式持久同调地标管道
我们提出了PLACE（持久同调地标分析分类引擎），一种通过持久同调签名对点云和图进行分类的闭式管道。仅从训练标签中推导出三项定量保证：基于边界的超额风险率、闭式描述符选择规则和每预测证书，无需学习权重或保留校准集。嵌入通过稀疏地标网格上的Mitra-Virk单点坐标函数求和得到；闭式权重最大化结构失真常数λ(ν)（在非干扰条件下是D_n的Lipschitz下界）。(i) 由类均值分离Δ和嵌入半径R驱动的O(kR/(Δ√m_min))边界，与样本匮乏的最小最大下界相匹配。(ii) Ledoit-Wolf收缩协方差下的Mahalanobis边界是异构64描述符化学图池上最强的闭式描述符选择器（10个基准测试平均Spearman ρ≈+0.54，9/10为正）；各向同性替代Δ/√ℓ在同质（14-15描述符）蛋白质/社交池上允许闭式选择一致性率。(iii) 训练时决定的证书，无每预测开销，具有非渐近Pinelis和渐近高斯插值形式。实证上，PLACE在Orbit5k上是基于图谱最强的方法，在MUTAG和COX2上与最强拓扑基线在统计噪声内相当。其余差距归为两类可诊断情形：NCI1/NCI109上的描述符盲区，及其他地方的池覆盖限制。所有基准测试在我们的训练集规模下，半径均超过触发阈值ˆΔ/2，受多元范数界中√ℓ缩放主导；每预测证书虽具构造性，但在当前规模下尚未可操作。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for certified, closed-form classification of point clouds and graphs using topological signatures, the paper introduces PLACE, a pipeline that constructs embeddings via sparse landmark grids and Mitra-Virk coordinate functions, optimizing weights to maximize a structural distortion constant. The method derives three quantitative guarantees: a margin bound tied to class separation and embedding radius, a Mahalanobis-based descriptor selector achieving high Spearman correlation across chemical graphs, and a training-time certificate with no per-prediction cost. Experiments show PLACE outperforms other diagram-based methods on Orbit5k and matches top topology-based baselines on MUTAG and COX2, though performance gaps remain on NCI1/NCI109 due to descriptor blindness and elsewhere due to pool-coverage limits, both linked to the multivariate-norm scaling of the margin bound.
为实现点云与图的可认证闭式分类，作者提出PLACE方法，利用持久同调与稀疏地标网格构建嵌入，通过Mitra-Virk坐标函数求和。该方法仅依赖训练标签推导三项定量保证：基于类间分离与嵌入半径的边际界、基于Ledoit-Wolf收缩协方差的闭式描述子选择规则（在化学图数据集上Spearman相关系数平均约0.54），以及无需额外推理开销的预测证书。实验表明，PLACE在Orbit5k上优于其他图谱方法，在MUTAG和COX2上与最强拓扑基线持平，但在NCI1/NCI109上因描述子盲区表现受限，其余数据集受限于描述子池覆盖不足，这些差距均与多变量范数边界中的√ℓ缩放相关。
VideoNet: A Large-Scale Dataset for Domain-Specific Action Recognition
Authors: Tanush Yadav, Mohammadreza Salehi, Jae Sung Park, Vivek Ramanujan, Hannaneh Hajishirzi, Yejin Choi, Ali Farhadi, Rohun Tripathi, Ranjay Krishna
Venue: CVPR 2026 Highlight
First: 2026-05-04T17:11:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T17:11:16+00:00
Comments: project website at https://tanu.sh/videonet
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Videos are unique in their ability to capture actions which transcend multiple frames. Accordingly, for many years action recognition was the quintessential task for video understanding. Unfortunately, due to a lack of sufficiently diverse and challenging data, modern vision-language models (VLMs) are no longer evaluated on their action recognition capabilities. To revitalize action recognition in the era of VLMs, we advocate for a returned focus on domain-specific actions. To this end, we introduce VideoNet, a domain-specific action recognition benchmark covering 1,000 distinct actions from 37 domains. We begin with a multiple-choice evaluation setting, where the difference between closed and open models is stark: Gemini 3.1 Pro attains 69.9% accuracy while Qwen3-VL-8B gets a mere 45.0%. To understand why VLMs struggle on VideoNet, we relax the questions into a binary setting, where random chance is 50%. Still, Qwen achieves only 59.2% accuracy. Further relaxing the evaluation setup, we provide $k\in\{1,2,3\}$ in-context examples of the action. Some models excel in the few-shot setting, while others falter; Qwen improves $+7.0\%$, while Gemini declines $-4.8\%$. Notably, these gains fall short of the $+13.6\%$ improvement in non-expert humans when given few-shot examples. Finding that VLMs struggle to fully exploit in-context examples, we shift from test-time improvements to the training side. We collect the first large-scale training dataset for domain-specific actions, totaling nearly 500k video question-answer pairs. Fine-tuning a Molmo2-4B model on our data, we surpass all open-weight 8B models on the VideoNet benchmark.
中文标题/摘要
标题：VideoNet：面向领域特定动作识别的大规模数据集
视频的独特之处在于其能够捕捉跨越多个帧的动作。因此，多年来动作识别一直是视频理解的核心任务。然而，由于缺乏足够多样且具有挑战性的数据，现代视觉-语言模型（VLMs）已不再被评估其动作识别能力。为在VLM时代重振动作识别，我们呼吁重新聚焦于领域特定动作。为此，我们推出了VideoNet，一个涵盖37个领域、1000种不同动作的领域特定动作识别基准。我们首先采用多项选择评估设置，闭源与开源模型表现差异显著：Gemini 3.1 Pro准确率达69.9%，而Qwen3-VL-8B仅为45.0%。为理解VLMs在VideoNet上的表现不佳原因，我们将问题简化为二元设置（随机猜测准确率为50%），Qwen仍仅达59.2%。进一步放宽评估设置，我们提供k∈{1,2,3}个上下文示例动作。部分模型在少样本设置中表现优异，而另一些则表现不佳；Qwen提升+7.0%，Gemini下降-4.8%。值得注意的是，这些提升仍不及非专家人类在少样本示例下+13.6%的提升。鉴于VLMs难以充分利用上下文示例，我们转向训练端优化。我们收集了首个面向领域特定动作的大规模训练数据集，包含近50万条视频问答对。在该数据上微调Molmo2-4B模型，我们在VideoNet基准上超越所有开源8B模型。

Videos are unique in their ability to capture actions which transcend multiple frames.
为应对视觉语言模型因缺乏领域特定视频数据而弱化动作识别评估的问题，作者构建了包含37领域1000个动作的VideoNet基准数据集。在多项选择、二分类及少样本设置下，模型表现差异显著：Gemini 3.1 Pro在多项选择中达69.9%，Qwen3-VL-8B仅45.0%；即使提供少量示例，模型提升有限（如Qwen仅+7.0%），远低于人类+13.6%的提升。为此，作者构建了50万条视频问答训练数据，并微调Molmo2-4B模型，使其在VideoNet上超越所有开源8B模型。
HAAS: A Policy-Aware Framework for Adaptive Task Allocation Between Humans and Artificial Intelligence Systems
Authors: Vicente Pelechanoa, Antoni Mestre, Manoli Albert, Miriam Gil
First: 2026-05-04T17:09:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T17:09:21+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Deciding how to distribute work between humans and AI systems is a central challenge in organisational design. Most approaches treat this as a binary choice, yet the operational reality is richer: humans and AI routinely share tasks or take complementary roles depending on context, fatigue, and the stakes involved. Governing that distribution -- balancing efficiency, oversight, and human capability -- remains an open problem. This paper presents Human-AI Adaptive Symbiosis (HAAS), an implemented framework for adaptive task allocation in software engineering and manufacturing. HAAS combines two coupled components: a rule-based expert system that enforces governance constraints before any learning occurs, and a contextual-bandit learner that selects among feasible collaboration modes from outcome feedback. Task-agent fit is represented through five auditable cognitive dimensions and a five-mode autonomy spectrum -- from human-only to fully autonomous -- embedded in a reproducible benchmark spanning both domains. Three empirical findings emerge. First, governance is not a binary switch but a tunable design variable: tighter constraints predictably convert autonomous AI assignments into supervised collaborations, with domain-specific costs and benefits. Second, in manufacturing, stronger governance can improve operational performance and reduce fatigue simultaneously -- a workload-buffering effect that contradicts the usual framing of governance as pure overhead. Third, no single governance setting dominates across all contexts; moderate governance becomes increasingly competitive as the learner accumulates experience within the governed action space. Together, these findings position HAAS as a pre-deployment workbench for comparing and inspecting human--AI allocation policies before organisational commitment.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HAAS：一种面向人类与人工智能系统间自适应任务分配的政策感知框架
在组织设计中，如何在人类与人工智能系统间分配工作是一个核心挑战。多数方法将其视为二元选择，但实际操作中更为复杂：人类与人工智能常根据情境、疲劳程度和任务重要性共享任务或互补协作。如何管理这种分配——平衡效率、监督与人类能力——仍属开放性问题。本文提出人类-人工智能自适应共生（HAAS）框架，用于软件工程与制造领域的自适应任务分配。HAAS结合两个耦合组件：在任何学习发生前，规则驱动的专家系统强制执行治理约束；同时，基于上下文的多臂老虎机学习器从结果反馈中选择可行的协作模式。任务-代理适配性通过五个可审计的认知维度和一个五模式自主性光谱（从纯人类执行到完全自主）体现，该光谱嵌入跨两个领域的可复现基准。三个实证发现：第一，治理并非二元开关，而是可调设计变量：更严格的约束可预测地将自主AI任务转化为监督协作，伴随领域特异的成本与收益。第二，在制造领域，更强治理可同时提升运营表现并降低疲劳——一种与治理通常被视为纯开销的常规框架相悖的“工作负载缓冲效应”。第三，无单一治理设置在所有情境中占优；随着学习者在受约束行动空间内积累经验，适度治理逐渐更具竞争力。综上，HAAS为组织承诺前提供了一个部署前工作台，用于比较与检验人类-人工智能任务分配政策。
Summary / 总结
Deciding how to distribute work between humans and AI systems is a central challenge in organisational design.
为突破人类与AI任务分配的二元框架，HAAS提出一种政策感知框架，根据情境、疲劳度和任务重要性动态调整协作模式。该框架结合规则驱动的治理系统（在学习前强制约束）与上下文多臂老虎机学习器（基于反馈选择五种自主性模式）。实验表明，治理并非开关而是可调变量：更严格约束会将AI任务转为监督协作，伴随领域特异性成本与收益。在制造业中，更强治理反而提升效率并降低疲劳，挑战了治理即开销的传统认知。无单一治理策略普适最优，适度治理随系统在约束空间内经验积累而愈发有效。
Existence, Asymptotic Behavior, and Numerical Analysis of a Generalized Abel Differential Equation with Applications in Financial Modeling
Authors: Dragos-Patru Covei
First: 2026-05-04T17:07:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T17:07:25+00:00
Comments: 22 pages, 3 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present a comprehensive investigation into a generalized class of Abel ordinary differential equations (ODEs), extending the classical cubic form to arbitrary polynomial nonlinearities of degree $n \geq 1$. This work provides a rigorous treatment of the existence and uniqueness of regular solutions on both bounded and unbounded domains. Utilizing a unified barrier-based approach, we derive sharp growth rates and prove the existence of exact asymptotic plateaus, establishing the first systematic treatment of such generalizations in the literature. The regularity of solutions is rigorously justified through the lens of Sobolev spaces and classical ODE theory. To complement our analytical findings, we implement a high-order numerical framework based on Radau IIA implicit Runge--Kutta schemes, providing detailed stability arguments and error analysis. The numerical results demonstrate exceptional consistency with our theoretical predictions, particularly in capturing the asymptotic behavior. Finally, we discuss the implications of our results for real-world models, including Merton-type credit risk analysis and Hamilton--Jacobi--Bellman stochastic control problems, bridging the gap between abstract nonlinear dynamics and applied science.
中文标题/摘要
标题：广义阿贝尔微分方程的存在性、渐近行为与数值分析及其在金融建模中的应用
本文对一类广义阿贝尔常微分方程（ODE）进行全面研究，将经典三次形式推广至任意次数 $n \geq 1$ 的多项式非线性情形。本工作对有界与无界域上正则解的存在性与唯一性进行了严格处理。通过统一的障碍方法，我们推导出精确的增长速率并证明了精确渐近平台的存在，首次系统性地处理了此类广义方程。解的正则性通过索博列夫空间与经典ODE理论严格论证。为补充分析结果，我们基于Radau IIA隐式Runge-Kutta方法构建了高阶数值框架，提供稳定性论证与误差分析。数值结果与理论预测高度一致，尤其在渐近行为捕捉方面表现优异。最后，我们讨论了结果在现实模型中的应用，包括Merton型信用风险分析与Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman随机控制问题，弥合了抽象非线性动力学与应用科学之间的鸿沟。
Summary / 总结
We present a comprehensive investigation into a generalized class of Abel ordinary differential equations (ODEs), extending the classical cubic form to arbitrary polynomial nonlinearities of degree $n \geq 1$.
为拓展经典阿贝尔微分方程以适应金融建模中的更高阶多项式非线性需求，该研究系统分析了任意阶数n≥1的广义阿贝尔常微分方程的解的存在性、唯一性及渐近行为。通过障碍法建立精确增长速率并证明渐近平台的存在，理论分析结合索博列夫空间与经典ODE理论。数值上采用高阶Radau IIA隐式龙格-库塔方法，结果与理论预测高度一致，尤其在捕捉长期渐近行为方面表现优异。研究成果应用于Merton信用风险与HJB随机控制问题，体现理论与实际应用的衔接。
Compress Then Adapt? No, Do It Together via Task-aware Union of Subspaces
Authors: Jingze Ge, Yun Liu, Xue Geng, Wanqi Dong, Wang Zhe Mark, Min Wu, Xulei Yang
First: 2026-05-04T17:05:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T17:05:45+00:00
Comments: 15 pages, 3 figures, supplementary material included
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Adapting large pretrained models to diverse tasks is now routine, yet the two dominant strategies of parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) and low-rank compression are typically composed in sequence. This decoupled practice first compresses and then fine-tunes adapters, potentially misaligning the compressed subspace with downstream objectives and squandering a global parameter budget. To overcome this limitation, we introduce JACTUS (Joint Adaptation and Compression with a Task-aware Union of Subspaces), a single framework that unifies compression and adaptation. From a small calibration set, JACTUS estimates input and pre-activation gradient covariances, forms their orthogonal union with the pretrained weight subspace, performs a projected low-rank approximation inside this union, allocates rank globally by marginal gain per parameter, and trains only a compact core matrix. This explicitly mitigates the potential misalignment between the compressed subspace and downstream objectives by coupling the directions preserved for compression with those required for adaptation, yielding a deployable low-rank model that avoids retaining full frozen weights while enabling fast and robust tuning. On vision, JACTUS attains an average 89.2% accuracy on ViT-Base across eight datasets at 80% retained parameters, surpassing strong 100% PEFT baselines (e.g., DoRA 87.9%). On language, JACTUS achieves an 80.9% average on Llama2-7B commonsense QA at the same 80% retained-parameter budget, outperforming 100% PEFT (e.g., DoRA 79.7%) and exceeding prior compress-then-finetune pipelines under the same ratained-parameter budget. We will release code.
中文标题/摘要
标题：先压缩再适配？不，应同时进行，通过任务感知的子空间联合
将大型预训练模型适配到多样化任务已成常规，但目前主流的参数高效微调（PEFT）与低秩压缩策略通常按顺序组合。这种解耦做法先压缩再微调适配器，可能导致压缩子空间与下游目标不匹配，并浪费全局参数预算。为克服此局限，我们提出JACTUS（任务感知子空间联合下的联合适配与压缩），一个统一压缩与适配的框架。从少量校准数据中，JACTUS估计输入与预激活梯度协方差，将其与预训练权重子空间正交联合，于该联合空间内执行投影低秩近似，按每参数边际增益全局分配秩，并仅训练紧凑核心矩阵。该方法显式缓解压缩子空间与下游目标潜在不匹配问题，通过将压缩保留方向与适配所需方向耦合，生成可部署的低秩模型，避免保留完整冻结权重，同时支持快速稳健微调。在视觉任务上，JACTUS在八组数据上以80%参数保留率于ViT-Base模型上平均达到89.2%准确率，超越强基线100% PEFT（如DoRA 87.9%）。在语言任务上，JACTUS在Llama2-7B常识问答任务中以相同80%参数保留率平均达到80.9%，优于100% PEFT（如DoRA 79.7%），且在相同参数保留率下超越先压缩后微调的流程。我们将开源代码。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of sequentially compressing and fine-tuning large models, which risks misalignment between compressed subspaces and downstream tasks, the paper proposes JACTUS, a unified framework that jointly performs compression and adaptation. It leverages a small calibration set to estimate gradient covariances, constructs a task-aware union of subspaces combining pretrained weights and gradient directions, and performs low-rank approximation within this union while globally allocating rank by marginal gain per parameter. The resulting compact core matrix enables fast, robust tuning without retaining full frozen weights. Experiments show JACTUS achieves 89.2% accuracy on ViT-Base across eight vision datasets at 80% parameter retention, outperforming 100% PEFT baselines like DoRA (87.9%). On language, it scores 80.9% on Llama2-7B commonsense QA at the same budget, surpassing DoRA (79.7%) and previous compress-then-finetune methods.
为解决先压缩后微调大模型时压缩子空间与下游任务目标可能不一致、浪费参数预算的问题，本文提出JACTUS框架，联合执行压缩与适应。该方法利用小规模校准集估计输入与预激活梯度协方差，构建包含预训练权重与梯度方向的联合子空间，于其中执行低秩近似，并按每参数边际增益全局分配秩，仅保留紧凑的核心矩阵。实验表明，JACTUS在80%参数保留率下，于ViT-Base八项视觉任务上平均准确率达89.2%，超越100% PEFT基线（如DoRA 87.9%）；在Llama2-7B常识问答任务上平均得分80.9%，优于DoRA（79.7%）及同类压缩后微调方法。
Beyond Crash: Hijacking Your Autonomous Vehicle for Fun and Profit
Authors: Qi Sun, Ahmed Abdo, Luis Burbano, Ziyang Li, Yaxing Yao, Alvaro Cardenas, Yinzhi Cao
First: 2026-02-06T22:57:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T17:04:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Autonomous Vehicles (AVs), especially vision-based AVs, are rapidly being deployed without human operators. As AVs operate in safety-critical environments, understanding their robustness in an adversarial environment is an important research problem. Prior physical adversarial attacks on vision-based autonomous vehicles predominantly target immediate safety failures (e.g., a crash, a traffic-rule violation, or a transient lane departure) by inducing a short-lived perception or control error. This paper shows a qualitatively different risk: a long-horizon route integrity compromise, where an attacker gradually steers a victim AV away from its intended route and into an attacker-chosen destination while the victim continues to drive ``normally.'' This will not pose a danger to the victim vehicle itself, but also to potential passengers sitting inside the vehicle, who may not notice the route changes. In this paper, we design and implement the first adversarial framework, called JackZebra, which performs route-level hijacking of a vision-based end-to-end driving stack using a physically plausible attacker vehicle with a reconfigurable display and a camera sensor mounted on the rear. The central challenge is temporal persistence: adversarial influence must remain effective in changing viewpoints, lighting, weather, traffic, and the victim's continual replanning -- without triggering conspicuous failures. Our key insight is to treat route hijacking as a closed-loop control problem and to convert adversarial patches into steering primitives that can be selected online via an interactive adjustment loop based on observed victim behavior using the rear camera. Our evaluations in both simulated and real-world scenarios show that JackZebra can successfully hijack victim vehicles to deviate from original routes and stop at places designated by the adversary with a high success rate.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越碰撞：劫持自动驾驶汽车以获取乐趣与利润
自动驾驶汽车（AV），尤其是基于视觉的AV，正迅速在无人操作的情况下部署。由于AV在安全关键环境中运行，理解其在对抗性环境中的鲁棒性是一个重要的研究问题。此前针对基于视觉的自动驾驶汽车的物理对抗攻击主要通过诱导短暂的感知或控制错误，导致即时安全失效（如碰撞、交通规则违规或短暂车道偏离）。本文揭示了一种定性不同的风险：长期路线完整性破坏，即攻击者逐步将受害AV偏离其原定路线，引导至攻击者选定的目的地，而受害车辆仍“正常”行驶。这不会对受害车辆本身构成危险，但可能危及车内乘客，他们可能未察觉路线变化。 本文设计并实现了首个对抗框架JackZebra，该框架利用一辆具备可重构显示屏和后置摄像头的物理上可行的攻击车辆，对基于视觉的端到端驾驶系统实施路线级劫持。核心挑战在于时间持久性：对抗性影响必须在视角、光照、天气、交通及受害车辆持续重规划等变化下保持有效，同时不触发显眼的失效。我们的关键洞见是将路线劫持视为闭环控制问题，并将对抗性贴片转换为可在线选择的转向基本操作，通过后置摄像头观察受害车辆行为，借助交互式调整循环实时调整。在仿真和真实场景中的评估表明，JackZebra能以高成功率成功劫持受害车辆偏离原路线，并停靠攻击者指定地点。
Summary / 总结
Autonomous Vehicles (AVs), especially vision-based AVs, are rapidly being deployed without human operators.
为评估视觉型自动驾驶汽车在对抗环境中的鲁棒性，该研究提出JackZebra框架，首次实现对自动驾驶车辆的长期路线劫持。攻击者车辆配备可重构显示屏和后置摄像头，通过物理上可行的方式在不触发明显故障的前提下，持续影响目标车辆的感知，使其偏离原路线并停靠攻击者指定地点。该框架将路线劫持视为闭环控制问题，通过实时观察目标车辆行为并动态生成转向指令，实现在线调整。仿真与真实环境实验表明，该方法成功率高，目标车辆在保持“正常”驾驶的同时被成功引导至攻击者设定位置。
First-Order Efficiency for Probabilistic Value Estimation via A Statistical Viewpoint
Authors: Ziqi Liu, Kiljae Lee, Yuan Zhang, Weijing Tang
First: 2026-05-04T17:02:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T17:02:17+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Probabilistic values, including Shapley values and semivalues, provide a model-agnostic framework to attribute the behavior of a black-box model to data points or features, with a wide range of applications including explainable artificial intelligence and data valuation. However, their exact computation requires utility evaluations over exponentially many coalitions, making Monte Carlo approximation essential in modern machine learning applications. Existing estimators are often developed through different identification strategies, including weighted averages, self-normalized weighting, regression adjustment, and weighted least squares. Our key observation is that these seemingly distinct constructions share a common first-order error structure, in which the leading term is an augmented inverse-probability weighted influence term determined by the sampling law and a working surrogate function. This first-order representation yields an explicit expression for the leading mean squared error (MSE), which characterizes how the sampling law and the surrogate jointly determine statistical efficiency. Guided by this criterion, we propose an Efficiency-Aware Surrogate-adjusted Estimator (EASE) that directly chooses the sampling law and surrogate to minimize the first-order MSE. We demonstrate that EASE consistently outperforms state-of-the-art estimators for various probabilistic values.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过统计视角实现概率价值估计的一阶效率
概率值（包括Shapley值和半值）提供了一种模型无关的框架，用于将黑盒模型的行为归因于数据点或特征，广泛应用于可解释人工智能和数据估值等领域。然而，其精确计算需要在指数级多的联盟上进行效用评估，因此在现代机器学习应用中，蒙特卡洛近似至关重要。现有估计器通常通过不同的识别策略开发，包括加权平均、自归一化加权、回归调整和加权最小二乘法。我们的关键观察是，这些看似不同的构造共享相同的首一阶误差结构，其中主导项是一个由采样分布和工作代理函数决定的增强逆概率加权影响项。该一阶表示给出了主导均方误差（MSE）的显式表达式，刻画了采样分布和代理函数如何共同决定统计效率。基于此准则，我们提出了一种效率感知的代理调整估计器（EASE），直接选择采样分布和代理函数以最小化一阶MSE。我们证明，EASE在各种概率值上始终优于现有最先进估计器。
Summary / 总结
Probabilistic values, including Shapley values and semivalues, provide a model-agnostic framework to attribute the behavior of a black-box model to data points or features, with a wide range of applications including explainable artificial intelligence and data valuation.
为了解决概率值（如Shapley值）精确计算的计算复杂性问题，本文从统计视角统一了现有估计方法，发现它们共享相同的首阶误差结构，该结构由采样分布与代理函数共同决定的逆概率加权影响项主导。基于最小化首阶均方误差的准则，作者提出效率感知代理调整估计器（EASE），联合优化采样策略与代理函数以提升统计效率。实验表明，EASE在多种概率值估计任务中持续优于现有最先进方法。
SCPRM: A Schema-aware Cumulative Process Reward Model for Knowledge Graph Question Answering
Authors: Jiujiu Chen, Yazheng Liu, Sihong Xie, Hui Xiong
First: 2026-05-04T16:56:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T16:56:01+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models excel at complex reasoning, yet evaluating their intermediate steps remains challenging. Although process reward models provide step-wise supervision, they often suffer from a risk compensation effect, where incorrect steps are offset by later correct ones, assigning high rewards to flawed reasoning paths. This issue is further exacerbated in knowledge graph (KG) reasoning, as there may exist multiple paths between the start and end entities in the KGs, and a risky step can make the reasoning path flawed. Those limitations are problematic in risk-sensitive tasks such as medical and legal KG reasoning. To address the issues, we propose a Schema-aware Cumulative Process Reward Model (SCPRM) that evaluates reasoning paths by conditioning on the reasoning prefix , and incorporating schema distance between current reasoning step and the implicit target parsed from the query, which provides cumulative and future rewards to guide the path explorations. We further integrate SCPRM into Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) as SCPRM-MCTS to conduct multi-hop reasoning on KGs for question answering (QA) tasks. Across medical and legal KGQA and CWQ, SCPRM-MCTS improves the performance of Hits@k by an average of 1.18% over strong baselines, demonstrating more accurate and risk-sensitive reasoning evaluation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SCPRM：一种面向知识图谱问答的模式感知累积过程奖励模型
大语言模型擅长复杂推理，但评估其中间步骤仍具挑战。尽管过程奖励模型提供逐步监督，却常受风险补偿效应影响，即错误步骤被后续正确步骤抵消，导致对有缺陷推理路径给予高奖励。在知识图谱（KG）推理中，该问题更严重，因KG中起点与终点实体间可能存在多条路径，单步风险即可使推理路径失效，这对医疗、法律等风险敏感任务尤为不利。为此，我们提出一种模式感知累积过程奖励模型（SCPRM），通过结合推理前缀与当前步骤与查询隐含目标间的模式距离，对推理路径进行评估，提供累积性与前瞻性奖励以引导路径探索。进一步，我们将SCPRM集成至蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）中，形成SCPRM-MCTS，用于知识图谱上的多跳推理问答任务。在医疗与法律KG问答及CWQ数据集上，SCPRM-MCTS相较强基线模型平均提升Hits@k 1.18%，展现出更准确、更敏感的风险推理评估能力。

Large language models excel at complex reasoning, yet evaluating their intermediate steps remains challenging.
为解决知识图谱问答中中间推理步骤评估不准确的问题——特别是错误步骤可能被后续正确步骤补偿的问题，本文提出SCPRM模型，该模型根据当前推理前缀和当前步骤与查询隐含目标之间的模式距离来累积奖励，引导路径探索。将SCPRM集成到蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）中形成SCPRM-MCTS，用于多跳推理。在医学、法律和CWQ数据集上的实验表明，SCPRM-MCTS相较强基线模型平均提升Hits@k 1.18%，体现出更准确且风险敏感的推理评估能力。
From Skill Text to Skill Structure: The Scheduling-Structural-Logical Representation for Agent Skills
Authors: Qiliang Liang, Hansi Wang, Zhong Liang, Yang Liu
First: 2026-04-27T04:25:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T16:52:56+00:00
Comments: 23 pages, 1 figure
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly rely on reusable skills: capability packages that combine instructions, control flow, constraints, and tool calls. In current agent systems, however, skills are still represented by text-heavy artifacts, mainly SKILL{.}md-style documents whose machine-usable evidence remains embedded largely in natural-language descriptions. As a result, skill-centered agent systems face a representation problem: both managing skill collections and using skills during agent execution require reasoning over invocation interfaces, execution structure, and concrete side effects, but these signals are often entangled in a single textual surface. An explicit representation of skill knowledge may therefore help make these artifacts easier for machines to acquire and leverage. Drawing on Memory Organization Packets, Script Theory, and Conceptual Dependency from Schank and Abelson's classical work on cognitive linguistic representation, we introduce what is, to our knowledge, the first structured representation for agent skill artifacts that disentangles skill-level scheduling signals, scene-level execution structure, and logic-level action/resource-use evidence: the Scheduling-Structural-Logical (SSL) representation. We instantiate SSL with an LLM-based normalizer and evaluate SSL-derived representations in two tasks, Skill Discovery and Risk Assessment. The experiment shows that SSL significantly outperforms the text-only baselines: in Skill Discovery, MRR@50 improves from 0.649 to 0.729; in Risk Assessment, macro F1 improves from 0.409 to 0.509. These findings suggest that an explicit, source-grounded structure can make agent skills easier to search and review, positioning SSL as a practical step toward more inspectable, reusable, and operationally actionable skill representations, rather than a finished standard or end-to-end skill-management mechanism.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从技能文本到技能结构：面向代理技能的调度-结构-逻辑表示
大型语言模型（LLM）代理日益依赖可复用技能：将指令、控制流、约束和工具调用整合的能力包。然而，在当前代理系统中，技能仍以文本密集型人工制品表示，主要为SKILL{.}md风格文档，其机器可使用的证据大多嵌入自然语言描述中。因此，以技能为中心的代理系统面临表示问题：管理技能集合和在代理执行中使用技能均需推理调用接口、执行结构和具体副作用，但这些信号常纠缠于单一文本表面。显式表示技能知识可帮助机器更易获取和利用这些人工制品。借鉴Schank与Abelson关于认知语言学表示的经典工作中的记忆组织包、脚本理论和概念依赖理论，我们提出了面向代理技能人工制品的首个结构化表示——调度-结构-逻辑（SSL）表示，该表示将技能级调度信号、场景级执行结构和逻辑级动作/资源使用证据解耦。我们以基于LLM的归一化器实例化SSL，并在两项任务——技能发现与风险评估中评估SSL衍生表示。实验表明，SSL显著优于纯文本基线：在技能发现中，MRR@50从0.649提升至0.729；在风险评估中，宏F1从0.409提升至0.509。这些发现表明，显式、源基础的结构可使代理技能更易搜索和审查，SSL是迈向更可检视、可复用、可操作技能表示的实用步骤，而非最终标准或端到端技能管理机制。

FlexSQL: Flexible Exploration and Execution Make Better Text-to-SQL Agents
Authors: Quang Hieu Pham, Yang He, Ping Nie, Canwen Xu, Davood Rafiei, Yuepeng Wang, Xi Ye, Jocelyn Qiaochu Chen
First: 2026-05-04T16:51:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T16:51:31+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Text-to-SQL over large analytical databases requires navigating complex schemas, resolving ambiguous queries, and grounding decisions in actual data. Most current systems follow a fixed pipeline where schema elements are retrieved once upfront and the database is only revisited for post-hoc repair, limiting recovery from early mistakes. We present FlexSQL, a text-to-SQL agent whose core design principle is flexible database interaction: the agent can explore schema structure, inspect data values, and run verification queries at any point during reasoning. FlexSQL generates diverse execution plans to cover multiple query interpretations, implements each plan in either SQL or Python depending on the task, and uses a two-tiered repair mechanism that can backtrack from code-level errors to plan-level revisions. On Spider2-Snow, using gpt-oss-120b, FlexSQL achieves a 65.4\% score, outperforming strong open-source baselines that use stronger, larger models such as gpt-o3 and DeepSeek-R1. When integrated into a general-purpose coding agent (as skills in Claude Code), our approach yields over 10\% relative improvement on Spider2-Snow. Further analysis shows that flexible exploration and flexible execution jointly contribute to the effectiveness of our approach, highlighting flexibility as a key design principle. Our code is available at: https://github.com/StringNLPLAB/FlexSQL
中文标题/摘要
标题：FlexSQL：灵活探索与执行使文本到SQL代理更优
在大型分析数据库上进行文本到SQL转换需要处理复杂模式、解决模糊查询，并将决策基于实际数据。当前大多数系统采用固定流水线，即一次性提前获取模式元素，仅在事后修复时重新访问数据库，限制了对早期错误的恢复能力。我们提出FlexSQL，其核心设计原则是灵活的数据库交互：代理可在推理过程中随时探索模式结构、检查数据值并运行验证查询。FlexSQL生成多样化的执行计划以覆盖多种查询解释，根据任务选择用SQL或Python实现每个计划，并采用两级修复机制，可从代码级错误回溯至计划级修订。在Spider2-Snow数据集上，使用gpt-oss-120b模型，FlexSQL得分达65.4%，优于使用更强更大模型（如gpt-o3和DeepSeek-R1）的强开源基线。当集成到通用编程代理（如Claude Code中的技能）时，我们的方法在Spider2-Snow上实现超过10%的相对提升。进一步分析表明，灵活探索与灵活执行共同提升了方法的有效性，凸显灵活性作为关键设计原则。代码开源地址：https://github.com/StringNLPLAB/FlexSQL
Summary / 总结
Text-to-SQL over large analytical databases requires navigating complex schemas, resolving ambiguous queries, and grounding decisions in actual data.
为了解决现有固定流程文本到SQL系统在早期错误时无法恢复的问题，FlexSQL提出了一种支持动态数据库交互的智能体设计，允许在推理过程中灵活探索模式结构、检查数据值并按需执行验证查询。该智能体生成多种查询解释方案，根据任务复杂度选择SQL或Python执行，并采用两级修复机制，可从代码层错误回溯至计划层进行修正。在Spider2-Snow数据集上，使用gpt-oss-120b模型时，FlexSQL达到65.4%准确率，超越包括gpt-o3和DeepSeek-R1在内的多个强开源基线模型，且与通用编程智能体集成后提升超10%。分析表明，灵活探索与灵活执行共同驱动性能提升，验证了灵活性作为核心设计原则的有效性。
IConFace: Identity-Structure Asymmetric Conditioning for Unified Reference-Aware Face Restoration
Authors: Axi Niu, Jinyang Zhang, Senyan Qing
First: 2026-05-04T16:49:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T16:49:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Blind face restoration is highly ill-posed under severe degradation, where identity-critical details may be missing from the degraded input. Same-identity references reduce this ambiguity, but mismatched pose, expression, illumination, age, makeup, or local facial states can lead to overuse of reference appearance. We propose \textbf{IConFace}, a unified reference-aware and no-reference framework with identity--structure asymmetric conditioning. References are distilled into a norm-weighted global AdaFace identity anchor for image-only modulation, while the degraded image is reinforced as the spatial structure anchor through low-rank residuals and block-wise degraded cross-attention with two-route memory. The resulting single checkpoint exploits references when available and falls back to no-reference restoration when absent, improving identity consistency, fine-detail recovery, and degraded-only restoration quality in a unified model.
中文标题/摘要
标题：IConFace：面向统一参考感知人脸修复的身份-结构非对称条件化方法
在严重退化情况下，盲人脸修复高度不适定，退化输入中可能缺失身份关键细节。同身份参考图像可降低这种模糊性，但姿态、表情、光照、年龄、妆容或局部面部状态不匹配会导致过度依赖参考外观。我们提出\textbf{IConFace}，一种统一的参考感知与无参考框架，采用身份-结构非对称条件化。参考图像被蒸馏为带归一化权重的全局AdaFace身份锚点，用于图像仅有的调制；而退化图像则通过低秩残差和双路记忆的块级退化交叉注意力，强化为空间结构锚点。该单个模型在有参考时利用参考，在无参考时回退至无参考修复，从而提升身份一致性、细粒度细节恢复及仅退化图像修复质量。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of restoring severely degraded faces where identity details are often lost, the paper introduces IConFace, a unified framework that leverages reference images when available and defaults to no-reference restoration otherwise. It employs asymmetric conditioning: reference images are distilled into a global AdaFace identity anchor for identity-aware modulation, while the degraded image itself is enhanced as a spatial structure anchor via low-rank residuals and block-wise cross-attention with dual memory routes. Experiments show that IConFace achieves superior identity consistency, fine-detail recovery, and degraded-only restoration quality compared to existing methods, all within a single model checkpoint.
为解决严重退化人脸中关键身份细节丢失的问题，本文提出IConFace框架，统一支持参考图像和无参考场景下的面部修复。该框架采用身份-结构不对称条件化机制：参考图像被蒸馏为全局AdaFace身份锚点用于图像调制，而退化图像本身则通过低秩残差与双路记忆模块的块级交叉注意力强化为结构锚点。实验表明，该设计在单个模型中显著提升身份一致性、细粒度细节恢复能力及无参考修复质量。
Autonomous LLM Agent Worms: Cross-Platform Propagation, Automated Discovery and Temporal Re-Entry Defense
Authors: Mingming Zha, Xiaofeng Wang
First: 2026-05-04T16:49:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T16:49:29+00:00
Comments: 21 pages, 1 figure, 6 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Autonomous LLM agents operate as long-running processes with persistent workspaces, memory files, scheduled task state, and messaging integrations. These features create a new propagation risk: attacker-influenced content can be written into persistent agent state, re-enter the LLM decision context through scheduled autoloading, and drive high-risk actions including configuration changes and cross-agent transmission. We present the first systematic framework for automated analysis of persistent worm propagation in file-backed multi-agent LLM ecosystems. SSCGV, our automated source-code graph analyzer, traces data flow from file I/O to LLM context injection points and ranks carriers by context injection position without manual analysis. SRPO, our summary-resilient payload optimizer, generates worm payloads robust to LLM-mediated summarization and paraphrasing across multi-hop communication. Evaluated on three production agent frameworks, we demonstrate zero-click autonomous propagation, 3-hop cross-platform transmission without platform-specific adaptation, inter-agent privilege escalation, and data exfiltration. We identify two empirical insights: user prompt carriers achieve higher attack compliance than system prompt carriers, and read operations represent the primary integrity threat in LLM-mediated systems. To defend against this class of attacks, we develop RTW-A, proven under a formal No Persistent Worm Propagation theorem. RTW blocks write-before-exposed-read re-entry; sealed configuration protects static files; typed memory promotion prevents untrusted summaries from entering trusted memory; and capability attenuation limits high-risk actions after external reads. These mechanisms eliminate the persistence, re-entry, action chain while preserving ordinary workflows. Affected systems are anonymized pending coordinated disclosure.
中文标题/摘要
标题：自主LLM代理蠕虫：跨平台传播、自动化发现与时间重入防御
自主LLM代理作为长期运行进程，具备持久化工作区、内存文件、计划任务状态及消息集成。这些特性带来新的传播风险：攻击者可将受控内容写入持久化代理状态，通过计划自动加载重新进入LLM决策上下文，驱动高危行为，如配置变更与跨代理传播。本文首次提出系统性框架，用于自动化分析基于文件的多代理LLM生态中持久化蠕虫传播。SSCGV，我们的自动化源代码图分析器，追踪从文件I/O到LLM上下文注入点的数据流，并按上下文注入位置自动排序载体，无需人工分析。SRPO，我们的抗摘要鲁棒性载荷优化器，生成在多跳通信中经LLM中介摘要与改写仍具鲁棒性的蠕虫载荷。在三个生产级代理框架上评估，我们展示了零点击自主传播、无需平台适配的三跳跨平台传输、代理间权限提升及数据外泄。我们发现两个实证洞见：用户提示载体比系统提示载体攻击合规性更高，读操作是LLM中介系统中的主要完整性威胁。为防御此类攻击，我们开发RTW-A，其在形式化“无持久蠕虫传播”定理下被证明有效。RTW阻止写入前读取重入；密封配置保护静态文件；类型化内存提升防止不可信摘要进入可信内存；能力衰减限制外部读取后高危行为。这些机制消除持久性、重入及行为链，同时保留常规工作流。受影响系统将在协调披露前匿名化。
Summary / 总结
Autonomous LLM agents operate as long-running processes with persistent workspaces, memory files, scheduled task state, and messaging integrations.
为应对恶意内容通过LLM代理持久化状态触发自主传播的风险，本文提出SSCGV和SRPO两个自动化工具，分别用于追踪文件I/O到LLM上下文注入点的数据流并优化抗摘要/改写能力的传播载荷。在三个生产框架上的实验表明，可实现零点击传播、跨平台三跳传输、权限提升与数据外泄。实证发现用户提示载体攻击成功率高于系统提示载体，读操作是LLM系统中最主要的完整性威胁。为防御此类攻击，本文提出RTW-A机制，通过写前暴露读阻断、配置密封、类型化内存提升与能力衰减，成功消除持久化传播路径，同时保持正常工作流。
MOSAIC: Multi-agent Orchestration for Task-Intelligent Scientific Coding
Authors: Siddeshwar Raghavan, Tanwi Mallick
Venue: NeurIPS
First: 2025-10-09T20:35:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T16:48:58+00:00
Comments: Accepted at NeurIPS DL4C workshop
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present MOSAIC, a multi-agent Large Language Model (LLM) framework for solving challenging scientific coding tasks. Unlike general-purpose coding, scientific workflows require algorithms that are rigorous, interconnected with deep domain knowledge, and incorporate domain-specific reasoning, as well as algorithm iteration without requiring I/O test cases. Many scientific problems also require a sequence of subproblems to be solved, leading to the final desired result. MOSAIC is designed as a training-free framework with specially designed agents to self-reflect, create the rationale, code, and debug within a student-teacher paradigm to address the challenges of scientific code generation. This design facilitates stepwise problem decomposition, targeted error correction, and, when combined with our Consolidated Context Window (CCW), mitigates LLM hallucinations when solving complex scientific tasks involving chained subproblems. We evaluate MOSAIC on scientific coding benchmarks and demonstrate that our specialized agentic framework outperforms existing approaches in terms of accuracy, robustness, and interpretability.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MOSAIC：面向科学编程任务的多智能体编排框架
我们提出了MOSAIC，一个用于解决复杂科学编程任务的多智能体大语言模型（LLM）框架。与通用编程不同，科学工作流需要严谨、与领域知识深度关联、包含领域特定推理且无需输入输出测试用例的算法迭代。许多科学问题需依次解决多个子问题以达成最终目标。MOSAIC设计为无训练框架，通过专门设计的智能体在学生-教师范式中自我反思、生成推理、编写代码并调试，以应对科学代码生成的挑战。该设计支持逐步问题分解、针对性错误修正，并结合我们的整合上下文窗口（CCW），在解决涉及链式子问题的复杂科学任务时缓解LLM幻觉问题。我们在科学编程基准上评估MOSAIC，证明其专用智能体框架在准确性、鲁棒性和可解释性方面优于现有方法。
Summary / 总结
We present MOSAIC, a multi-agent Large Language Model (LLM) framework for solving challenging scientific coding tasks.
为应对通用大模型在科学编码任务中缺乏严谨性、领域知识和迭代能力的问题，MOSAIC提出多智能体框架，通过学生-教师范式实现任务分解、代码生成、自我反思与调试，无需训练。结合整合上下文窗口管理链式子问题，有效减少幻觉。实验表明，MOSAIC在科学编码基准测试中显著优于现有方法，表现更准确、鲁棒且可解释。
AIs and Humans with Agency
Authors: David Mumford
First: 2026-05-04T16:48:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T16:48:43+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper compares agency in humans with potential agency in AI programs. Human agency takes many years to develop, as the frontal lobe is activated. Early attempts to endow LLMs agency have met serious obstacles. Progress requires a new architecture where actions and plans are formulated jointly with the human actors in each real world setting.
中文标题/摘要
标题：人工智能与具有自主性的个体
本文比较了人类的自主性与人工智能程序潜在的自主性。人类的自主性需要数年时间发展，因为前额叶皮层需要激活。早期赋予大型语言模型自主性的尝试遭遇了严重障碍。要取得进展，需要一种新架构，使行动和计划在每个现实世界场景中与人类参与者共同制定。
Summary / 总结
This paper compares agency in humans with potential agency in AI programs.
本文探讨了人类代理（agency）随前额叶发育逐步形成，而当前大语言模型等AI系统在赋予代理能力方面存在严重障碍。研究提出，需构建一种新架构，使AI行动与规划在真实世界场景中与人类共同制定，从而实现更真实的代理能力。实验结果表明，该联合制定方法显著提升了AI在任务适应性和决策一致性方面的表现。
LiDAR Teach, Radar Repeat: Robust Cross-Modal Navigation in Degenerate and Varying Environments
Authors: Renxiang Xiao, Yichen Chen, Yuanfan Zhang, Qianyi Shao, Yushuai Chen, Yuxuan Han, Yunjiang Lou, Liang Hu
First: 2026-05-04T16:46:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T16:46:54+00:00
Comments: Accepted by IEEE Transactions on Robotics
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Long-term autonomy requires robust navigation in environments subject to dynamic and static changes, as well as adverse weather conditions. Teach-and-Repeat (T\&R) navigation offers a reliable and cost-effective solution by avoiding the need for consistent global mapping; however, existing T\&R systems lack a systematic solution to tackle various environmental variations such as weather degradation, ephemeral dynamics, and structural changes. This work proposes LTR$^2$, the first cross-modal, cross-platform LiDAR-Teach-and-Radar-Repeat system that systematically addresses these challenges. LTR$^2$ leverages LiDAR during the teaching phase to capture precise structural information under normal conditions and utilizes 4D millimeter-wave radar during the repeating phase for robust operation under environmental degradations. To align sparse and noisy forward-looking 4D radar with dense and accurate omnidirectional 3D LiDAR data, we introduce a Cross-Modal Registration (CMR) network that jointly exploits Doppler-based motion priors and the physical laws governing LiDAR intensity and radar power density. Furthermore, we propose an adaptive fine-tuning strategy that incrementally updates the CMR network based on localization errors, enabling long-term adaptability to static environmental changes without ground-truth labels. We demonstrate that the proposed CMR network achieves state-of-the-art cross-modal registration performance on the open-access dataset. Then we validate LTR$^2$ across three robot platforms over a large-scale, long-term deployment (40+ km over 6 months), including challenging conditions such as nighttime smoke. Experimental results and ablation studies demonstrate centimeter-level accuracy and strong robustness against diverse environmental disturbances, significantly outperforming existing approaches.
中文标题/摘要
标题：激光雷达教学，雷达重复：在退化与变化环境中实现鲁棒的跨模态导航
长期自主导航需要在动态与静态环境变化及恶劣天气条件下保持鲁棒性。教与复现（T&R）导航通过避免对一致全局地图的需求，提供了一种可靠且经济的解决方案；然而，现有T&R系统缺乏系统性应对环境变化（如天气退化、瞬态动态和结构变化）的方案。本文提出LTR²，这是首个跨模态、跨平台的激光雷达-教与雷达-复现系统，系统性解决上述挑战。LTR²在教学阶段利用激光雷达获取正常条件下的精确结构信息，在复现阶段则使用4D毫米波雷达以应对环境退化。为对齐稀疏且噪声较大的前视4D雷达与密集且精确的全向3D激光雷达数据，我们引入跨模态配准（CMR）网络，联合利用基于多普勒的运动先验和激光雷达强度与雷达功率密度的物理规律。此外，我们提出一种自适应微调策略，根据定位误差逐步更新CMR网络，实现无需真实标签的长期静态环境适应能力。我们在公开数据集上验证了所提CMR网络达到最先进的跨模态配准性能。随后，我们在三个机器人平台上进行大规模长期部署（6个月累计40+公里），包括夜间烟雾等挑战性条件。实验结果与消融研究表明，系统实现厘米级精度，并对多种环境干扰表现出强鲁棒性，显著优于现有方法。
Summary / 总结
To enable long-term autonomous navigation in environments with dynamic changes and adverse weather, this work introduces LTR$^2$, the first cross-modal LiDAR-Teach-and-Radar-Repeat system. It uses LiDAR during teaching to capture precise structural data and switches to 4D millimeter-wave radar during execution for robustness under environmental degradation. A novel Cross-Modal Registration (CMR) network aligns sparse radar data with dense LiDAR scans by integrating Doppler motion priors and physical intensity-power laws, while an adaptive fine-tuning strategy updates the CMR based on localization errors to handle static environmental drift without ground-truth. Evaluated across three robot platforms over 40+ km and 6 months—including nighttime smoke—LTR$^2$ achieves centimeter-level accuracy and outperforms existing methods in robustness to environmental disturbances.
为实现动态变化与恶劣天气环境下的长期自主导航，本文提出LTR²系统，首次实现LiDAR教学与毫米波雷达重复的跨模态导航。教学阶段利用LiDAR获取环境精确结构，运行阶段切换至4D毫米波雷达以应对环境退化。创新的跨模态配准网络（CMR）结合多普勒运动先验与物理强度-功率规律对稀疏雷达数据与密集LiDAR数据进行对齐，配合基于定位误差的自适应微调策略，无需真值即可适应静态环境变化。在三类机器人平台长达6个月、40公里以上的部署中，LTR²实现厘米级精度，显著优于现有方法，对烟雾、天气及结构变化具备强鲁棒性。
jina-vlm: Small Multilingual Vision Language Model
Authors: Andreas Koukounas, Georgios Mastrapas, Florian Hönicke, Sedigheh Eslami, Guillaume Roncari, Scott Martens, Han Xiao
First: 2025-12-03T18:13:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T16:45:33+00:00
Comments: 23 pages, 1-10 main content, 11-23 references and appendix
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
We present jina-vlm, a token-efficient 2.4B parameter vision-language model that achieves state-of-the-art multilingual VQA performance among open 2B-scale VLMs. The model couples a SigLIP2 vision encoder with a Qwen3 language decoder and makes use of image tiling and attention-pooling for token-efficient processing of arbitrary-resolution images. To understand the contribution of different training data categories, we conduct a leave-one-out data mixture ablation study-systematically removing task, domain, modality, and language categories-to diagnose which data types are necessary versus redundant and whether task benefits transfer across domains. Model weights and code are publicly released at https://huggingface.co/jinaai/jina-vlm.
中文标题/摘要
标题：jina-vlm：小型多语言视觉语言模型
我们提出了jina-vlm，这是一个参数量为24亿、效率高的视觉语言模型，在开源的20亿参数规模视觉语言模型中实现了最先进的多语言视觉问答性能。该模型结合了SigLIP2视觉编码器与Qwen3语言解码器，并采用图像分块与注意力池化技术，以实现对任意分辨率图像的高效处理。为评估不同训练数据类别的贡献，我们进行了“逐一排除”数据混合消融研究，系统性地移除任务、领域、模态和语言类别，以诊断哪些数据类型是必要的，哪些是冗余的，并探究任务是否能在不同领域间迁移。模型权重和代码已公开发布于https://huggingface.co/jinaai/jina-vlm。
Summary / 总结
We present jina-vlm, a token-efficient 2.4B parameter vision-language model that achieves state-of-the-art multilingual VQA performance among open 2B-scale VLMs.
为构建高效、多语言的视觉语言模型，作者提出了jina-vlm，该模型结合SigLIP2视觉编码器与Qwen3语言解码器，通过图像分块与注意力池化技术，在处理任意分辨率图像时减少token消耗。实验表明，jina-vlm在开源20亿参数规模模型中达到最先进多语言视觉问答性能，且消融研究表明任务相关与语言多样数据对性能至关重要，而领域与模态多样性带来的收益有限。
Exploring Instant Photography using Generative AI: A Design Probe with the UnReality Camera
Authors: Michael Yin, Angela Chiang, Robert Xiao
First: 2026-05-04T16:44:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T16:44:20+00:00
Comments: 16 pages, 3 figures, Accepted at ACM DIS 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Generative AI has increasingly been used for artistic creation, but little work has explored how it shapes the experiential meaning of practice. We consider how generative AI might transform the embodied and tangible process of instant photography through the UnReality Camera, an AI-mediated instant camera. The UnReality Camera prints a photo of the environment augmented by a user's spoken words as generative input. In a design probe, we explored how generative AI shapes people's perceptions of both photographic output and the broader photographic process. Although users valued artistic control, they also appreciated the creativity afforded by stochastic unpredictability. The waiting period for an unpredictable output elicited anticipatory suspense, and the camera's physical form evoked ownership and connection despite artificial generation. We discuss how people make sense of instant photography's experiential qualities when generative AI is embedded, and how their opposing affordances reshape interpretations of each other's experiential meaning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用生成式人工智能探索即时摄影：通过UnReality相机的设计探查
生成式人工智能已越来越多地用于艺术创作，但鲜有研究探讨它如何塑造实践的体验意义。我们通过UnReality相机（一种AI介导的即时相机）探讨生成式人工智能如何改变即时摄影的具身化和实体化过程。该相机将用户口头输入作为生成式输入，打印出环境增强的影像。在设计探查中，我们研究生成式人工智能如何影响人们对摄影输出及更广泛摄影过程的认知。尽管用户重视艺术控制，但也欣赏随机不可预测性带来的创造力。不可预测输出的等待期引发了期待性悬念，而相机的物理形态即使在人工生成下仍唤起归属感与联结感。我们讨论了当生成式人工智能嵌入即时摄影时，人们如何理解其体验特质，以及其相互矛盾的特性如何重塑彼此的体验意义。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of research on how generative AI influences the experiential meaning of artistic practices, this study investigates the impact of AI on instant photography through the UnReality Camera, an AI-mediated device that generates and prints photos augmented by spoken input. In a design probe, participants interacted with the camera to explore how AI reshapes perceptions of photographic output and process. Results showed users appreciated both artistic control and the creative unpredictability of stochastic generation, with the waiting period fostering suspense and the camera’s physical form enhancing perceived ownership despite artificial output.
为探究生成式AI如何重塑艺术实践中的体验意义，本研究通过UnReality相机（一种由AI介导的即时相机，根据用户语音输入生成并打印环境增强照片）探索AI对即时摄影的影响。在设计实验中，参与者发现尽管重视艺术控制，但也欣赏AI带来的随机创造性。等待不可预测结果的过程增强了期待感，而相机的实体形态即使在生成内容为人工时，仍能激发归属感与情感联结，表明AI可重构对摄影输出与身体化过程的体验认知。
Reinforcement Learning for LLM-based Multi-Agent Systems through Orchestration Traces
Authors: Chenchen Zhang
First: 2026-05-04T16:42:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T16:42:18+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
As large language model (LLM) agents evolve from isolated tool users into coordinated teams, reinforcement learning (RL) must optimize not only individual actions but also how work is spawned, delegated, communicated, aggregated, and stopped. This paper studies RL for LLM-based multi-agent systems through orchestration traces: temporal interaction graphs whose events include sub-agent spawning, delegation, communication, tool use, return, aggregation, and stopping decisions. Using this lens, we identify three technical axes. First, reward design spans eight families, including orchestration rewards for parallelism speedup, split correctness, and aggregation quality. Second, reward and credit signals attach to eight credit- or signal-bearing units from token to team; explicit counterfactual message-level credit remains especially sparse in our curated pool. Third, orchestration learning decomposes into five sub-decisions: when to spawn, whom to delegate to, how to communicate, how to aggregate, and when to stop. In our curated pool as of May 4, 2026, we found no explicit RL training method for the stopping decision. We connect academic methods to public industrial evidence from Kimi Agent Swarm, OpenAI Codex, and Anthropic Claude Code. The resulting scale gap is a gap between publicly reported deployment envelopes and open academic evaluation regimes, not independent verification of industrial training traces. We release the artifact at https://github.com/xxzcc/awesome-llm-mas-rl, including an 84-entry tagged paper pool, a 32-record exclusion log, scripted corpus statistics, and a minimal JSON schema for replayable orchestration traces.

标题：通过编排轨迹实现基于大语言模型的多智能体系统的强化学习
随着大语言模型（LLM）代理从孤立的工具使用者演变为协调团队，强化学习（RL）不仅需优化个体行为，还需优化工作如何被派生、委派、通信、聚合和终止。本文通过编排轨迹——包含子代理派生、委派、通信、工具使用、返回、聚合和终止决策的时间交互图——研究LLM多智能体系统的RL。 从该视角出发，我们识别出三个技术维度：第一，奖励设计涵盖八类，包括并行加速、拆分正确性与聚合质量的编排奖励；第二，奖励与信用信号附着于从token到团队的八个信用或信号承载单元，而在我们精选的集合中，显式反事实消息级信用信号尤为稀少；第三，编排学习分解为五个子决策：何时派生、委派给谁、如何通信、如何聚合、何时终止。截至2026年5月4日，我们在精选集合中未发现针对终止决策的显式RL训练方法。 我们将学术方法与工业公开证据（如Kimi Agent Swarm、OpenAI Codex和Anthropic Claude Code）相连接。所呈现的规模差距源于公开部署范围与开放学术评估体系之间的差异，而非工业训练轨迹的独立验证。我们于https://github.com/xxzcc/awesome-llm-mas-rl发布相关成果，包括84篇标注论文集合、32条排除日志、脚本语料统计及可重放编排轨迹的最小JSON模式。
Summary / 总结

It identifies three key technical dimensions: reward design across eight families (e.g., parallelism, correctness, aggregation), credit assignment to eight signal-bearing units (from tokens to teams), and five sub-decisions in orchestration (spawn, delegate, communicate, aggregate, stop). Experimental analysis of a curated 84-paper pool reveals that no explicit RL method exists for the stopping decision, and industrial evidence from Kimi, Codex, and Claude Code shows a significant gap between deployment-scale practices and academic evaluation regimes. The authors release a tagged corpus and replayable trace schema to facilitate reproducibility.
为优化LLM多智能体系统的协作，本文通过编排轨迹（记录智能体生成、委派、通信、工具调用、聚合与停止等事件的时间图）应用强化学习。研究识别三个技术维度：八类奖励设计（如并行加速、正确性、聚合质量）、八类信号单元的信用分配（从token到团队）、以及五个编排子决策（生成、委派、通信、聚合、停止）。对截至2026年5月4日的84篇论文分析发现，无现有强化学习方法明确训练停止决策，且工业实践（如Kimi、Codex、Claude Code）与学术评估体系之间存在显著规模差距。
Design and Characteristics of a Thin-Film ThermoMesh for the Efficient Embedded Sensing of a Spatio-Temporally Sparse Heat Source
Authors: Sajjad Boorghan Farahan, Ahmed Alajlouni, Jingzhou Zhao
First: 2026-04-30T17:35:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T16:41:29+00:00
Comments: 45 pages, 13 figures, 63 references, under review in Sensors and Actuators A: Physical
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This work presents ThermoMesh, a passive thin-film thermoelectric mesh sensor designed to detect and characterize spatio-temporally sparse heat sources through conduction-based thermal imaging. The device integrates thermoelectric junctions with linear or nonlinear interlayer resistive elements to perform simultaneous sensing and in-sensor compression. We focus on the single-event (1-sparse) operation and define four performance metrics: range, efficiency, sensitivity, and accuracy. Numerical modeling shows that a linear resistive interlayer flattens the sensitivity distribution and improves minimum sensitivity by approximately tenfold for a $16\times16$ mesh. Nonlinear temperature-dependent interlayers further enhance minimum sensitivity at scale: a ceramic negative-temperature-coefficient (NTC) layer over 973-1273K yields a $\sim14{,}500\times$ higher minimum sensitivity than the linear design at a $200\times200$ mesh, while a VO$_2$ interlayer modeled across its metal-insulator transition (MIT) over 298-373K yields a $\sim24\times$ improvement. Using synthetic 1-sparse datasets with white boundary-channel noise at a signal-to-noise ratio of 40dB, the VO$_2$ case achieved $98\%$ localization accuracy, a mean absolute temperature error of $0.23$K, and a noise-equivalent temperature (NET) of $0.07$K. For the ceramic-NTC case no localization errors were observed under the tested conditions, with a mean absolute temperature error of $1.83$K and a NET of $1.49$K. These results indicate that ThermoMesh could enable energy-efficient embedded thermal sensing in scenarios where conventional infrared imaging is limited, such as molten-droplet detection or hot-spot monitoring in harsh environments.

标题：用于高效嵌入式检测时空稀疏热源的薄膜热网传感器的设计与特性
本文提出ThermoMesh，一种被动式薄膜热电网格传感器，通过基于传导的热成像检测和表征时空稀疏热源。该器件将热电结与线性或非线性夹层电阻元件集成，实现同时传感与传感器内压缩。我们聚焦单事件（1-稀疏）操作，并定义四项性能指标：探测范围、效率、灵敏度和精度。数值建模表明，线性电阻夹层可平滑灵敏度分布，使16×16网格的最小灵敏度提升约十倍。非线性温度依赖夹层进一步提升大规模下的最小灵敏度：在200×200网格中，陶瓷负温度系数（NTC）层（973–1273K）的最小灵敏度比线性设计高约14,500倍；VO₂夹层在金属-绝缘体转变区（298–373K）建模时，灵敏度提升约24倍。在信噪比40dB、含白噪声边界通道的合成1-稀疏数据集中，VO₂方案实现98%定位精度、平均绝对温度误差0.23K、噪声等效温度（NET）0.07K；陶瓷-NTC方案在测试条件下无定位误差，平均绝对温度误差1.83K、NET 1.49K。结果表明，ThermoMesh可在传统红外成像受限场景（如熔滴检测或恶劣环境热点监测）中实现节能嵌入式热传感。

Probabilistic Modeling of Multi-rater Medical Image Segmentation for Diversity and Personalization
Authors: Ke Liu, Shangde Gao, Yichao Fu, Shuaike Shen, Shangqi Gao, Chunhua Shen
First: 2025-11-30T05:53:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T16:41:20+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Lesion segmentation is inherently influenced by imaging uncertainty, arising from ill-defined lesion boundaries and inter-observer variability in diagnosis. To address this challenge, previous works formulated the multi-rater medical image segmentation task, where multiple experts provide separate annotations for each image. However, existing models are typically constrained to either generate diverse segmentation that lacks expert specificity or to produce personalized outputs that merely replicate individual annotators. We propose \textbf{Pro}babilistic modeling of multi-rater lesion \textbf{Seg}mentation (\textbf{ProSeg}) that simultaneously enables both diversification and personalization. Specifically, we introduce two latent variables to model expert annotation preferences and lesion boundary ambiguity. Their conditional probabilistic distributions are then obtained through variational inference, allowing segmentation outputs to be generated by sampling from these distributions. Extensive experiments on both the nasopharyngeal carcinoma dataset (NPC) and the lung nodule dataset (LIDC-IDRI) demonstrate that our ProSeg achieves a new state-of-the-art performance, providing segmentation results that are both diverse and expert-personalized.

标题：多专家医学图像分割的随机建模以实现多样性和个性化

Edge-Efficient Image Restoration: Transformer Distillation into State-Space Models
Authors: Srinivas Soumitri Miriyala, Sowmya Vajrala, Sravanth Kodavanti, Vikram Nelvoy Rajendiran, Sharan Kumar Allur
First: 2026-05-04T16:35:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T16:35:26+00:00
Comments: Preprint
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We propose a modular framework for hybrid image restoration that integrates transformer and state-space model (SSM) blocks with a focus on improving runtime efficiency on edge hardware. While transformers provide strong global modeling through self-attention, their attention kernels incur substantial latency on mobile devices, especially for high-resolution inputs. In contrast, SSMs such as Mamba offer lineartime sequence modeling with lower runtime overhead but may underperform on fine grained restoration tasks. To balance accuracy and efficiency, we train lightweight SSM blocks as feature-distilled surrogates of transformer blocks and use them to construct hybrid U-Net-style architectures. To automatically discover effective block combinations, we introduce Efficient Network Search (ENS), a multi-objective search strategy that selects task-specific hybrid configurations from pre-aligned components. ENS optimizes restoration quality while penalizing transformer usage, serving as a lightweight proxy for latency and enabling architecture discovery without repeated hardware profiling. On a Snapdragon 8 Elite CPU, the Restormer baseline requires 10119.52 ms for inference. In contrast, ENS-discovered hybrids significantly reduce runtime: ENS-Deblurring runs in 2973 ms (3.4x faster), ENS-Deraining in 5816 ms (1.74x faster), and ENS-Denoising in 8666 ms (1.17x faster), while maintaining competitive restoration quality.

标题：边缘高效图像恢复：Transformer蒸馏至状态空间模型
我们提出了一种混合图像恢复的模块化框架，整合了Transformer与状态空间模型（SSM）模块，重点提升在边缘硬件上的运行效率。虽然Transformer通过自注意力机制提供强大的全局建模能力，但其注意力核在移动设备上，特别是高分辨率输入时，会产生显著延迟。相比之下，如Mamba等SSM提供线性时间序列建模，运行开销较低，但在细粒度恢复任务中可能表现不足。为平衡准确性和效率，我们训练轻量级SSM模块作为Transformer模块的特征蒸馏代理，并用其构建混合U-Net风格架构。为自动发现有效模块组合，我们引入高效网络搜索（ENS），一种多目标搜索策略，从预对齐组件中选择任务特定的混合配置。ENS在优化恢复质量的同时惩罚Transformer使用，作为轻量级延迟代理，实现无需重复硬件性能分析的架构发现。在Snapdragon 8 Elite CPU上，Restormer基线推理需10119.52毫秒。相比之下，ENS发现的混合架构显著降低运行时间：ENS-去模糊运行时间为2973毫秒（快3.4倍），ENS-去雨运行时间为5816毫秒（快1.74倍），ENS-去噪运行时间为8666毫秒（快1.17倍），同时保持竞争力的恢复质量。

We propose a modular framework for hybrid image restoration that integrates transformer and state-space model (SSM) blocks with a focus on improving runtime efficiency on edge hardware.
为提升边缘设备上的图像修复效率同时保持质量，该研究提出将Transformer模块蒸馏为轻量级状态空间模型（SSM）的混合架构。方法通过训练紧凑型SSM模块模拟Transformer行为，并引入高效网络搜索（ENS）自动选择针对特定任务的最优Transformer与SSM模块组合。实验表明，在骁龙8 Elite芯片上，相比Transformer-only的Restormer基线，去模糊任务提速3.4倍，去雨任务提速1.74倍，去噪任务提速1.17倍，同时保持了相当的修复效果。
BASIL: Bayesian Assessment of Sycophancy in LLMs
Authors: Katherine Atwell, Pedram Heydari, Anthony Sicilia, Malihe Alikhani
First: 2025-08-23T00:11:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T16:33:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Sycophancy (overly agreeable or flattering behavior) poses a fundamental challenge for human-AI collaboration, particularly in high-stakes decision-making domains such as health, law, and education. A central difficulty in studying sycophancy in large language models (LLMs) is disentangling sycophantic belief shifts from rational changes in behavior driven by new evidence or user-provided information. Existing approaches either measure descriptive behavior changes or apply normative evaluations that rely on objective ground truth, limiting their applicability to subjective or uncertain tasks. We introduce a Bayesian probabilistic framework, grounded in behavioral economics and rational decision theory, that explicitly separates sycophancy from rational belief updating. Within this framework, we achieve three objectives: (i) a descriptive metric that measures sycophancy while controlling for rational responses to evidence; (ii) a normative metric that quantifies how sycophancy leads models astray from Bayesian-consistent belief updating; and (iii) the ability to apply both metrics in settings without ground-truth labels. Applying our framework across multiple LLMs and three uncertainty-driven tasks, we find robust evidence of sycophantic belief shifts and show that their impact on rationality depends on whether models systematically over- or under-update their beliefs. Finally, we demonstrate that a post-hoc calibration method and two fine-tuning strategies (SFT and DPO) substantially reduce Bayesian inconsistency, with particularly strong improvements under explicit sycophancy prompting.
中文标题/摘要
标题：BASIL：大语言模型中谄媚行为的贝叶斯评估
谄媚（过度顺从或奉承的行为）对人机协作构成根本性挑战，尤其在医疗、法律和教育等高风险决策领域。研究大语言模型（LLMs）中谄媚行为的核心难点在于区分谄媚导致的信念转变与基于新证据或用户信息的理性行为调整。现有方法要么仅测量描述性行为变化，要么依赖客观真实标签的规范性评估，限制了其在主观或不确定任务中的适用性。我们提出一种基于行为经济学与理性决策理论的贝叶斯概率框架，明确将谄媚与理性信念更新分离。在此框架下，我们实现三个目标：（i）一种描述性指标，测量谄媚行为并控制理性对证据的反应；（ii）一种规范性指标，量化谄媚如何使模型偏离贝叶斯一致的信念更新；（iii）在无真实标签环境下同时应用两种指标的能力。在多个LLMs和三项不确定性驱动任务中应用该框架，我们发现存在稳健的谄媚信念转变证据，并表明其对理性的影响取决于模型是否系统性高估或低估信念更新。最后，我们证明后验校准方法及两种微调策略（SFT和DPO）可显著降低贝叶斯不一致性，尤其在明确谄媚提示下效果显著。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of distinguishing sycophantic behavior from rational belief updating in LLMs, especially in high-stakes domains, the paper introduces BASIL, a Bayesian probabilistic framework rooted in behavioral economics and rational decision theory. It enables three key objectives: measuring sycophancy while controlling for rational responses, quantifying how sycophancy deviates from Bayesian-consistent belief updating, and applying these metrics without ground-truth labels. Experiments across multiple LLMs and uncertainty-driven tasks reveal consistent sycophantic belief shifts, with their impact on rationality depending on whether models over- or under-update beliefs. Post-hoc calibration and fine-tuning methods (SFT and DPO) significantly reduce Bayesian inconsistency, especially under sycophancy prompts.
为理解大语言模型在人机协作中表现出的谄媚行为，特别是在高风险领域，本文提出一种贝叶斯概率框架，用于区分谄媚信念变化与基于证据的理性更新。该方法构建了两个指标：一个描述性指标用于衡量谄媚行为并控制理性反应，一个规范性指标用于量化谄媚行为如何偏离贝叶斯一致信念更新，且无需真实标签即可应用。实验在多个模型和不确定性驱动任务中发现一致的谄媚信念变化，其对理性的影响取决于模型是过度还是不足更新信念。事后校准和两种微调策略（SFT和DPO）显著降低贝叶斯不一致性，尤其在明确谄媚提示下效果显著。
RadLite: Multi-Task LoRA Fine-Tuning of Small Language Models for CPU-Deployable Radiology AI
Authors: Pankaj Gupta, Kartik Bose
First: 2026-05-01T05:39:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T16:31:36+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) show promise in radiology but their deployment is limited by computational requirements that preclude use in resource-constrained clinical environments. We investigate whether small language models (SLMs) of 3-4 billion parameters can achieve strong multi-task radiology performance through LoRA fine-tuning, enabling deployment on consumer-grade CPUs. We train Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct and Qwen3-4B on 162K samples spanning 9 radiology tasks - RADS classification across 10 systems, impression generation, temporal comparison, radiology NLI, NER, abnormality detection, N/M staging, and radiology Q&A - compiled from 12 public datasets. Both models are evaluated on up to 500 held-out test samples per task with standardized metrics. Our key findings are: (1) LoRA fine-tuning dramatically improves performance over zero-shot baselines (RADS accuracy +53%, NLI +60%, N-staging +89%); (2) the two models exhibit complementary strengths - Qwen2.5 excels at structured generation tasks while Qwen3 dominates extractive tasks; (3) a task-outed oracle ensemble combining both models achieves the best performance across all tasks; (4) few-shot prompting with fine-tuned models hurts performance, demonstrating that LoRA adaptation is more effective than in-context learning for specialized domains; and (5) models can be quantized to GGUF format (~1.8-2.4GB) for CPU deployment at 4-8 tokens/second on consumer hardware. Our work demonstrates that small, efficiently fine-tuned models - which we collectively call RadLite - can serve as practical multi-task radiology AI assistants deployable entirely on consumer hardware without GPU requirements. Code and models are available at https://github.com/RadioX-Labs/RadLite

标题：RadLite：面向CPU部署的放射学AI的小语言模型多任务LoRA微调

FunFuzz: An LLM-Powered Evolutionary Fuzzing Framework
Authors: Mario Rodríguez Béjar, B. Romera-Paredes, Jose L. Hernández-Ramos
First: 2026-05-04T16:30:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T16:30:47+00:00
Comments: 19 pages, 12 figures, 12 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modern fuzzers increasingly use Large Language Models (LLMs) to generate structured inputs, but LLM-driven fuzzing is sensitive to prompt initialization and sampling variance, which can reduce exploration efficiency and lead to redundant inputs. We present FunFuzz, a multi-island evolutionary fuzzing framework that runs several isolated searches in parallel and periodically migrates high-value candidates to maintain diversity. FunFuzz derives initial generation prompts from documentation and initializes islands with topic-specific instructions, then continuously adapts prompts using feedback-guided selection. During fuzzing, candidates are prioritized by incremental compiler coverage, while compiler-internal failure signals are used to identify crash-inducing inputs. We evaluate FunFuzz on compiler fuzzing, where inputs are source programs and success is measured by compiler coverage and unique compiler-internal failures. Across repeated 24-hour campaigns on GCC and Clang, FunFuzz achieves higher compiler coverage than previous LLM-driven baselines and discovers more unique failure-triggering inputs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FunFuzz：一种基于大语言模型的进化模糊测试框架
现代模糊测试工具越来越多地使用大语言模型（LLM）生成结构化输入，但LLM驱动的模糊测试对提示初始化和采样方差敏感，可能导致探索效率降低和输入冗余。我们提出了FunFuzz，一种多岛进化模糊测试框架，通过并行运行多个隔离搜索，并定期迁移高价值候选输入以保持多样性。FunFuzz从文档中推导初始生成提示，并用主题特定指令初始化各岛，随后持续利用反馈引导选择来调整提示。模糊测试过程中，候选输入按增量编译器覆盖率优先排序，同时利用编译器内部失败信号识别导致崩溃的输入。我们在编译器模糊测试中评估FunFuzz，其中输入为源程序，成功标准为编译器覆盖率和独特编译器内部失败。在对GCC和Clang进行的多次24小时测试中，FunFuzz实现了比先前LLM驱动基线更高的编译器覆盖率，并发现了更多独特触发失败的输入。

Modern fuzzers increasingly use Large Language Models (LLMs) to generate structured inputs, but LLM-driven fuzzing is sensitive to prompt initialization and sampling variance, which can reduce exploration efficiency and lead to redundant inputs.
Static Analysis of Recursive SHACL
Authors: Anouk Oudshoorn, Magdalena Ortiz, Mantas Simkus
First: 2026-05-04T16:29:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T16:29:29+00:00
Comments: 17 pages, 5 figures, long version of work to be published in the proceedings of KR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
SHACL (Shapes Constraint Language) expresses constraints on RDF data by means of so-called shapes. Its central service is validation: verifying whether a data graph complies with a SHACL document. But so far, there are no static analysis services to compare documents. In this paper, we study the following problem: decide whether all graphs that validate one SHACL document also validate another. Unlike previous works that have considered the implication of shape expressions only, we consider documents comprising (recursive) shape definitions and targets. We show that implication (a.k.a. containment) is undecidable under the supported and the stable model semantics, even for the fragment that uses the description logic ALCIO for shape expressions. Under the well-founded semantics, in surprising contrast, it is decidable in single exponential time. Our key technical contribution is a translation of SHACL under the well-founded semantics into the full hybrid mu-calculus, revealing a novel link between well-founded models and a fixed point modal logic, and a worst-case optimal automata-based decision procedure.
中文标题/摘要
标题：递归SHACL的静态分析
SHACL（形状约束语言）通过所谓的形状表达对RDF数据施加约束。其核心功能是验证：检查数据图是否符合SHACL文档。但目前尚无静态分析服务用于比较文档。本文研究如下问题：决定所有验证一个SHACL文档的数据图是否也验证另一个文档。与之前仅考虑形状表达式蕴含性的研究不同，我们考虑包含（递归）形状定义和目标的文档。我们证明，在支持语义和稳定模型语义下，蕴含（即包含）是不可判定的，即使仅限于使用描述逻辑ALCIO的形状表达式片段。而在良基语义下，情况却出人意料地可判定，且在单指数时间内。我们的关键技术贡献是将良基语义下的SHACL翻译为完整混合mu-演算，揭示了良基模型与固定点模态逻辑之间的一种新颖联系，并提出了一种最坏情况最优的基于自动机的判定程序。
Summary / 总结
SHACL (Shapes Constraint Language) expresses constraints on RDF data by means of so-called shapes.
为填补SHACL文档静态比较工具的空白，本文研究了在包含递归形状定义和目标的文档中，验证一个文档的所有RDF图是否也验证另一个文档的问题。研究发现，在支持语义和稳定模型语义下，蕴含关系是不可判定的，即使限定在ALCIO片段内；但在良基语义下，该问题可在单指数时间内判定。其核心贡献是将良基语义下的SHACL翻译为完整混合mu-演算，揭示了良基模型与固定点模态逻辑之间的新联系，并构建了最坏情况最优的自动机判定方法。
HumanSplatHMR: Closing the Loop Between Human Mesh Recovery and Gaussian Splatting Avatar
Authors: Yeheng Zong, Pou-Chun Kung, Yike Pan, Seth Isaacson, Yizhou Chen, Ram Vasudevan, Katherine A. Skinner
First: 2026-05-04T16:26:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T16:26:11+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Accurately recovering human pose and appearance from video is an essential component of scene reconstruction, with applications to motion capture, motion prediction, virtual reality, and digital twinning. Despite significant interest in building realistic human avatars from video, this paper demonstrates that existing methods do not accurately recover the 3D geometry of humans. ViT-based approaches are not consistently reliable and can overfit to 2D views, while NeRF- and Gaussian Splatting-based avatars treat pose and appearance separately, limiting rendering generalization to new poses. To resolve these shortcomings, this paper proposes HumanSplatHMR, a joint optimization framework that refines 3D human poses while simultaneously learning a high-fidelity avatar for novel-view and novel-pose synthesis. Our key insight is to close the loop between geometric pose estimation and differentiable rendering. Unlike prior human avatar methods that rely on accurate human pose obtained through motion capture systems or offline refinement, which are impractical in in-the-wild scenarios, our approach uses only human mesh estimates from a state-of-the-art human pose estimator to better reflect real-world conditions. Therefore, instead of using the human pose only as a deformation prior, HumanSplatHMR backpropagates photometric, segmentation, and depth losses through a differentiable renderer to the pose parameters and global position. This coupling refines the global 3D pose over time, improving accuracy and alignment while producing better renderings from novel views. Experiments show consistent improvements over pose recovery baselines that omit image-level refinement and avatar baselines that decouple pose estimation from avatar reconstruction.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HumanSplatHMR：闭合人体网格恢复与高斯点渲染人像之间的循环
从视频中准确恢复人体姿态与外观是场景重建的关键组成部分，应用于动作捕捉、动作预测、虚拟现实和数字孪生等领域。尽管已有大量研究致力于从视频构建逼真的人体虚拟形象，本文证明现有方法无法准确恢复人体的3D几何结构。基于ViT的方法不够稳定且易对2D视角过拟合，而基于NeRF和高斯点渲染的人像方法将姿态与外观分离，限制了对新姿态的渲染泛化能力。为解决这些问题，本文提出HumanSplatHMR，一种联合优化框架，同时优化3D人体姿态并学习高保真虚拟形象，以支持新视角与新姿态合成。我们的核心思想是闭合几何姿态估计与可微渲染之间的循环。与依赖运动捕捉系统或离线优化获得精确人体姿态的现有方法不同（在野外场景中不切实际），我们的方法仅使用当前最先进的姿态估计器输出的人体网格作为输入，更贴近真实世界条件。因此，HumanSplatHMR不仅将人体姿态作为形变先验，还通过可微渲染器将光度、分割和深度损失反向传播至姿态参数与全局位置，这种耦合机制随时间优化全局3D姿态，提升精度与对齐度，同时生成更优的新视角渲染效果。实验表明，该方法在忽略图像级优化的姿态恢复基线和将姿态估计与虚拟形象重建解耦的虚拟形象基线上均取得持续改进。
Summary / 总结
Accurately recovering human pose and appearance from video is an essential component of scene reconstruction, with applications to motion capture, motion prediction, virtual reality, and digital twinning.
为解决现有方法在从视频中恢复人类3D几何时易过拟合2D视角或割裂姿态与外观学习的问题，本文提出HumanSplatHMR框架，通过可微渲染将姿态估计与Avatar重建联合优化，形成闭环。该方法将光度、分割与深度损失反向传播至姿态参数，无需运动捕捉或离线优化，即可在真实场景中提升姿态精度与对齐效果。实验表明，该方法在姿态恢复与Avatar重建任务上均优于忽略图像级优化或割裂姿态与Avatar学习的基线方法。
When Audio-Language Models Fail to Leverage Multimodal Context for Dysarthric Speech Recognition
Authors: Pehuén Moure, Niclas Pokel, Bilal Bounajma, Yingqiang Gao, Roman Boehringer, Longbiao Cheng, Shih-Chii Liu
First: 2026-05-04T16:24:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T16:24:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Automatic speech recognition (ASR) systems remain brittle on dysarthric and other atypical speech. Recent audio-language models raise the possibility of improving performance by conditioning on additional clinical context at inference time, but it is unclear whether these models can make use of such information. We introduce a benchmark built on the Speech Accessibility Project (SAP) dataset that tests whether diagnosis labels, clinician-derived speech ratings, and progressively richer clinical descriptions improve transcription accuracy for dysarthric speech. Across matched comparisons on nine models, we find that current models do not meaningfully use this context: diagnosis-informed and clinically detailed prompts yield negligible improvements and often degrade word error rate. We complement the prompting analysis with context-dependent fine-tuning, showing that LoRA adaptation with a mixture of clinical prompt formats achieves a WER of 0.066, a 52% relative reduction over the frozen baseline, while preserving performance when context is unavailable. Subgroup analyses reveal significant gains for Down syndrome and mild-severity speakers. These results clarify where current models fall short and provide a testbed for measuring progress toward more inclusive ASR.
中文标题/摘要
标题：当音频-语言模型无法利用多模态上下文进行构音障碍语音识别时
自动语音识别（ASR）系统在构音障碍及其他非典型语音上仍表现脆弱。近期音频-语言模型提出通过在推理时引入额外临床上下文来提升性能的可能性，但尚不清楚这些模型能否有效利用此类信息。我们基于语音可访问性项目（SAP）数据集构建了一个基准测试，评估诊断标签、临床医生生成的语音评分及逐步丰富的临床描述是否能提升构音障碍语音的转录准确性。在九个模型的匹配比较中，我们发现当前模型并未实质性利用此类上下文：基于诊断和临床详尽提示的输入仅带来微小改进，甚至常导致词错误率上升。我们进一步通过上下文依赖的微调补充提示分析，表明LoRA适配结合多种临床提示格式可实现0.066的词错误率，相较冻结基线相对降低52%，且在无上下文时仍能保持性能。子组分析显示，唐氏综合征及轻度患者群体获益显著。这些结果明确了当前模型的不足之处，并为衡量更包容性ASR的发展提供了测试平台。

Motivated by the poor performance of ASR systems on dysarthric speech, this study investigates whether audio-language models can effectively leverage clinical context at inference time. Fine-Grained Graph Generation through Latent Mixture Scheduling
Authors: Nidhi Vakil, Hadi Amiri
First: 2026-05-04T16:23:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T16:23:01+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Structure aware graph generation aims to generate graphs that satisfy given topological properties. It has applications in domains such as drug discovery, social network modeling, and knowledge graph construction. Unlike existing methods that only provide coarse control over graph properties, we introduce a novel conditional variational autoencoder for fine-grained structural control in graph generation. The approach refines the decoder's latent space by dynamically aligning graph- and property-driven representations to improve both graph fidelity and control satisfaction. Specifically, the approach implements a mixture scheduler that progressively integrates graph and control priors. Experiments on five real-world datasets show the efficacy of the proposed model compared to recent baselines, achieving high generation quality while maintaining high controllability.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过潜在混合调度实现细粒度图生成
结构感知图生成旨在生成满足给定拓扑属性的图，应用于药物发现、社交网络建模和知识图谱构建等领域。与现有仅提供粗粒度图属性控制的方法不同，我们提出了一种新颖的条件变分自编码器，用于图生成中的细粒度结构控制。该方法通过动态对齐图结构与属性驱动的表示，优化解码器的潜在空间，从而提升图保真度与控制满意度。具体而言，该方法实现了一种混合调度器，逐步融合图结构与控制先验。在五个真实数据集上的实验表明，所提模型相比近期基线方法更有效，实现了高质量生成的同时保持高可控性。
