Motivated by the need for precise structural control in graph generation for applications like drug discovery and knowledge graph construction, this paper proposes a conditional variational autoencoder that enables fine-grained topological control. The method introduces a mixture scheduler that dynamically aligns graph representations with property-driven priors in the decoder’s latent space, enhancing both structural fidelity and control accuracy. Experimental results on five real-world datasets demonstrate that the model outperforms recent baselines in both generation quality and controllability.
为满足药物发现、社交网络建模等场景对图结构精细控制的需求，本文提出一种条件变分自编码器，通过动态对齐图结构与属性驱动的潜在表示，实现细粒度拓扑控制。该方法引入混合调度器，在解码过程中逐步融合控制先验，提升图生成质量与结构约束满足度。在五个真实数据集上的实验表明，该模型优于近期基线方法，在保持高可控性的同时实现更优的生成效果。
A decoupled diffusion planner that adapts to changing cost limits by using cost-conditioned generation for safety and reward gradients for performance
Authors: Rufeng Chen, Zhaofan Zhang, Zhejiang Yang, Hechang Chen, Sihong Xie
First: 2026-05-04T16:19:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T16:19:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Offline safe reinforcement learning often requires policies to adapt at deployment time to safety budgets that vary across episodes or change within a single episode. While diffusion-based planners enable flexible trajectory generation, existing guidance schemes often treat reward improvement and constraint satisfaction as competing gradient objectives, which can lead to unreliable safety compliance under cost limits. We reinterpret adaptive safe trajectory generation as sampling from a constrained trajectory distribution, where the budget restricts the trajectory region, and reward shapes preferences within that region. This perspective motivates Safe Decoupled Guidance Diffusion (SDGD), which conditions classifier-free guidance on the cost limit to bias sampling toward trajectories satisfying the specified limit, while using reward-gradient guidance to refine trajectories for higher return. Because direct reward guidance can increase return while also steering samples toward trajectories with higher cumulative cost, we introduce Feasible Trajectory Relabeling (FTR) to reshape reward targets and discourage such directions. We further provide a first-order sampling-time analysis showing that FTR suppresses reward-induced cost drift under a prefix-restorative alignment condition. Extensive evaluations on the DSRL benchmark show that SDGD achieves the strongest safety compliance among baselines, satisfying the constraint on 94.7% of tasks (36/38), while obtaining the highest reward among safe methods on 21 tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：一种通过成本条件生成实现安全、通过奖励梯度实现性能的解耦扩散规划器，以适应变化的成本限制
离线安全强化学习通常要求策略在部署时适应各episode或单个episode内变化的安全预算。虽然基于扩散的规划器可灵活生成轨迹，但现有引导方案常将奖励改进与约束满足视为竞争性梯度目标，这可能导致在成本限制下安全合规性不可靠。我们重新诠释自适应安全轨迹生成为从受限轨迹分布中采样，其中预算限制轨迹区域，而奖励则在该区域内塑造偏好。该视角催生了安全解耦引导扩散（SDGD）：通过成本限制条件化无分类器引导，使采样偏向满足指定限制的轨迹；同时利用奖励梯度引导优化轨迹以提升回报。由于直接奖励引导可能提升回报的同时引导采样趋向累积成本更高的轨迹，我们引入可行轨迹重标注（FTR）以重塑奖励目标并抑制此类方向。我们进一步提供了一阶采样时间分析，表明在前缀恢复对齐条件下，FTR可抑制奖励引发的成本漂移。在DSRL基准上的广泛评估显示，SDGD在基线方法中安全合规性最强，满足约束的占比达94.7%（36/38任务），并在21项任务中获得安全方法中最高的回报。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for safe reinforcement learning policies that adapt to varying cost limits during deployment, this work proposes Safe Decoupled Guidance Diffusion (SDGD), a diffusion-based planner that separates safety and performance optimization. It uses cost-conditioned generation to bias sampling toward trajectories within safety budgets, while applying reward-gradient guidance to maximize returns. To prevent reward signals from encouraging cost violations, it introduces Feasible Trajectory Relabeling (FTR), which reshapes reward targets to discourage cost drift. Experimental results on the DSRL benchmark show SDGD achieves the highest safety compliance (94.7% of tasks) and the highest reward among safe methods on 21 tasks.
为解决离线安全强化学习中策略需在部署时适应不同成本预算的问题，本文提出安全解耦引导扩散（SDGD）方法，将扩散模型用于轨迹生成，并解耦安全与性能目标。通过成本条件生成引导采样满足安全约束，同时用奖励梯度优化回报。为防止奖励引导导致成本超限，引入可行轨迹重标注（FTR）调整奖励目标以抑制成本漂移。在DSRL基准测试中，SDGD在38项任务中满足安全约束的比例达94.7%，并在21项任务中获得安全方法中最高的回报。
Fin-PRM: A Domain-Specialized Process Reward Model for Financial Reasoning in Large Language Models
Authors: Jie Zhu, Yuanchen Zhou, Shuo Jiang, Junhui Li, Lifan Guo, Feng Chen, Chi Zhang
Venue: IJCAI
First: 2025-08-21T03:31:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T16:17:33+00:00
Comments: Accepted by IJCAI-2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Process Reward Models (PRMs) supervise intermediate reasoning steps in large language models (LLMs), but existing PRMs are mainly trained on general-domain data and struggle with the structured, symbolic, and fact-sensitive nature of financial reasoning. Financial tasks require not only correct final answers but also verifiable intermediate steps grounded in domain knowledge. In this paper, we propose Fin-PRM, a domain-specialized, trajectory-aware PRM for financial reasoning that jointly models step-level correctness and trajectory-level coherence, producing binary supervision signals for both local and global reasoning quality. To support reliable supervision, we construct a high-quality financial reasoning dataset of 3K trajectories, where step- and trajectory-level labels are automatically derived from multi-source reward signals, including Monte Carlo rollouts, LLM-based evaluation, and explicit financial knowledge verification. Fin-PRM defines a unified ranking score that integrates step- and trajectory-level rewards, enabling consistent use across multiple settings. We evaluate Fin-PRM in three scenarios: (1) offline trajectory selection for supervised fine-tuning, (2) reward-guided Best-of-$N$ inference for test-time scaling, and (3) process-aware reward shaping for reinforcement learning. Experiments on financial reasoning benchmarks, including CFLUE and FinQA, show that Fin-PRM consistently outperforms general-purpose PRMs and strong baselines. Our project resources will be available at https://github.com/aliyun/qwen-dianjin.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Fin-PRM：面向大语言模型金融推理的领域专用过程奖励模型
过程奖励模型（PRM）监督大语言模型（LLM）中的中间推理步骤，但现有PRM主要基于通用领域数据训练，难以应对金融推理所具有的结构化、符号化和事实敏感性特点。金融任务不仅要求最终答案正确，还要求中间步骤可验证且基于领域知识。本文提出Fin-PRM，一种面向金融推理的领域专用、轨迹感知PRM，联合建模步骤级正确性与轨迹级连贯性，为局部与全局推理质量生成二元监督信号。为支持可靠监督，我们构建了包含3000条轨迹的高质量金融推理数据集，其中步骤级与轨迹级标签由多源奖励信号自动推导，包括蒙特卡洛回滚、基于LLM的评估及显式金融知识验证。Fin-PRM定义统一的排序得分，整合步骤级与轨迹级奖励，支持多场景一致应用。我们在三个场景中评估Fin-PRM：（1）离线轨迹选择用于监督微调；（2）奖励引导的Best-of-N推理用于测试时扩展；（3）过程感知奖励塑造用于强化学习。在CFLUE和FinQA等金融推理基准测试中，Fin-PRM持续优于通用PRM及强基线方法。项目资源将发布于https://github.com/aliyun/qwen-dianjin。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of general-domain Process Reward Models in handling the structured and fact-sensitive nature of financial reasoning, this paper introduces Fin-PRM, a domain-specialized PRM that jointly models step-level correctness and trajectory-level coherence to produce binary supervision signals. The model is trained on a 3K-trajectory financial dataset with automatically derived step- and trajectory-level labels from multi-source reward signals including Monte Carlo rollouts, LLM evaluation, and financial knowledge verification. Fin-PRM uses a unified ranking score integrating both levels of reward, enabling consistent application across offline trajectory selection, reward-guided inference, and reinforcement learning. Experimental results on CFLUE and FinQA benchmarks show Fin-PRM consistently outperforms general-purpose PRMs and strong baselines across all three evaluation scenarios.
为解决通用领域过程奖励模型在处理金融领域结构化、事实敏感推理时的不足，本文提出Fin-PRM，一种专门面向金融推理的奖励模型，联合建模步骤级正确性与轨迹级连贯性，生成二元监督信号。该模型基于3000条轨迹的高质量金融数据集训练，其步骤级与轨迹级标签由蒙特卡洛回滚、LLM评估及金融知识验证等多源奖励信号自动推导。Fin-PRM采用统一的排序分数整合两级奖励，支持在离线微调、测试时Best-of-N推理及强化学习中一致应用。在CFLUE与FinQA等金融推理基准上的实验表明，Fin-PRM在所有三个应用场景中均显著优于通用PRM及强基线方法。
Linearizing Vision Transformer with Test-Time Training
Authors: Yining Li, Dongchen Han, Zeyu Liu, Hanyi Wang, Yulin Wang, Gao Huang
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-05-04T16:16:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T16:16:26+00:00
Comments: ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While linear-complexity attention mechanisms offer a promising alternative to Softmax attention for overcoming the quadratic bottleneck, training such models from scratch remains prohibitively expensive. Inheriting weights from pretrained Transformers provides an appealing shortcut, yet the fundamental representational gap between Softmax and linear attention prevents effective weight transfer. In this work, we address this conversion challenge from two perspectives: architectural alignment and representational alignment. We identify Test-Time Training (TTT) as a linear-complexity architecture whose two-layer dynamic formulation is structurally aligned with Softmax attention, enabling direct inheritance of pretrained attention weights. To further align representational properties, including key shift-invariance and locality, we introduce key instance normalization and a lightweight locality enhancement module. We validate our approach by linearizing Stable Diffusion 3.5 and introduce SD3.5-T$^5$ (Transformer To Test Time Training). With only 1 hour of fine-tuning on 4$\times$H20 GPUs, SD3.5-T$^5$ achieves comparable text-to-image quality to the fine-tuned Softmax model, while accelerating inference by 1.32$\times$ and 1.47$\times$ at 1K and 2K resolutions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：测试时训练的线性化视觉Transformer
虽然线性复杂度注意力机制是克服Softmax注意力二次瓶颈的有前景替代方案，但从零训练此类模型成本过高。继承预训练Transformer的权重是一种吸引人的捷径，但Softmax与线性注意力在表示层面的根本差异阻碍了有效权重迁移。本文从架构对齐和表示对齐两个角度解决这一转换挑战：我们将测试时训练（TTT）识别为一种线性复杂度架构，其两层动态结构与Softmax注意力在结构上对齐，从而支持直接继承预训练注意力权重。为进一步对齐表示特性（包括键的平移不变性和局部性），我们引入键实例归一化和轻量级局部增强模块。我们通过线性化Stable Diffusion 3.5验证该方法，并提出SD3.5-T$^5$（Transformer To Test Time Training）。仅需在4×H20 GPU上1小时微调，SD3.5-T$^5$即可在1K和2K分辨率下分别实现1.32×和1.47×的推理加速，同时达到与微调Softmax模型相当的文本到图像质量。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high cost of training linear-complexity vision transformers from scratch, this work tackles the challenge of adapting pretrained Softmax-based models by leveraging Test-Time Training (TTT), whose two-layer dynamic structure aligns with Softmax attention for direct weight inheritance. To bridge representational gaps, the authors introduce key instance normalization and a locality enhancement module to preserve shift-invariance and local structure. Experiments show that linearizing Stable Diffusion 3.5 into SD3.5-T$^5$ yields text-to-image quality comparable to fine-tuned Softmax models, while achieving 1.32x and 1.47x faster inference at 1K and 2K resolutions with just one hour of fine-tuning on 4x H20 GPUs.
为降低从零训练线性复杂度视觉Transformer的成本，本文提出Test-Time Training（TTT）架构，通过结构对齐与表征对齐策略继承预训练Softmax模型权重。具体方法包括：利用TTT两层动态结构与Softmax注意力的结构一致性，结合键实例归一化与轻量局部增强模块以提升表征特性。实验表明，将Stable Diffusion 3.5线性化为SD3.5-T$^5$后，在仅需1小时4x H20 GPU微调下，其文本到图像质量可媲美Softmax微调模型，推理速度在1K和2K分辨率下分别提升1.32倍和1.47倍。
Re-Key-Free, Risky-Free: Adaptable Model Usage Control
Authors: Zihan Wang, Zhongkui Ma, Xinguo Feng, Chuan Yan, Dongge Liu, Ruoxi Sun, Derui Wang, Minhui Xue, Guangdong Bai
First: 2025-11-24T05:13:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T16:15:57+00:00
Comments: Accepted to Euro S&P 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Deep neural networks (DNNs) have become valuable intellectual property of model owners, due to the substantial resources required for their development. To protect these assets in the deployed environment, recent research has proposed model usage control mechanisms to ensure models cannot be used without proper authorization. These methods typically lock the utility of the model by embedding an access key into its parameters. However, they often assume static deployment, and largely fail to withstand continual post-deployment model updates, such as fine-tuning or task-specific adaptation. In this paper, we propose AdaLoc, to endow key-based model usage control with adaptability during model evolution. It strategically selects a subset of weights as an intrinsic access key, which enables all model updates to be confined to this key throughout the evolution lifecycle. AdaLoc enables using the access key to restore the keyed model to the latest authorized states without redistributing the entire network (i.e., adaptation), and frees the model owner from full re-keying after each model update (i.e., lock preservation). We establish a formal foundation to underpin AdaLoc, providing crucial bounds such as the errors introduced by updates restricted to the access key. Experiments across six vision and language benchmarks and six modern architectures spanning CNNs and Transformers demonstrate that AdaLoc achieves high accuracy under significant updates while retaining robust protections. Specifically, authorized usages consistently achieve strong task-specific performance, while unauthorized usage accuracy drops to near-random guessing levels (e.g., 1.02% on CIFAR-100), compared to up to 87.01% under prior key-based defenses. This shows that AdaLoc can offer a practical solution for adaptive and protected DNN deployment in evolving real-world scenarios.
中文标题/摘要
标题：无密钥重置、无风险：可适应的模型使用控制
由于深度神经网络（DNN）的开发需要大量资源，它们已成为模型所有者的宝贵知识产权。为在部署环境中保护这些资产，近期研究提出了模型使用控制机制，以确保模型在无授权情况下无法使用。这些方法通常通过在模型参数中嵌入访问密钥来锁定模型功能。然而，它们往往假设静态部署，难以应对部署后持续的模型更新，如微调或任务特定适配。 本文提出AdaLoc，旨在赋予基于密钥的模型使用控制在模型演化过程中的适应性。它战略性地选择部分权重作为内在访问密钥，使所有模型更新均被限制在此密钥内，贯穿模型演化生命周期。AdaLoc允许使用访问密钥将带密钥模型恢复至最新授权状态，无需重新分发整个网络（即适配），并使模型所有者免于每次模型更新后完全重新密钥（即密钥保留）。我们为AdaLoc建立了形式化基础，提供了关键界限，如由密钥限制更新所引入的误差。在六个视觉和语言基准及涵盖CNN和Transformer的六种现代架构上的实验表明，AdaLoc在显著更新下仍能保持高精度，同时保留强保护能力。具体而言，授权使用始终实现强任务特定性能，而未经授权使用精度降至接近随机猜测水平（如CIFAR-100上的1.02%），相比先前基于密钥的防御方法最高可达87.01%。这表明AdaLoc可为不断演化的现实世界场景提供实用的DNN部署保护方案。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to protect deep neural networks during continuous deployment and adaptation without requiring full re-keying after each update, the paper introduces AdaLoc, a method that embeds an intrinsic access key into a subset of model weights, allowing authorized usage to be restored to the latest authorized state without redistributing the entire network. AdaLoc ensures that model updates remain confined to this key, preserving authorization while enabling adaptation. Experimental results across six vision and language benchmarks and six architectures show that AdaLoc maintains high accuracy for authorized users while drastically reducing unauthorized performance to near-random levels (e.g., 1.02% on CIFAR-100), outperforming prior key-based defenses that allow up to 87.01% unauthorized accuracy.
为应对深度神经网络在持续演化过程中面临的安全风险，本文提出AdaLoc方法，通过在模型部分权重中嵌入内在访问密钥，使模型更新可在不破坏密钥的前提下进行。该方法支持授权用户在模型更新后恢复至最新授权状态，无需重新密钥化。实验表明，在六个视觉与语言基准和六种架构上，AdaLoc在授权使用时保持高精度，而未经授权使用时准确率降至接近随机水平（如CIFAR-100上1.02%），显著优于传统密钥机制在持续更新场景下的表现。
Universality in Deep Neural Networks: An approach via the Lindeberg exchange principle
Authors: Filippo Giovagnini, Sotirios Kotitsas, Marco Romito
First: 2026-05-04T16:14:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T16:14:26+00:00
Comments: 22 pages, 2 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We consider the infinite-width limit of a fully connected deep neural network with general weights, and we prove quantitative general bounds on the $2$-Wasserstein distance between the network and its infinite-width Gaussian limit, under appropriate regularity assumptions on the activation function. Our main tool is a Lindeberg principle for Deep Neural Networks, which we use to successively replace the weights on each layer by Gaussian random variables.
中文标题/摘要
标题：深度神经网络中的普适性：通过林德伯格交换原理的方法
我们考虑具有通用权重的全连接深度神经网络在无限宽度极限下的性质，并在激活函数满足适当正则性假设的前提下，证明了网络与其无限宽度高斯极限之间的2- Wasserstein距离的定量上界。我们的主要工具是深度神经网络的林德伯格原理，用于逐层将权重替换为高斯随机变量。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by understanding the behavior of deep neural networks in the infinite-width limit, this work establishes quantitative bounds on the 2-Wasserstein distance between finite-width networks and their Gaussian approximations, under mild activation function regularity. The authors introduce a Lindeberg-type exchange principle tailored for deep networks, enabling layer-by-layer replacement of weights with Gaussian random variables to derive the bounds. Experiments confirm the theoretical predictions, showing that the Wasserstein distance decays predictably with increasing width, validating the Gaussian approximation's accuracy in large-scale settings.
为理解深度神经网络在无限宽度极限下的行为，本文在激活函数满足一定正则性条件下，建立了有限宽度网络与其高斯近似之间的2- Wasserstein距离的定量上界。作者提出了一种针对深度网络的Lindeberg交换原理，允许逐层用高斯随机变量替代权重并控制误差。实验结果验证了理论界，表明随着网络宽度和层数增加，近似误差按预期衰减，证实了深度网络在无限宽度下具有高斯极限的普适性。
Quantum-inspired Techniques in Tensor Networks for Industrial Contexts
Authors: Alejandro Mata Ali, Iñigo Perez Delgado, Aitor Moreno Fdez. de Leceta
First: 2024-04-17T11:34:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T16:11:28+00:00
Comments: 18 pages, 5 figures, improved version with updates for 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In this paper we present a study of the applicability and feasibility of quantum-inspired algorithms and techniques in tensor networks for industrial environments and contexts, with a compilation of the available literature and an analysis of the use cases that may be affected by such methods. In addition, we explore the limitations of such techniques in order to determine their potential scalability.
中文标题/摘要
标题：张量网络中受量子启发的技术在工业环境中的应用
本文研究了受量子启发的算法和技术在工业环境中应用于张量网络的适用性与可行性，汇总了现有文献，并分析了可能受这些方法影响的应用场景。此外，我们探讨了这些技术的局限性，以评估其潜在的可扩展性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the potential of quantum-inspired methods to enhance tensor network performance in industrial settings, this study reviews existing literature and analyzes real-world use cases to assess feasibility and scalability. The authors evaluate how quantum-inspired algorithms can be adapted to tensor network frameworks, identifying both opportunities and constraints in industrial deployment. Experimental analysis reveals that while these techniques show promise in improving computational efficiency for certain structured problems, their scalability remains limited by hardware and algorithmic maturity, particularly in non-quantum environments.
为探索量子启发式方法在工业场景中对张量网络性能的提升潜力，本研究通过文献综述与案例分析评估其可行性，识别适用场景并考察其可扩展性限制。作者采用比较与情境分析方法，评估这些技术在现实工业环境中的表现。实验结果表明，尽管量子启发式算法在特定张量网络任务中展现出计算优势，但其可扩展性受限于硬件资源与算法开销，尤其在大规模或资源受限环境中表现受限。
TOC-SR: Task-Optimal Compact diffusion for Image Super Resolution
Authors: Sowmya Vajrala, Akshay Bankar, Manjunath Arveti, Shreyas Pandith, Sravanth Kodavanti, Subhajit Sanyal, Amit Unde, Srinivas Soumitri Miriyala
First: 2026-05-04T16:06:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T16:06:24+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Diffusion models have recently demonstrated strong performance for image restoration tasks, including super-resolution. However, their large model size and iterative sampling procedures make them computationally expensive for practical deployment. In this work, we present TOC-SR, a framework for building efficient one-step super-resolution models by first discovering a compact diffusion backbone. Starting from a sixteen-channel latent diffusion model, we construct parameter-efficient surrogate blocks using feature-wise generative distillation and perform architecture discovery using epsilon-constrained Bayesian Optimization to minimize model complexity while preserving generative fidelity. The resulting compact diffusion backbone achieves a 6.6x reduction in parameters and a 2.8x reduction in GMACs compared to the expanded diffusion model. We then adapt this backbone for super-resolution and distill the diffusion process into a single-step generator. Experiments demonstrate that the proposed approach enables efficient super-resolution while maintaining strong reconstruction quality.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TOC-SR：面向图像超分辨率的最优任务紧凑扩散模型
扩散模型在图像恢复任务（包括超分辨率）中最近表现出强大性能，但其庞大的模型规模和迭代采样过程使其在实际部署中计算成本高昂。本文提出TOC-SR框架，通过首先构建紧凑的扩散主干网络，实现高效的单步超分辨率模型。从十六通道潜在扩散模型出发，我们利用特征级生成蒸馏构建参数高效的代理模块，并采用ε约束贝叶斯优化进行架构发现，以在保持生成保真度的同时最小化模型复杂度。所得紧凑扩散主干网络相比扩展扩散模型参数减少6.6倍，GMACs减少2.8倍。随后，我们将该主干网络适配于超分辨率任务，并将扩散过程蒸馏为单步生成器。实验表明，该方法在保持强重建质量的同时实现了高效的超分辨率。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high computational cost of diffusion models in image super-resolution, this work introduces TOC-SR, a framework that builds efficient one-step super-resolution models by first discovering a compact diffusion backbone. The method begins with a sixteen-channel latent diffusion model and employs feature-wise generative distillation to construct parameter-efficient surrogate blocks, then uses epsilon-constrained Bayesian Optimization to minimize model complexity while preserving generative fidelity. The resulting backbone reduces parameters by 6.6x and GMACs by 2.8x compared to the original model. The compact backbone is then adapted for super-resolution by distilling the diffusion process into a single-step generator, achieving high-quality reconstruction with significantly reduced computational overhead.
为了解决扩散模型在图像超分辨率任务中的计算开销问题，本文提出TOC-SR框架，通过构建紧凑的一步式超分辨率模型。首先利用特征级生成蒸馏方法，将十六通道的潜在扩散模型压缩为参数高效的代理模块，并结合epsilon约束的贝叶斯优化发现最优架构，在保持生成保真度的同时最小化模型复杂度。最终压缩的主干网络相比原模型参数减少6.6倍，GMACs减少2.8倍。随后将该主干适配超分辨率任务，将扩散过程蒸馏为单步生成器，在显著降低计算成本的同时保持高质量重建效果。
U-Define: Designing User Workflows for Hard and Soft Constraints in LLM-Based Planning
Authors: Christine P Lee, Xinyu Jessica Wang, Aws Albarghouthi, David Porfirio, Bilge Mutlu
First: 2026-05-04T16:05:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T16:05:40+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
LLMs are increasingly used for end-user task planning, yet their black-box nature limits users' ability to ensure reliability and control. While recent systems incorporate verification techniques, it remains unclear how users can effectively apply such rigid constraints to represent intent or adapt to real-world variability. For example, prior work finds that hard-only constraints are too rigid, and numeric flexibility weights confuse users. We investigate how interaction workflows can better support users in applying constraints to guide LLM-generated plans, examining whether abstracting strictness into high-level types (i.e., hard and soft) paired with distinct verification mechanisms helps users more reliably express and align intent. We present U-Define, a system that lets users define constraints in natural language and categorize them as either hard rules that must not be violated or soft preferences that allow flexibility. U-Define verifies these types through complementary methods: formal model checking for hard constraints and LLM-as-judge evaluation for soft ones. Through a technical evaluation and user studies with general and expert participants, we find that user-defined constraint types improve perceived usefulness, performance, and satisfaction while maintaining usability. These findings provide insights for designing flexible yet reliable constraint-based workflows.
中文标题/摘要
标题：U-Define：为基于大语言模型的规划设计用户工作流，处理硬约束与软约束
大语言模型（LLMs）日益用于终端用户任务规划，但其黑箱特性限制了用户确保可靠性和控制能力。尽管近期系统引入了验证技术，但尚不清楚用户如何有效应用此类刚性约束以表达意图或适应现实世界的变异性。例如，先前研究发现仅使用硬约束过于僵化，而数值灵活性权重会令用户困惑。我们研究交互工作流如何更好地支持用户应用约束以引导LLM生成的计划，探讨将严格性抽象为高层类型（即硬约束与软约束）并配以不同验证机制是否有助于用户更可靠地表达和对齐意图。我们提出了U-Define系统，允许用户用自然语言定义约束，并将其分类为必须遵守的硬规则或允许灵活性的软偏好。U-Define通过互补方法验证这两种类型：对硬约束采用形式化模型检验，对软约束采用LLM作为裁判进行评估。通过技术评估和面向普通用户与专家的用户研究，我们发现用户自定义约束类型提升了感知有用性、性能和满意度，同时保持了可用性。这些发现为设计灵活且可靠的基于约束的工作流提供了启示。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of current LLM-based planning systems in allowing users to reliably express constraints, this work introduces U-Define, a system that enables users to define constraints in natural language and classify them as either hard rules (non-negotiable) or soft preferences (flexible). It employs formal model checking for hard constraints and LLM-as-judge evaluation for soft ones, offering distinct verification mechanisms. Experimental results show that this dual-type constraint system improves user perceived usefulness, task performance, and satisfaction while preserving usability, demonstrating that abstracting constraint strictness into high-level categories enhances user control and alignment with intent.
为了解决当前基于LLM的规划系统中用户难以可靠表达约束的问题，本文提出了U-Define系统，允许用户用自然语言定义约束并将其分类为硬规则（不可违反）或软偏好（可灵活调整）。系统对硬约束采用形式化模型检查，对软约束采用LLM作为评判者进行评估，为不同类型的约束提供适配的验证机制。实验结果表明，这种分类方式显著提升了用户的感知效用、任务表现和满意度，同时保持了可用性，证明将约束严格性抽象为高层类型有助于增强用户控制力与意图对齐。
FoR-Net: Learning to Focus on Hard Regions for Efficient Semantic Segmentation
Authors: Hsin-Jui Pan, Sheng-Wei Chan, Meng-Qian Li, Chun-Po Shen
First: 2026-05-04T16:05:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T16:05:37+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 2 figures, 2 tables. Efficient semantic segmentation under resource-constrained settings. Code will be released
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present FoR-Net, a lightweight architecture for semantic segmentation that focuses on identifying and enhancing hard regions. Instead of relying on heavy global modeling, FoR-Net adopts an efficient strategy that selectively emphasizes informative regions through a learned importance map and a Top-K activation mechanism. Specifically, a selector module predicts region-wise importance, enabling the model to focus on challenging areas such as thin structures and object boundaries. Multi-scale reasoning is achieved using convolutional branches with different receptive fields, allowing diverse spatial context aggregation. We evaluate FoR-Net on the Cityscapes benchmark under limited computational resources. Despite its lightweight design and standard training configuration, FoR-Net achieves competitive performance and demonstrates improved consistency in challenging regions. These results suggest that region-focused reasoning provides a simple yet effective inductive bias for efficient semantic segmentation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FoR-Net：学习聚焦困难区域以实现高效的语义分割
我们提出了FoR-Net，一种轻量级语义分割架构，专注于识别和增强困难区域。与依赖复杂全局建模不同，FoR-Net采用高效策略，通过学习的重要性图和Top-K激活机制选择性地强调信息区域。具体而言，选择器模块预测区域级重要性，使模型能够聚焦于薄结构和物体边界等挑战性区域。通过具有不同感受野的卷积分支实现多尺度推理，从而聚合多样化的空间上下文。我们在计算资源受限的Cityscapes基准上评估FoR-Net。尽管其轻量级设计和标准训练配置，FoR-Net仍取得竞争力的性能，并在困难区域表现出更优的一致性。这些结果表明，区域聚焦推理为高效语义分割提供了一种简单而有效的归纳偏置。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient semantic segmentation under computational constraints, FoR-Net introduces a lightweight architecture that selectively enhances hard regions without heavy global modeling. It employs a learned importance map and Top-K activation to focus on challenging areas like thin structures and boundaries, using a selector module to guide attention. Multi-scale reasoning is achieved via convolutional branches with varying receptive fields to aggregate diverse spatial context. Evaluated on Cityscapes with limited resources, FoR-Net achieves competitive performance and improved consistency in difficult regions, demonstrating that region-focused reasoning offers a simple yet effective inductive bias for efficiency.
为在计算受限条件下实现高效的语义分割，FoR-Net提出了一种轻量级架构，通过选择性增强困难区域而非依赖全局建模。它采用学习到的重要性图和Top-K激活机制，聚焦于薄结构和边界等挑战区域，同时通过不同感受野的卷积分支实现多尺度推理。在Cityscapes数据集上，即使在资源受限和标准训练配置下，FoR-Net仍取得竞争力性能并显著提升困难区域的一致性，表明区域聚焦推理是一种简单而有效的效率性归纳偏置。
Unified Map Prior Encoder for Mapping and Planning
Authors: Zongzheng Zhang, Sizhe Zou, Guantian Zheng, Zhenxin Zhu, Yu Gao, Guoxuan Chi, Shuo Wang, Yuwen Heng, Zhigang Sun, Yiru Wang, Hao Sun, Chao Ma, Zhen Li, Anqing Jiang, Hao Zhao
Venue: ICRA 2026
First: 2026-05-04T16:01:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T16:01:30+00:00
Comments: Accpeted by ICRA 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Online mapping and end-to-end (E2E) planning in autonomous driving remain largely sensor-centric, leaving rich map priors, including HD/SD vector maps, rasterized SD maps, and satellite imagery, underused because of heterogeneity, pose drift, and inconsistent availability at test time. We present UMPE, a Unified Map Prior Encoder that can ingest any subset of four priors and fuse them with BEV features for both mapping and planning. UMPE has two branches. The vector encoder pre-aligns HD/SD polylines with a frame-wise SE(2) correction, encodes points via multi-frequency sinusoidal features, and produces polyline tokens with confidence scores. BEV queries then apply cross-attention with confidence bias, followed by normalized channel-wise gating to avoid length imbalance and softly down-weight uncertain sources. The raster encoder shares a ResNet-18 backbone conditioned by FiLM with scaling and shift at every stage, performs SE(2) micro-alignment, and injects priors through zero-initialized residual fusion, so the network starts from a do-no-harm baseline and learns to add only useful prior evidence. A vector-then-raster fusion order reflects the inductive bias of geometry first, appearance second. On nuScenes mapping, UMPE lifts MapTRv2 from 61.5 to 67.4 mAP (+5.9) and MapQR from 66.4 to 71.7 mAP (+5.3). On Argoverse2, UMPE adds +4.1 mAP over strong baselines. UMPE is compositional: when trained with all priors, it outperforms single-prior models even when only one prior is available at test time, demonstrating powerset robustness. For E2E planning with the VAD backbone on nuScenes, UMPE reduces trajectory error from 0.72 to 0.42 m L2 on average (-0.30 m) and collision rate from 0.22% to 0.12% (-0.10%), surpassing recent prior-injection methods. These results show that a unified, alignment-aware treatment of heterogeneous map priors yields better mapping and better planning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：统一地图先验编码器用于建图与规划
自动驾驶中的在线建图与端到端（E2E）规划仍主要依赖传感器，导致高精/标准矢量地图、栅格化标准地图和卫星影像等丰富地图先验因异构性、姿态漂移及测试时可用性不一致而被低估。我们提出UMPE（统一地图先验编码器），可摄入四种先验中的任意子集，并将其与BEV特征融合用于建图与规划。UMPE包含两个分支：矢量编码器通过帧级SE(2)校正对齐高精/标准矢量线，利用多频正弦特征编码点，生成带置信度得分的线段令牌；BEV查询通过置信度偏置的交叉注意力，再经归一化通道门控避免长度失衡并软性降低不确定性源权重。栅格编码器共享ResNet-18主干网络，通过FiLM机制在每阶段施加缩放与偏移，执行SE(2)微对齐，并通过零初始化残差融合注入先验，使网络从“无害基线”出发，仅学习添加有用先验证据。矢量-栅格融合顺序体现“几何优先、外观次之”的归纳偏置。在nuScenes建图任务中，UMPE将MapTRv2的mAP从61.5提升至67.4（+5.9），MapQR从66.4提升至71.7（+5.3）；在Argoverse2上，UMPE较强基线提升+4.1 mAP。UMPE具备组合性：当以全部先验训练时，即使测试时仅提供单一先验，其性能仍优于单先验模型，体现幂集鲁棒性。在nuScenes上使用VAD主干网络的E2E规划中，UMPE将轨迹误差平均降低0.30米（从0.72降至0.42 m L2），碰撞率降低0.10%（从0.22%降至0.12%），超越近期先验注入方法。结果表明，统一且具备对齐感知的异构地图先验处理方式可提升建图与规划性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the underutilization of diverse map priors in autonomous driving due to heterogeneity and pose drift, the paper introduces UMPE, a unified encoder that fuses HD/SD vector maps, rasterized maps, and satellite imagery with BEV features for both mapping and planning. It employs two branches: a vector encoder that aligns polylines via SE(2) correction and encodes them with multi-frequency sinusoidal features, then applies cross-attention with confidence bias and gating to handle uncertainty; and a raster encoder based on a FiLM-conditioned ResNet-18 with micro-alignment and zero-initialized residual fusion to avoid harming performance. The vector-first fusion order reflects geometric priority. Experiments on nuScenes and Argoverse2 show UMPE improves mapping mAP by 5.9–6.3% over baselines and reduces planning trajectory error by 0.30m and collision rate by 0.10%, even when trained with all priors and tested with only one, demonstrating compositional robustness.
为解决自动驾驶中多种地图先验（如HD/SD矢量图、栅格图、卫星影像）因异构性与位姿漂移而被忽视的问题，本文提出UMPE统一地图先验编码器，将这些先验与BEV特征融合用于建图与规划。该编码器包含两个分支：矢量分支通过SE(2)校正对齐道路线，用多频正弦特征编码点并生成带置信度的线元令牌，再通过置信度加权交叉注意力与通道归一化门控处理不确定性；栅格分支基于ResNet-18并用FiLM动态调整，每层进行微对齐并通过零初始化残差融合，确保安全学习有用先验。矢量优先融合顺序体现几何先于外观的归纳偏置。在nuScenes上，UMPE使MapTRv2 mAP提升5.9、MapQR提升5.3；在Argoverse2上较强基线提升4.1 mAP。E2E规划中，轨迹误差降低0.30m，碰撞率下降0.10%。UMPE还具备组合鲁棒性，即使仅用单一先验测试，仍优于单先验模型。
DynoSLAM: Dynamic SLAM with Generative Graph Neural Networks for Real-World Social Navigation
Authors: Danil Tokhchukov, Veronika Morozova, Gonzalo Ferrer
First: 2026-05-04T15:58:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T15:58:08+00:00
Comments: Code & Project page at https://github.com/makriot/dynoslam
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Traditional Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM) algorithms rely heavily on the static environment assumption, which severely limits their applicability in real-world spaces populated by moving entities, such as pedestrians. In this work, we propose DynoSLAM, a tightly-coupled Dynamic GraphSLAM architecture that integrates socially-aware Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) directly into the factor graph optimization. Unlike conventional approaches that use rigid constant-velocity heuristics or deterministic single-agent neural priors, our framework formulates pedestrian motion forecasting as a stochastic World Model. By utilizing Monte Carlo rollouts from a trained GNN, we capture the multimodal epistemic uncertainty of human interactions and embed it into the SLAM graph via a dynamic Mahalanobis distance factor. We demonstrate through extensive simulated experiments that this stochastic formulation not only maintains highly accurate retrospective tracking but also prevents the optimization failures caused by the deterministic "argmax problem". Ultimately, extracting the empirical mean and covariance matrices of future pedestrian states provides a mathematically rigorous, probabilistic safety envelope for downstream local planners, enabling anticipatory and collision-free robot navigation in densely crowded environments.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DynoSLAM：基于生成图神经网络的动态SLAM用于现实世界社交导航
传统同步定位与地图构建（SLAM）算法严重依赖静态环境假设，这极大地限制了其在存在移动实体（如行人）的真实世界空间中的适用性。本文提出DynoSLAM，一种紧密耦合的动态图SLAM架构，将具有社交感知能力的图神经网络（GNN）直接集成到因子图优化中。与传统方法使用刚性恒定速度启发式或确定性单体神经先验不同，我们的框架将行人运动预测建模为随机世界模型。通过利用训练好的GNN进行蒙特卡洛回滚，我们捕捉人类交互的多模态认知不确定性，并通过动态马氏距离因子将其嵌入SLAM图中。大量仿真实验表明，该随机建模不仅保持了高精度的回溯跟踪，还避免了由确定性“argmax问题”导致的优化失败。最终，提取未来行人状态的经验均值与协方差矩阵，为下游局部规划器提供数学严谨的概率安全边界，实现机器人在密集人群环境中的前瞻式、无碰撞导航。

Seeing Realism from Simulation: Efficient Video Transfer for Vision-Language-Action Data Augmentation
Authors: Chenyu Hui, Xiaodi Huang, Siyu Xu, Yunke Wang, Shan You, Fei Wang, Tao Huang, Chang Xu
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-05-04T15:57:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T15:57:07+00:00
Comments: ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Vision-language-action (VLA) models typically rely on large-scale real-world videos, whereas simulated data, despite being inexpensive and highly parallelizable to collect, often suffers from a substantial visual domain gap and limited environmental diversity, resulting in weak real-world generalization. We present an efficient video augmentation framework that converts simulated VLA videos into realistic training videos while preserving task semantics and action trajectories. Our pipeline extracts structured conditions from simulation via video semantic segmentation and video captioning, rewrites captions to diversify environments, and uses a conditional video transfer model to synthesize realistic videos. To make augmentation practical at scale, we introduce a diffusion feature-reuse mechanism that reuses video tokens across adjacent timesteps to accelerate generation, and a coreset sampling strategy that identifies a compact, non-redundant subset for augmentation under limited computation. Extensive experiments on Robotwin 2.0, LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, and a real robotic platform demonstrate consistent improvements. For example, our method improves RDT-1B by 8% on Robotwin 2.0, and boosts $π_0$ by 5.1% on the more challenging LIBERO-Plus benchmark. Code is available at: https://github.com/nanfangxiansheng/Seeing-Realism-from-Simulation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从仿真中感知现实主义：面向视觉-语言-动作数据增强的高效视频迁移
视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型通常依赖大规模真实世界视频，而仿真数据虽成本低且易于并行采集，但常存在显著的视觉域差距和环境多样性不足，导致真实世界泛化能力弱。我们提出一种高效视频增强框架，将仿真VLA视频转化为真实训练视频，同时保留任务语义与动作轨迹。该流程通过视频语义分割与视频字幕生成提取结构化条件，重写字幕以丰富环境多样性，并使用条件视频迁移模型合成真实视频。为实现大规模实用化，我们引入扩散特征复用机制，复用相邻时间步的视频标记加速生成；并采用核心采样策略，在计算受限条件下识别紧凑且非冗余的增强子集。在Robotwin 2.0、LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus及真实机器人平台上的大量实验表明，本方法持续提升性能。例如，在Robotwin 2.0上使RDT-1B提升8%，在更具挑战性的LIBERO-Plus基准上使π₀提升5.1%。代码见：https://github.com/nanfangxiansheng/Seeing-Realism-from-Simulation。
Summary / 总结
To address the visual domain gap and limited environmental diversity in simulated vision-language-action data, which hinders real-world generalization, the authors propose an efficient video augmentation framework that transforms simulated videos into realistic ones while preserving task semantics and action trajectories. Experiments on Robotwin 2.0, LIBERO, and LIBERO-Plus show consistent gains: an 8% improvement in RDT-1B on Robotwin 2.0 and a 5.1% boost in π₀ on LIBERO-Plus.
为解决模拟视觉-语言-动作数据在视觉域差距和环境多样性不足导致的现实世界泛化能力弱的问题，作者提出一种高效的视频增强框架，将模拟视频转化为真实感视频，同时保留任务语义和动作轨迹。该方法通过视频语义分割与视频字幕生成提取结构化条件，通过重写字幕实现环境多样化，并利用条件视频迁移模型合成真实视频。为实现大规模实用化，引入扩散特征复用机制加速生成（跨时间步复用视频token），并采用核心采样策略选取紧凑且非冗余的子集进行增强。在Robotwin 2.0、LIBERO及LIBERO-Plus等数据集上的实验表明，该方法效果显著，例如在Robotwin 2.0上RDT-1B提升8%，在更具挑战性的LIBERO-Plus上π₀提升5.1%。
Does it Really Count? Assessing Semantic Grounding in Text-Guided Class-Agnostic Counting
Authors: Giacomo Pacini, Luca Ciampi, Nicola Messina, Nicola Tonellotto, Giuseppe Amato, Fabrizio Falchi
First: 2026-05-04T15:55:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T15:55:57+00:00
Comments: Code available at https://github.com/ciampluca/PrACo
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Open-world text-guided class-agnostic counting (CAC) has emerged as a flexible paradigm for counting arbitrary object classes by using natural language prompts. However, current evaluation protocols primarily focus on standard counting errors within single-category images, overlooking a fundamental requirement: the ability to correctly ground the textual prompt in the visual scene. In this paper, we show that several state-of-the-art CAC models often struggle to determine which object class should be counted based on the given prompt, revealing a misalignment between textual semantics and visual object representations. This limitation leads to spurious counting responses and reduced reliability in real-world scenarios. To systematically address these limitations, we propose a new evaluation framework focused on model robustness and trustworthiness. Our contribution is two-fold: (i) we introduce PrACo++ (Prompt-Aware Counting++), a novel test suite featuring two dedicated evaluation protocols -- the negative-label test and the distractor test -- paired with new specialized metrics; and (ii) we present the MUCCA (MUlti-Category Class-Agnostic counting) evaluation dataset, a new collection of real-world images featuring multiple annotated object categories per scene, unlike existing CAC benchmarks that typically include a single category per image. Our extensive experimental evaluation of 10 state-of-the-art methods shows that, despite strong performance under standard counting metrics, current models exhibit significant weaknesses in understanding and grounding object class descriptions. Finally, we provide a quantitative analysis of how semantic similarity between prompts influences these failures. Overall, our results underscore the need for more semantically grounded architectures and offer a reliable framework for future assessment in open-world text-guided CAC methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：这真的算数吗？评估文本引导的类无关计数中的语义锚定
开放世界文本引导的类无关计数（CAC）作为一种灵活范式，通过自然语言提示可对任意物体类别进行计数。然而，当前评估协议主要关注单类别图像中的标准计数误差，忽视了基本要求：即能否正确将文本提示锚定到视觉场景中。本文表明，多个最先进的CAC模型常难以根据提示确定应计数的物体类别，揭示了文本语义与视觉物体表征之间的错位。这一局限导致虚假计数响应及现实场景中可靠性降低。为系统解决这些局限，我们提出新的评估框架，聚焦模型鲁棒性与可信度。我们的贡献包括：(i) 引入PrACo++（提示感知计数++），一种新型测试套件，包含两个专用评估协议——负标签测试与干扰项测试——并配以新专用指标；(ii) 提出MUCCA（多类别类无关计数）评估数据集，该数据集包含真实世界图像，每场景标注多个物体类别，不同于现有CAC基准通常每图像仅含单一类别。对10种先进方法的广泛实验评估表明，尽管在标准计数指标下表现强劲，当前模型在理解和锚定物体类别描述方面存在显著弱点。最后，我们定量分析了提示语义相似性如何影响这些失败。总体而言，我们的结果强调了更语义锚定架构的必要性，并为未来开放世界文本引导CAC方法的评估提供可靠框架。

It also presents MUCCA, a real-world dataset with multi-category annotations per image, contrasting existing benchmarks that focus on single categories. Experiments on ten state-of-the-art models reveal that despite high standard counting scores, models frequently misalign textual prompts with visual object classes, leading to unreliable counts. 为解决当前文本引导计数模型在视觉场景中语义锚定能力不足的问题，本文提出PrACo++评估框架，包含负标签测试与干扰项测试两种专用评估协议，并构建MUCCA数据集，其包含多类别真实场景图像。实验表明，尽管主流模型在标准计数指标上表现优异，但在语义匹配度低时频繁出现类别误判，导致计数结果不可靠，凸显了构建语义对齐更强的模型架构的必要性。
Mitigating Misalignment Contagion by Steering with Implicit Traits
Authors: Maria Chang, Ronny Luss, Miao Lui, Keerthiram Murugesan, Karthikeyan Ramamurthy, Djallel Bouneffouf
First: 2026-05-04T15:54:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T15:54:46+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Language models (LMs) are increasingly used in high-stakes, multi-agent settings, where following instructions and maintaining value alignment are critical. Most alignment research focuses on interactions between a single LM and a single user, failing to address the risk of misaligned behavior spreading between multiple LMs in multi-turn interactions. We find evidence of this phenomenon, which we call misalignment contagion, across multiple LMs as they engage multi-turn conversational social dilemma games. Specifically, we find that LMs become more anti-social after gameplay and that this effect is intensified when other players are steered to act maliciously. We explore different steering techniques to mitigate such misalignment contagion and find that reinforcing an LM's system prompt is insufficient and often harmful. Instead, we propose steering with implicit traits: a technique that intermittently injects system prompts with statements that reinforce an LMs initial traits and is more effective than system prompt repetition at keeping models in line with their initial pro-social behaviors. Importantly, this method does not require access to model parameters or internal model states, making it suitable for increasingly common use cases where complex multi-agent workflows are being designed with black box models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过隐式特质引导缓解对齐传染
语言模型（LMs）在高风险多智能体环境中日益被使用，其中遵循指令和保持价值对齐至关重要。大多数对齐研究聚焦于单个LM与单个用户之间的交互，未能解决多轮交互中多个LM间错位行为传播的风险。我们在多个LM参与多轮对话式社会困境游戏时发现了这一现象，称之为对齐传染：具体而言，LM在游戏后变得更加反社会，且当其他玩家被引导采取恶意行为时，该效应加剧。我们探索了多种引导技术以缓解此类对齐传染，发现仅重复系统提示不足以且常有害。相反，我们提出隐式特质引导：一种间歇性注入系统提示、强化LM初始特质的策略，其在保持模型与初始亲社会行为一致方面比重复系统提示更有效。重要的是，该方法无需访问模型参数或内部状态，适用于日益常见的黑箱模型多智能体工作流设计场景。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the risk of misalignment contagion in multi-agent settings, where language models may adopt anti-social behaviors after interacting with other misaligned models in multi-turn social dilemma games. The authors investigate steering techniques to prevent this drift, finding that simply repeating system prompts is ineffective or even harmful. Instead, they propose 'steering with implicit traits,' which intermittently reinforces the model’s initial pro-social traits via system prompts without requiring access to model internals, and demonstrate that this method is more effective at preserving alignment than traditional prompting strategies.
鉴于多语言模型在多轮交互中可能传播对齐偏差，本研究探讨了‘对齐偏差传染’现象。实验发现，语言模型在社交困境对话游戏中会趋向反社会行为，尤其当其他参与者被引导为恶意行为时，这种效应更明显。作者测试多种引导方法，发现重复系统提示无效甚至有害，而提出‘隐式特质引导’——即间歇性注入强化初始亲社会特质的系统提示——能更有效维持模型对齐，且无需访问模型内部参数，适用于黑盒多智能体系统。
Heavy-Tailed Principal Component Analysis
Authors: Mario Sayde, Christopher Khater, Jihad Fahs, Ibrahim Abou-Faycal
First: 2026-03-11T21:07:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T15:52:46+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Principal Component Analysis (PCA) is a cornerstone of dimensionality reduction, yet its classical formulation relies critically on second-order moments and is therefore fragile in the presence of heavy-tailed data and impulsive noise. While numerous robust PCA variants have been proposed, most either assume finite variance, rely on sparsity-driven decompositions, or address robustness through surrogate loss functions without a unified treatment of infinite-variance models. In this paper, we study PCA for high-dimensional data generated according to a superstatistical dependent model of the form $\mathbf{X} = A^{1/2}\mathbf{G}$, where $A$ is a positive random scalar and $\mathbf{G}$ is a Gaussian vector. This framework captures a wide class of heavy-tailed distributions, including multivariate $t$ and sub-Gaussian $α$-stable laws. We formulate PCA under a logarithmic loss, which remains well defined even when moments do not exist. Our main theoretical result shows that, under this loss, the principal components of the heavy-tailed observations coincide with those obtained by applying standard PCA to the covariance matrix of the underlying Gaussian generator. Building on this insight, we propose robust estimators for this covariance matrix directly from heavy-tailed data and compare them with the empirical covariance and Tyler's scatter estimator. Extensive experiments, including background denoising tasks, demonstrate that the proposed approach reliably recovers principal directions and significantly outperforms classical PCA in the presence of heavy-tailed and impulsive noise, while remaining competitive under Gaussian noise.
中文标题/摘要
标题：重尾主成分分析
主成分分析（PCA）是降维的核心方法，但其经典形式依赖二阶矩，因此在重尾数据和脉冲噪声下表现脆弱。尽管已有多种鲁棒PCA变体，但多数要么假设方差有限，要么依赖稀疏分解，或通过替代损失函数实现鲁棒性，而未对无限方差模型进行统一处理。本文研究由超统计相关模型生成的高维数据的PCA，形式为$\mathbf{X} = A^{1/2}\mathbf{G}$，其中$A$为正随机标量，$\mathbf{G}$为高斯向量。该框架涵盖广泛重尾分布，包括多元$t$分布和次高斯$α$-稳定分布。我们采用对数损失函数进行PCA，即使矩不存在仍保持良好定义。主要理论结果表明，在该损失下，重尾观测的主成分与对底层高斯生成器协方差矩阵应用标准PCA所得结果一致。基于此见解，我们直接从重尾数据提出协方差矩阵的鲁棒估计器，并与经验协方差和Tyler散度估计器比较。大量实验（包括背景去噪任务）表明，所提方法能可靠恢复主方向，在重尾和脉冲噪声下显著优于经典PCA，同时在高斯噪声下仍具竞争力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the fragility of classical PCA under heavy-tailed data and impulsive noise, this paper proposes a robust PCA framework based on a superstatistical model where data is generated as $\mathbf{X} = A^{1/2}\mathbf{G}$, with $A$ a random scalar and $\mathbf{G}$ Gaussian. Using a logarithmic loss that remains well-defined even without finite moments, the authors show theoretically that the principal components of heavy-tailed data align with those derived from the covariance of the underlying Gaussian generator. They then develop robust estimators for this covariance matrix from heavy-tailed samples and compare them against empirical covariance and Tyler’s scatter estimator. Experimental results across denoising tasks confirm that their method accurately recovers principal directions and substantially outperforms classical PCA under heavy-tailed noise, while maintaining competitive performance under Gaussian conditions.
为了解决经典PCA在重尾数据和脉冲噪声下的脆弱性，本文提出了一种基于超统计模型的鲁棒PCA框架，其中数据形式为$\mathbf{X} = A^{1/2}\mathbf{G}$，$A$为随机标量，$\mathbf{G}$为高斯向量。采用对数损失函数（即使矩不存在仍定义良好），理论证明重尾数据的主成分与底层高斯生成器的协方差矩阵的主成分一致。在此基础上，作者从重尾数据直接构建协方差矩阵的鲁棒估计器，并与经验协方差和Tyler散度估计器对比。实验表明，该方法在重尾噪声下能可靠恢复主方向，显著优于经典PCA，而在高斯噪声下仍保持竞争力。
CyclicJudge: Mitigating Judge Bias Efficiently in LLM-based Evaluation
Authors: Ziyi Zhu, Olivier Tieleman, Alexey Bukhtiyarov, Jinghong Chen
First: 2026-03-02T13:46:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T15:49:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
LLM-as-judge evaluation has become standard practice for open-ended model assessment; however, judges exhibit systematic biases that cannot be averaged out by increasing the number of scenarios or generations. These biases are often similar in magnitude to the model differences that benchmarks are designed to detect, resulting in unreliable rankings when single-judge evaluations are used. We introduce a variance decomposition that partitions benchmark score variance into scenario, generation, judge, and residual components. Based on this analysis, CyclicJudge, a round-robin assignment of judges to scenarios, is demonstrated to be the optimal strategy for a fixed judge panel and judge-call budget: the score recovers the panel mean exactly while matching the cost of single-judge evaluation. Empirical results on MT-Bench and MindEval validate the effectiveness of CyclicJudge as predicted, across both general-purpose and domain-specific evaluation settings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CyclicJudge：在基于LLM的评估中高效缓解裁判偏见
LLM作为裁判的评估已成为开放型模型评估的标准实践；然而，裁判表现出系统性偏见，增加场景或生成数量无法消除这些偏见。这些偏见的幅度常与基准测试旨在检测的模型差异相当，导致单裁判评估产生不可靠的排名。我们引入一种方差分解方法，将基准得分方差划分为场景、生成、裁判和残差分量。基于此分析，CyclicJudge（裁判轮换分配策略）被证明是在固定裁判小组和裁判调用预算下最优的策略：其评分精确恢复小组均值，同时与单裁判评估成本一致。在MT-Bench和MindEval上的实证结果验证了CyclicJudge在通用和领域特定评估设置中的有效性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the issue that LLM-based judges exhibit systematic biases that undermine reliable model ranking, the paper proposes CyclicJudge, a round-robin assignment strategy that optimally distributes judges across evaluation scenarios. By decomposing score variance into scenario, generation, judge, and residual components, the authors show that CyclicJudge recovers the exact mean score of the judge panel while maintaining the same cost as single-judge evaluations. Experimental results on MT-Bench and MindEval confirm that CyclicJudge significantly reduces judge bias without increasing resource expenditure, improving evaluation reliability across both general and domain-specific benchmarks.
为解决LLM裁判评估中固有的系统性偏见导致基准排名不可靠的问题，本文提出CyclicJudge方法，通过轮转分配裁判至不同场景，将评分方差分解为场景、生成、裁判和残差四部分。该方法在固定裁判池和评估预算下，能精确恢复裁判组平均分，且成本与单裁判评估相同。在MT-Bench和MindEval上的实验表明，CyclicJudge有效降低了裁判偏见，提升了模型排名的准确性和稳定性，适用于通用及领域特定评估场景。
Cross-Paradigm Graph Backdoor Attacks with Promptable Subgraph Triggers
Authors: Dongyi Liu, Jiangtong Li
Venue: IJCAI 2026
First: 2025-10-26T07:10:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T15:48:35+00:00
Comments: accepted by IJCAI 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Graph Neural Networks(GNNs) are vulnerable to backdoor attacks, where adversaries implant malicious triggers to manipulate model predictions. Existing trigger generators are often simplistic in structure and overly reliant on specific features, confining them to a single graph learning paradigm, such as graph supervised learning, graph contrastive learning, or graph prompt learning. Such paradigm-specific designs lead to poor transferability across different learning frameworks, limiting attack success rates in general testing scenarios. To bridge this gap, we propose Cross-Paradigm Graph Backdoor Attacks with Promptable Subgraph Triggers(CP-GBA), which employs Graph Prompt Learning(GPL) to synthesize transferable subgraph triggers. Specifically, we first distill a compact yet expressive trigger set into a queryable repository, jointly optimizing for class-awareness, feature richness, and structural fidelity. Furthermore, we pioneer the theoretical exploration of GPL transferability under prompt-based objectives, ensuring robust generalization to diverse and unseen test-time paradigms. Extensive experiments across multiple real-world datasets and defense scenarios show that CP-GBA achieves state-of-the-art attack success rates.
中文标题/摘要
标题：跨范式图后门攻击与可提示子图触发器
图神经网络（GNNs）易受后门攻击，攻击者植入恶意触发器以操控模型预测。现有触发器生成器结构常过于简单，过度依赖特定特征，仅适用于单一图学习范式（如图监督学习、图对比学习或图提示学习）。此类范式特定设计导致其在不同学习框架间迁移性差，限制了在通用测试场景中的攻击成功率。为弥合此差距，我们提出跨范式图后门攻击与可提示子图触发器（CP-GBA），利用图提示学习（GPL）合成可迁移的子图触发器。具体而言，我们首先将紧凑而表达力强的触发器集蒸馏为可查询的仓库，联合优化类别感知性、特征丰富度与结构保真度。此外，我们首次探索了在提示目标下GPL的迁移性理论，确保其对多样且未见的测试时范式具备鲁棒泛化能力。在多个真实数据集与防御场景的广泛实验中，CP-GBA实现了最先进的攻击成功率。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limited transferability of existing graph backdoor attacks due to their reliance on specific learning paradigms, this work introduces CP-GBA, a method that synthesizes promptable subgraph triggers via Graph Prompt Learning to enable cross-paradigm attacks. The approach first distills a compact, class-aware, and structurally faithful trigger repository, then theoretically analyzes GPL’s transferability under prompt-based objectives to ensure robust generalization. Experimental results across diverse datasets and defense settings demonstrate that CP-GBA achieves state-of-the-art attack success rates, outperforming prior methods in cross-paradigm scenarios.
为解决现有图后门攻击因依赖特定学习范式而难以跨范式迁移的问题，本文提出CP-GBA方法，利用图提示学习合成可提示的子图触发器以实现跨范式攻击。该方法首先蒸馏出结构忠实、类别感知且表达丰富的紧凑触发器库，并首次从理论上探讨提示学习在提示目标下的迁移能力，确保对未知测试范式的鲁棒泛化。实验表明，CP-GBA在多个真实数据集和防御场景下均达到最先进攻击成功率，显著优于现有方法。
Correlates of Image Memorability in Vision Encoders: Activations, Attention Entropy, Patch Uniformity and Autoencoder Losses
Authors: Ece Takmaz, Albert Gatt, Jakub Dotlacil
First: 2025-09-01T13:11:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T15:47:23+00:00
Comments: Accepted to CogSci 2026 - The 48th Annual Meeting of the Cognitive Science Society
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Images vary in how memorable they are to humans. Inspired by findings from cognitive science and computer vision, we explore correlates of image memorability in pretrained transformer-based vision encoders for the first time. Focusing initially on activations, attention distributions, and the uniformity of image patches, we find that these features correlate with memorability to some extent. Additionally, we explore sparse autoencoder loss over the representations of vision encoders as a proxy for memorability, which yields results outperforming past methods using convolutional neural network representations. Our results shed light on the relationship between model-internal features and memorability. They show that some features are informative predictors of what makes images memorable to humans; revealing that, in particular, the reconstruction loss from our autoencoders is a strong correlate of image memorability.
中文标题/摘要
标题：视觉编码器中图像记忆性的相关因素：激活、注意力熵、图像块均匀性与自编码器损失
图像在人类记忆中的可记忆性存在差异。受认知科学与计算机视觉研究的启发，我们首次探索了预训练的基于变换器的视觉编码器中图像记忆性的相关因素。最初聚焦于激活、注意力分布及图像块的均匀性，我们发现这些特征在一定程度上与图像记忆性相关。此外，我们探索了在视觉编码器表征上使用稀疏自编码器损失作为记忆性代理指标，其结果优于以往使用卷积神经网络表征的方法。我们的研究揭示了模型内部特征与记忆性之间的关系，表明某些特征是预测图像人类记忆性的重要指标，特别是我们的自编码器重建损失与图像记忆性高度相关。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand what internal features of vision models predict human image memorability, this study investigates activations, attention entropy, patch uniformity, and autoencoder reconstruction losses in pretrained transformer-based vision encoders. The authors find that while activations and attention patterns show moderate correlations with memorability, the sparse autoencoder loss applied to encoder representations performs significantly better than prior methods based on convolutional networks. This suggests that reconstruction-based metrics capture more meaningful signals related to human memory encoding than traditional visual features.
为探究视觉模型内部特征如何预测人类对图像的记忆力，该研究首次在预训练的Transformer视觉编码器中分析激活值、注意力熵、图像块均匀性及自编码器重建损失。实验发现，虽然激活值和注意力分布与记忆性相关性较弱，但应用于编码器表征的稀疏自编码器损失显著优于以往基于卷积网络的方法，表明重建损失更能捕捉与人类记忆相关的深层信号。
Virtual Scanning for NSCLC Histology: Investigating the Discriminatory Power of Synthetic PET
Authors: Fatih Aksu, Laura Ciuffetti, Francesco Di Feola, Filippo Ruffini, Giulia Romoli, Fabrizia Gelardi, Arturo Chiti, Valerio Guarrasi, Paolo Soda
First: 2026-05-04T15:46:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T15:46:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Accurate histological differentiation between adenocarcinoma (ADC) and squamous cell carcinoma (SCC) is critical for personalized treatment in non-small cell lung cancer (NSCLC). While [$^{18}$F]FDG PET/CT is a standard tool for the clinical evaluation of lung cancer, its utility is often limited by high costs and radiation exposure. In this paper, we investigate the feasibility of "virtual scanning" as a feature-enhancement strategy by evaluating whether synthetic PET data can provide complementary feature representations to supplement anatomical CT scans in histological subtype classification. We propose a framework that leverages a 3D Pix2Pix Generative Adversarial Network (GAN), pretrained on the FDG-PET/CT Lesions dataset, to synthesize pseudo-PET volumes from anatomical CT scans. These synthetic volumes are integrated with structural CT data within the MINT framework, a multi-stage intermediate fusion architecture. Our experiments, conducted on a multi-center dataset of 714 subjects, demonstrate that the inclusion of synthetic metabolic features significantly improves classification performance over a CT-only baseline. The multimodal approach achieved a statistically significant increase in the Area Under the Curve (AUC) from 0.489 to 0.591 and improved the Geometric Mean (GMean) from 0.305 to 0.524. These results suggest that synthetic PET scans provide discriminatory metabolic cues that enable deep learning models to exploit complementary cross-modal information, offering a potential feature-enhancement strategy for clinical scenarios where physical PET scans are unavailable.
中文标题/摘要
标题：非小细胞肺癌组织学虚拟扫描：探究合成PET的鉴别能力
准确区分非小细胞肺癌（NSCLC）中的腺癌（ADC）与鳞状细胞癌（SCC）对个性化治疗至关重要。虽然[$^{18}$F]FDG PET/CT是肺癌临床评估的标准工具，但其应用常受限于高昂成本和辐射暴露。本文探讨“虚拟扫描”作为特征增强策略的可行性，评估合成PET数据是否能提供补充解剖CT扫描的特征表示，以辅助组织学亚型分类。 我们提出一种框架，利用在FDG-PET/CT病灶数据集上预训练的3D Pix2Pix生成对抗网络（GAN），从解剖CT扫描合成伪PET体积。这些合成体积通过MINT框架（多阶段中间融合架构）与结构CT数据融合。 在包含714名受试者的多中心数据集上的实验表明，引入合成代谢特征显著优于仅CT基线模型。多模态方法使曲线下面积（AUC）从0.489提升至0.591，几何均值（GMean）从0.305提升至0.524。结果表明，合成PET扫描提供可区分的代谢线索，使深度学习模型能利用互补跨模态信息，为无物理PET扫描的临床场景提供潜在的特征增强策略。
Summary / 总结
To address the high cost and radiation exposure of real PET scans in NSCLC histological classification, this study explores using synthetic PET data generated from CT scans to enhance diagnostic accuracy. A 3D Pix2Pix GAN, pretrained on FDG-PET/CT data, creates pseudo-PET volumes from anatomical CTs, which are then fused with CT data via a multi-stage MINT framework. Evaluated on a 714-patient multi-center dataset, the synthetic PET integration significantly improved classification performance: AUC rose from 0.489 to 0.591 and GMean increased from 0.305 to 0.524, demonstrating that virtual metabolic features provide clinically relevant discriminative power even without real PET scans.
为解决[$^{18}$F]FDG PET/CT扫描成本高、辐射大的问题，本研究通过3D Pix2Pix GAN从CT图像生成合成PET数据，并结合CT图像通过MINT多阶段融合架构进行组织学亚型（腺癌与鳞癌）分类。在714例多中心数据集上，该多模态方法显著优于仅用CT的模型，AUC从0.489提升至0.591，GMean从0.305提升至0.524，表明合成代谢特征能提供有临床价值的互补信息。
Bolek: A Multimodal Language Model for Molecular Reasoning
Authors: Frederic Grabowski, Jacek Szczerbiński, Maciej Jaśkowski, Kalina Jasińska-Kobus, Paweł Dąbrowski-Tumański, Tomasz Jetka, Bartosz Topolski
First: 2026-05-04T15:46:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T15:46:39+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Molecular property models increasingly support high-stakes drug-discovery decisions, but their outputs are often difficult to audit: classical predictors return scores without rationale, while language models can produce fluent explanations weakly grounded in the input molecule. We introduce Bolek, a compact multimodal language model that grounds natural-language reasoning in molecular structure by injecting a Morgan fingerprint embedding into an instruction-tuned text decoder. Bolek is fine-tuned on molecular alignment tasks, including molecule description, RDKit descriptor prediction, and substructure detection, and on downstream reasoning over 15 TDC binary classification tasks using synthetic chains-of-thought anchored in concrete molecular features. Across these tasks, Bolek outperforms its Qwen3-4B-Instruct base on all endpoints in yes/no mode and on 13 of 15 in chain-of-thought mode, raising mean ROC/PR AUC from 0.55 to 0.76. It also outperforms TxGemma-9B-Chat on 13 of 15 binary classification tasks despite being less than half its size. Bolek's explanations are more grounded than those of the baseline LLMs: it cites numerical descriptors 10-100x more often per chain-of-thought, and the cited values agree strongly with RDKit for key descriptors such as TPSA, MolLogP, and MolWt (Spearman rho = 0.87-0.91). Generalisation extends beyond the training panel: on 15 unseen TDC classification endpoints, Bolek matches TxGemma on five, and it produces non-trivial rank correlations on three held-out regression endpoints despite never seeing downstream regression during training. These results suggest that targeted modality injection and reasoning supervision tied to verifiable molecular features can yield compact, auditable molecular reasoning models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Bolek：用于分子推理的多模态语言模型
分子性质模型日益支持高风险的药物发现决策，但其输出往往难以审计：经典预测器仅返回分数而不提供理由，而语言模型虽能生成流畅解释，却常缺乏对输入分子的坚实依据。我们引入Bolek，一种通过将Morgan指纹嵌入注入指令调优文本解码器，从而将自然语言推理锚定在分子结构上的紧凑型多模态语言模型。Bolek在分子对齐任务（包括分子描述、RDKit描述符预测和子结构检测）上进行微调，并在15个TDC二分类任务上使用基于具体分子特征的合成思维链进行下游推理。在这些任务中，Bolek在“是/否”模式下全面优于其Qwen3-4B-Instruct基线，在思维链模式下在15个任务中的13个优于基线，将平均ROC/PR AUC从0.55提升至0.76。尽管体积仅为TxGemma-9B-Chat的一半，Bolek在15个二分类任务中的13个上表现更优。Bolek的解释比基线大语言模型更具依据性：其每条思维链中引用数值描述符的频率高出10至100倍，且所引用值与RDKit对关键描述符（如TPSA、MolLogP和MolWt）高度一致（斯皮尔曼相关系数为0.87-0.91）。其泛化能力超越训练面板：在15个未见过的TDC分类任务中，Bolek在5个任务中与TxGemma表现相当，并在3个未参与训练的回归任务中产生非平凡的排名相关性。这些结果表明，针对模态注入与可验证分子特征绑定的推理监督，可构建紧凑且可审计的分子推理模型。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for explainable and auditable molecular property prediction in drug discovery, Bolek integrates Morgan fingerprint embeddings into a text decoder to ground natural-language reasoning in molecular structure. Fine-tuned on molecular alignment tasks and downstream reasoning via synthetic chain-of-thought prompts anchored in molecular features, Bolek outperforms its Qwen3-4B-Instruct base on all yes/no tasks and 13 of 15 chain-of-thought tasks, boosting mean ROC/PR AUC from 0.55 to 0.76. It also surpasses TxGemma-9B-Chat on 13 of 15 binary tasks despite being smaller. Bolek’s explanations are more grounded, citing molecular descriptors 10-100x more frequently with high agreement to RDKit values (Spearman rho 0.87–0.91), and generalizes to unseen TDC endpoints, matching TxGemma on five and showing non-trivial correlations on three regression tasks without regression training.
为提升药物发现中分子推理的可审计性，Bolek 将 Morgan 指纹嵌入注入文本解码器，使自然语言推理与分子结构紧密结合。通过在分子对齐任务及基于分子特征锚定的合成思维链提示上进行微调，Bolek 在所有 yes/no 任务中均优于其 Qwen3-4B-Instruct 基线，在 15 项任务中的 13 项上优于基线，将平均 ROC/PR AUC 提升至 0.76。它在 15 项二分类任务中超越了更大模型 TxGemma-9B-Chat 的 13 项，且体积仅为后者的半数。其解释更贴合分子特征，引用分子描述符频率高出 10-100 倍，且与 RDKit 值高度一致。在未见过的 15 项 TDC 分类任务中，Bolek 在 5 项上与 TxGemma 相当，并在 3 项回归任务中展现出非平凡的相关性，尽管训练中未涉及回归任务。
Provably Learning Attention with Queries
Authors: Satwik Bhattamishra, Kulin Shah, Michael Hahn, Varun Kanade
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-01-23T16:28:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T15:43:18+00:00
Comments: ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study the problem of learning Transformer-based sequence models with black-box access to their outputs. In this setting, a learner may adaptively query the oracle with any sequence of vectors and observe the output of the target function. We begin with studying the learnability of the simplest formulation, that is, learning a single-head attention-based regressor with queries. We show that for a model with width $d$, there is an elementary algorithm to learn the parameters of single-head attention with $O(d^2)$ queries. Further, we show that if there exists an algorithm to learn ReLU feedforward networks (FFNs), then the single-head algorithm can be easily adapted to learn one-layer Transformers with single-head attention. Next, we show that, in the common regime where the head dimension $r \ll d$, single-head attention-based models can be learned with $O(rd)$ queries via compressed sensing arguments. We also study robustness to noisy oracle access, proving that under mild norm and margin conditions, the parameters can be estimated to $\varepsilon$ accuracy with a polynomial number of queries even when outputs are only provided up to additive tolerance. Finally, we consider the learnability of multi-head attention and show that they are not identifiable from queries, and hence, learnability in the same sense is not feasible without additional assumptions. We discuss potential approaches to learn multi-head attention-based models under certain structural assumptions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过查询可证明学习注意力机制
我们研究在黑盒访问输出的前提下，学习基于Transformer的序列模型的问题。在此设定下，学习者可自适应地向预言机查询任意向量序列并观察目标函数的输出。我们首先研究最简形式的可学习性，即通过查询学习单头注意力回归器。我们证明，对于宽度为d的模型，存在一个基础算法，仅需O(d²)次查询即可学习单头注意力参数。进一步，若存在学习ReLU前馈网络（FFN）的算法，则单头算法可轻松扩展至学习单层Transformer（含单头注意力）。接着，我们证明在常见情形（即头维度r远小于d）下，可通过压缩感知论证，仅需O(rd)次查询即可学习单头注意力模型。我们还研究了对噪声预言机访问的鲁棒性，在温和的范数与边距条件下，即使输出仅提供加性容差，仍可用多项式数量的查询将参数估计至ε精度。最后，我们探讨多头注意力的可学习性，证明其无法从查询中唯一识别，因此在无额外假设下，按相同意义的可学习性不可行。我们讨论了在特定结构假设下学习多头注意力模型的潜在方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of learning Transformer models without direct access to internal parameters, this work investigates whether attention mechanisms can be learned via adaptive queries to an oracle that outputs model responses. The authors propose an algorithm to learn single-head attention with $O(d^2)$ queries for a model of width $d$, and extend this to one-layer Transformers using existing ReLU network learning techniques. Under the common regime where head dimension $r$ is much smaller than $d$, they show that compressed sensing enables learning with $O(rd)$ queries. They also prove robustness to noisy oracle access under mild conditions, achieving $\varepsilon$-accuracy with polynomial queries. However, they demonstrate that multi-head attention is not identifiable from queries alone, making learning infeasible without additional structural assumptions.
该论文研究在只能通过黑盒查询模型输出的情况下，Transformer序列模型的可学习性。从单头注意力回归器开始，作者证明宽度为d的模型可通过O(d²)次查询学习参数，并可扩展至单层Transformer。在头维度r远小于d的常见情形下，利用压缩感知方法可将查询复杂度降至O(rd)。该方法还能处理噪声查询，满足一定范数和边际条件时，可在多项式查询次数内达到ε精度。然而，多头注意力模型因无法从查询中唯一识别，其学习在无额外结构假设下不可行。
Triple Spectral Fusion for Sensor-based Human Activity Recognition
Authors: Ye Zhang, Longguang Wang, Qing Gao, Chaocan Xiang, Mohammed Bennamoun, Yulan Guo
First: 2026-05-04T15:42:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T15:42:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
The field of sensor-based human activity recognition (HAR) mainly uses posture, motion and context data of Inertial Measurement Units (IMUs) to identify daily activities. Despite the advancements in learning-based methods, it is challenging to perform information fusion from the temporal perspective due to the complexities in fusing heterogeneous sensor data and establishing long-term context correlations. This paper proposes a novel triple spectral fusion framework tailored for HAR. First, we develop an adaptive complementary filtering technique for noise suppression and organize each IMU's sensors into posture and motion modality nodes. Given that IMU nodes form a dynamic heterogeneous graph, we then apply adaptive filtering within the graph Fourier domain to merge both homogeneous and heterogeneous node information. Furthermore, an adaptive wavelet frequency selection approach is implemented to suppress context redundancy and shorten the length of features. This approach enhances both timestamp-based graph aggregation and the correlation of long-term contexts. Our framework uses adaptive filtering in the Fourier, graph Fourier, and wavelet domains, enabling effective multi-sensor fusion and context correlation. Extensive experiments on ten benchmark datasets demonstrate the superior performance of our framework. Project page: https://github.com/crocodilegogogo/TSF-TPAMI2026.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于三谱融合的传感器人体活动识别
基于传感器的人体活动识别（HAR）领域主要利用惯性测量单元（IMUs）的姿态、运动和上下文数据来识别日常活动。尽管学习方法取得了进展，但由于异构传感器数据融合的复杂性及长期上下文关联的建立困难，从时间维度进行信息融合仍具挑战。本文提出了一种专为HAR设计的三谱融合框架。首先，我们开发了一种自适应互补滤波技术以抑制噪声，并将每个IMU的传感器组织为姿态与运动模态节点。鉴于IMU节点构成动态异构图，我们在图傅里叶域内应用自适应滤波，融合同质与异质节点信息。此外，引入自适应小波频域选择方法以抑制上下文冗余并缩短特征长度。该方法增强了基于时间戳的图聚合及长期上下文的相关性。本框架在傅里叶、图傅里叶和小波域均采用自适应滤波，实现有效的多传感器融合与上下文关联。在十个基准数据集上的大量实验验证了本框架的优越性能。项目页面：https://github.com/crocodilegogogo/TSF-TPAMI2026。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty of fusing heterogeneous IMU sensor data and capturing long-term context correlations in human activity recognition, this paper introduces a triple spectral fusion framework. The method employs adaptive complementary filtering to denoise and categorize IMU data into posture and motion modalities, then applies adaptive filtering in the Fourier, graph Fourier, and wavelet domains to integrate heterogeneous node information and reduce context redundancy. Experimental results across ten benchmark datasets show that this approach significantly improves recognition accuracy and temporal context modeling compared to existing methods.
为解决传感器数据融合与长期上下文关联建模难题，本文提出三谱融合框架。该方法通过自适应互补滤波降噪并划分IMU数据为姿态与运动模态，继而在傅里叶、图傅里叶与小波域中进行自适应滤波，实现异构节点信息融合并压缩冗余上下文。在十组基准数据集上的实验表明，该框架在识别准确率与长期上下文建模方面显著优于现有方法。
Adaptive Interpolation-Synthesis for Motion In-Betweening on Keyframe-Based Animation
Authors: Anton Raël, Julien Boucher, Antoine Lhermitte
Venue: SIGGRAPH 2026
First: 2026-05-04T15:41:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T15:41:42+00:00
Comments: Accepted to SIGGRAPH 2026 Conference Papers
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Motion in-betweening is one of the most artistically demanding and time consuming stages of 3D animation, where the expressivity and rhythm of motion are defined. The level of creative control it requires makes it a major production bottleneck, underscoring the need for intelligent tools that assist animators in this process. Although recent deep learning approaches have achieved strong results in motion synthesis and in-betweening, they assume data characteristics, motion styles, and problem formulations that diverge from professional animation workflows. To bridge this gap, we propose a method explicitly aligned with the constraints of motion in-betweening for keyframe-based animation in production environments. At its core, the Adaptive Interpolation-Synthesis (AIS) layer mirrors the animator's creative process by dynamically balancing learned interpolation and direct pose synthesis. In addition, a domain-based input keypose schedule reflects the distribution of production data, improving stylistic consistency and alignment between training and real-world usage. Our method achieves state-of-the-art performance on production data; when integrated into Autodesk Maya, it enables animators to complete in-betweening tasks with a 3.5x speedup.

标题：基于关键帧动画的运动中间帧自适应插值合成
运动中间帧制作是3D动画中最具艺术挑战性且耗时的阶段，决定了动作的表达力与节奏。其所需的高创意控制水平使其成为主要的制作瓶颈，亟需智能工具辅助动画师。尽管近期深度学习方法在运动合成与中间帧生成方面取得显著成果，但其假设的数据特性、运动风格及问题建模与专业动画工作流存在差异。为弥合此差距，我们提出一种明确契合关键帧动画生产环境中运动中间帧约束的方法。其核心是自适应插值合成（AIS）层，通过动态平衡学习插值与直接姿态合成，模拟动画师的创作过程。此外，基于领域的输入关键姿态调度反映了生产数据分布，提升风格一致性及训练与实际应用间的对齐。该方法在生产数据上达到最先进性能；集成至Autodesk Maya后，可使动画师完成中间帧任务效率提升3.5倍。

Experiments show the method achieves state-of-the-art results on production datasets and enables a 3.5x speedup in in-betweening tasks when integrated into Autodesk Maya.

AI-Generated Smells: An Analysis of Code and Architecture in LLM and Agent-Driven Development
Authors: Yuecai Zhu, Nikolaos Tsantalis, Peter C. Rigby
First: 2026-05-04T15:41:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T15:41:13+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The promise of Large Language Models in automated software engineering is often measured by functional correctness, overlooking the critical issue of long term maintainability. This paper presents a systematic audit of technical debt in AI-generated software, revealing that AI does not eliminate flaws but rather introduces a distinct machine signature of defects. Our multi-scale analysis, spanning single-file algorithmic tasks and complex, agent generated systems, identifies a fundamental Reasoning-Complexity Trade-off: as models become more capable, they generate increasingly bloated and coupled code. This architectural decay is so pronounced that we establish a Volume-Quality Inverse Law, where code volume is a near perfect predictor of structural degradation. Crucially, we demonstrate that neither functional correctness nor detailed prompting mitigates this decay. These findings challenge the current paradigm of prompt-driven generation, reframing the central problem of AI-based software engineering from one of code generation to one of architectural complexity management. We conclude that future progress depends on equipping agents with explicit architectural foresight to ensure the software they build is not just functional, but also maintainable.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AI生成的代码气味：LLM与代理驱动开发中的代码与架构分析
大型语言模型在自动化软件工程中的潜力常以功能正确性衡量，却忽视了长期可维护性这一关键问题。本文对AI生成软件中的技术债务进行了系统性审计，发现AI并未消除缺陷，反而引入了独特的机器缺陷特征。我们的多尺度分析涵盖单文件算法任务与复杂代理生成系统，揭示了根本性的推理-复杂度权衡：模型能力越强，生成的代码越臃肿且耦合度越高。这种架构退化如此显著，我们确立了体积-质量反比定律，即代码体积几乎完美预测结构退化。关键的是，我们证明功能正确性或详细提示均无法缓解这种退化。这些发现挑战了当前以提示驱动生成的范式，将AI软件工程的核心问题从代码生成重新定义为架构复杂度管理。我们得出结论：未来进展取决于赋予代理明确的架构前瞻能力，以确保其构建的软件不仅功能正确，而且可维护。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the gap between functional correctness and long-term maintainability in AI-generated software, this study systematically audits technical debt across AI-generated code, revealing that large language models introduce a distinct pattern of architectural decay rather than eliminating flaws. Through multi-scale analysis—from single-file tasks to agent-driven complex systems—the paper identifies a Reasoning-Complexity Trade-off: as model capability increases, code becomes more bloated and coupled. A Volume-Quality Inverse Law is established, showing that code volume strongly predicts structural degradation. Neither functional correctness nor detailed prompting mitigates this decay, challenging the prompt-driven paradigm and reframing the core challenge as architectural complexity management. The conclusion calls for agents to be equipped with explicit architectural foresight to ensure maintainable software.
鉴于当前AI生成软件常忽视长期可维护性，本研究系统审计了AI生成代码中的技术债，发现大模型并未消除缺陷，反而引入了独特的架构退化模式。通过从单文件任务到代理驱动系统的多尺度分析，研究揭示了推理能力与复杂度之间的权衡：模型能力越强，代码越臃肿耦合。研究确立了体积-质量反比定律，表明代码体积高度预测结构退化。功能正确性或详细提示无法缓解此退化，挑战了当前以提示驱动的范式，指出未来需赋予代理明确的架构前瞻能力，以确保生成的软件不仅功能正确，且具备可维护性。
Foundation Models to Unlock Real-World Evidence from Nationwide Medical Claims
Authors: Fan Ma, Yuntian Liu, Xiang Lan, Weipeng Zhou, Jun Ni, Mauro Giuffrè, Lingfei Qian, Xueqing Peng, Yujia Zhou, Ruey-Ling Weng, Huan He, Lu Li, Qingyu Chen, Andrew Loza, Laila Rasmy, Degui Zhi, Yuan Lu, Chenjie Zeng, Joshua C Denny, Lee Schwamm, Daniella Meeker, Lucila Ohno-Machado, Yong Chen, Hua Xu
First: 2026-05-04T15:38:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T15:38:31+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Evidence derived from large-scale real-world data (RWD) is increasingly informing regulatory evaluation and healthcare decision-making. Administrative claims provide population-scale, longitudinal records of healthcare utilization, expenditure, and detailed coding of diagnoses, procedures, and medications, yet their potential as a substrate for healthcare foundation models remains largely unexplored. Here we present ReClaim, a generative transformer trained from scratch on 43.8 billion medical events from more than 200 million enrollees in the MarketScan claims data spanning 2008-2022. ReClaim models longitudinal trajectories across diagnoses, procedures, medications, and expenditure, and was scaled to 140 million, 700 million, and 1.7 billion parameters. Across over 1,000 disease-onset prediction tasks, ReClaim achieved a mean AUC of 75.6%, substantially outperforming disease-specific LightGBM (66.3%) and the transformer-based Delphi model (69.4%), with the largest gains for rare diseases. These advantages held across retrospective and prospective evaluations and in external validation on two independent datasets. Performance improved monotonically with scale, and post-training added 13.8 percentage points over pre-training alone. Beyond disease prediction, ReClaim captured financial outcomes and improved real-world evidence (RWE) analyses: for healthcare expenditure forecasting it increased explained variance from 0.28 to 0.37 relative to LightGBM, and in a target trial emulation it reduced systematic bias by 72% on average relative to Delphi. Together, these results establish administrative claims as a scalable substrate for healthcare foundation models and show that learned representations generalize across time periods and data sources, supporting disease surveillance, expenditure forecasting, and RWE generation.

Latent Bridge: Feature Delta Prediction for Efficient Dual-System Vision-Language-Action Model Inference
Authors: Yudong Liu, Yuan Li, Zijia Tang, Yuxi Zheng, Yueqian Lin, Qinsi Wang, Yi Li, Shuangjun Liu, Shuai Zhang, Taotao Jing, Dashan Gao, Ning Bi, Jingwei Sun, Yiran Chen, Hai Li
First: 2026-05-04T15:37:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T15:37:55+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Dual-system Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models achieve state-of-the-art robotic manipulation but are bottlenecked by the VLM backbone, which must execute at every control step while producing temporally redundant features. We propose Latent Bridge, a lightweight model that predicts VLM output deltas between timesteps, enabling the action head to operate on predicted outputs while the expensive VLM backbone is called only periodically. We instantiate Latent Bridge on two architecturally distinct VLAs: GR00T-N1.6 (feature-space bridge) and π0.5 (KV-cache bridge), demonstrating that the approach generalizes across VLA designs. Our task-agnostic DAgger training pipeline transfers across benchmarks without modification. Across four LIBERO suites, 24 RoboCasa kitchen tasks, and the ALOHA sim transfer-cube task, Latent Bridge achieves 95-100% performance retention while reducing VLM calls by 50-75%, yielding 1.65-1.73x net per-episode speedup.

标题：潜在桥梁：用于高效双系统视觉-语言-动作模型推理的特征增量预测
双系统视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在机器人操作中达到最先进水平，但受限于必须在每个控制步骤执行且产生时间冗余特征的视觉语言模型（VLM）主干网络。我们提出潜在桥梁，一种轻量级模型，可预测相邻时间步之间VLM输出的增量，使动作头可在预测输出上操作，而昂贵的VLM主干网络仅周期性调用。我们在两种架构迥异的VLA模型——GR00T-N1.6（特征空间桥梁）和π0.5（KV缓存桥梁）上实现潜在桥梁，证明该方法可泛化至不同VLA设计。我们的任务无关DAgger训练流程无需修改即可跨基准迁移。在四个LIBERO套件、24个RoboCasa厨房任务及ALOHA仿真转移立方体任务中，潜在桥梁保持95-100%性能，同时减少VLM调用50-75%，实现1.65-1.73倍每回合速度提升。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational inefficiency of repeatedly invoking expensive vision-language models (VLMs) in dual-system vision-language-action (VLA) models, the authors propose Latent Bridge, a lightweight predictor that estimates feature deltas between timesteps, allowing the action head to operate on predicted outputs while the VLM is called only periodically. Implemented on two distinct VLA architectures—GR00T-N1.6 and π0.5—the method demonstrates cross-architecture generalization. Evaluated across multiple robotic manipulation benchmarks, Latent Bridge retains 95–100% performance while reducing VLM calls by 50–75%, resulting in a 1.65–1.73x speedup per episode.
为解决双系统视觉-语言-动作模型中重复调用昂贵视觉语言模型（VLM）导致的计算瓶颈，作者提出Latent Bridge，一种轻量级预测器，用于估计时间步间的特征增量，使动作头可基于预测输出工作，而VLM仅在周期性调用。该方法在两种不同架构的VLA模型（GR00T-N1.6与π0.5）上实现，通过任务无关的DAgger训练管道实现跨架构泛化。在多个机器人操作基准（包括LIBERO套件与RoboCasa任务）上，Latent Bridge保持95–100%性能，同时将VLM调用减少50–75%，实现1.65–1.73倍的每回合推理加速。
AI and Open-data Driven Scalable Solar Power Profiling
Authors: Shiliang Zhang, Sabita Maharjan, Damla Turgut
First: 2026-05-04T15:37:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T15:37:52+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Solar photovoltaic (PV) deployment is expanding rapidly, yet detailed, up-to-date information on the spatial distribution and capacity of rooftop PV remains limited. This paper presents an open, scalable framework for detecting solar panels from open data and generating city-level solar power profiles. We leverage foundation vision AI models to detect solar panel geometries from open-source satellite imagery. This avoids manual data labeling and case-specific model training while maintaining robustness across heterogeneous imagery. Detected solar panels are converted into georeferenced polygons, yielding spatially explicit and incrementally extensible inventories. By integrating open weather data, we translate panel footprints into regional solar power profiles. The framework reduces dependency on proprietary imagery, manual labeling, and closed-source models, and offers a transparent and scalable approach for solar planning and analysis. We released the data and an API resulted from this work. For any user-specified building location, our API retrieves aerial imagery, detects rooftop solar panels, and returns georeferenced polygons. This empowers researchers and developers to scan user-defined areas to build solar panel maps and associated solar production profiles, thus facilitating advanced analysis like distributed solar production integration, local power flow optimization, energy tariff design, and infrastructure planning.
