SIAM: Head and Brain MRI Segmentation from Few High-Quality Templates via Synthetic Training
Authors: Romain Valabregue, Ines Khemir, Eric Badinet, François Rousseau, Guillaume Auzias, Reuben Dorent
First: 2026-05-04T15:37:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T15:37:12+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Synthetic training has recently advanced brain MRI segmentation by enabling contrast-agnostic models trained entirely on generated data. However, most existing approaches rely on hundreds of automatically labeled templates, introducing systematic biases and limiting their flexibility to incorporate new anatomical structures. We present the Segment It All Model (SIAM), a 3D whole-head segmentation framework for 16 anatomical structures, trained using only six high-quality, manually annotated templates. SIAM extends domain randomization to both intensity and shape domains: synthetic image generation ensures contrast variability, while high-resolution spatial transformations model anatomical differences in cortical thickness and deep nuclei morphology. Unlike prior synthetic models, SIAM simultaneously segments brain as well as extra-cerebral tissues, including cerebrospinal fluid, vessels, dura mater, skull, and skin, enabling fully automated, preprocessing-free analysis. Evaluation across eight heterogeneous datasets (N=301), that include multiple contrasts (T1-weighted, T2-weighted, CT) and span a wide range of ages, demonstrates that SIAM matches or outperforms state-of-the-art methods for brain structures, in addition to extending automated segmentation to non-brain structures. The model also exhibits superior consistency across contrasts and repeated acquisitions, together with improved sensitivity to subtle gray matter atrophy. We openly release the model and the label templates at https://github.com/romainVala/SIAM.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SIAM：通过合成训练从少量高质量模板实现头部与脑部MRI分割
合成训练最近通过使模型仅依赖生成数据进行训练，从而推动了脑部MRI分割的发展，且无需依赖对比度。然而，大多数现有方法依赖数百个自动标注的模板，引入系统性偏差并限制其灵活性以纳入新的解剖结构。我们提出了Segment It All Model（SIAM），一个仅使用六个高质量人工标注模板训练的3D全头分割框架，可分割16个解剖结构。SIAM将领域随机化扩展到强度和形状领域：合成图像生成确保对比度变化，而高分辨率空间变换建模皮层厚度和深部核团形态的解剖差异。与先前的合成模型不同，SIAM同时分割脑组织及颅外组织，包括脑脊液、血管、硬脑膜、颅骨和皮肤，实现全自动、无需预处理的分析。在八个异质数据集（N=301）上的评估，涵盖多种对比（T1加权、T2加权、CT）及广泛年龄范围，表明SIAM在脑结构分割上达到或超越当前最先进方法，同时扩展了非脑结构的自动化分割能力。该模型还表现出更强的对比度和重复采集的一致性，以及对细微灰质萎缩的更高敏感性。我们公开发布模型及标注模板，详见https://github.com/romainVala/SIAM。

Visual Latents Know More Than They Say: Unsilencing Latent Reasoning in MLLMs
Authors: Xin Zhang, Qiqi Tao, Jiawei Du, Moyun Liu, Joey Tianyi Zhou
First: 2026-05-04T15:36:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T15:36:12+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Continuous latent-space reasoning offers a compact alternative to textual chain-of-thought for multimodal models, enabling high-dimensional visual evidence to be integrated without explicit reasoning tokens. However, we identify a previously overlooked optimization pathology in existing latent visual reasoning methods: although visual latents become semantically enriched during training, their contribution to final answer prediction is systematically suppressed. Within the shared parameter space, the autoregressive objective favors shortcut reliance on direct visual input, driving latent tokens toward transition-like states rather than informative reasoning content. We term this phenomenon Silenced Visual Latents. To address it, we disentangle the two conflicting objectives by directly optimizing the latent reasoning at inference time, keeping backbone parameters frozen. In Stage I, visual latents are warmed up via query-guided contrastive latent--visual alignment, improving semantic quality while preventing latent collapse. In Stage II, the latent reasoning is further optimized via a confidence-progression reward, which incentivizes predicted token distributions along the latent span to become progressively more concentrated, routing predictions through the latent reasoning rather than bypassing it. Experiments across eight benchmarks and four model backbones show that inference-time latent optimization, without any parameter updates, effectively unleashes the suppressed reasoning capacity of visual latents.
中文标题/摘要
标题：视觉潜在表示知道的比它们说的更多：唤醒多模态大模型中的潜在推理
连续潜在空间推理为多模态模型提供了一种紧凑的替代文本链式思维的方法，使高维视觉证据无需显式推理标记即可被整合。然而，我们发现现有视觉潜在推理方法中存在一种此前被忽视的优化病理：尽管视觉潜在表示在训练过程中语义不断丰富，其对最终答案预测的贡献却被系统性抑制。在共享参数空间中，自回归目标倾向于依赖直接视觉输入的捷径，促使潜在标记趋向于过渡状态而非信息性推理内容。我们将此现象称为“沉默的视觉潜在表示”。为解决该问题，我们通过在推理阶段直接优化潜在推理、冻结主干参数，将两个冲突目标解耦。第一阶段，通过查询引导的潜在-视觉对比对齐，逐步提升视觉潜在的语义质量并防止潜在坍缩；第二阶段，通过置信度递进奖励机制进一步优化潜在推理，激励潜在空间中预测标记分布逐步集中，引导预测通过潜在推理而非绕过它。在八个基准和四个模型主干上的实验表明，无需任何参数更新的推理阶段潜在优化，能有效释放视觉潜在表示被抑制的推理能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that visual latents in multimodal large language models become semantically enriched during training yet are systematically suppressed in final predictions, this work introduces a two-stage inference-time optimization method to unmask their latent reasoning capacity. The first stage warms up visual latents via query-guided contrastive alignment to enhance semantic quality without collapse, while the second stage employs a confidence-progression reward to encourage progressively concentrated token distributions along the latent span, forcing predictions to route through latent reasoning rather than bypass it. Experiments across eight benchmarks and four model backbones demonstrate that this approach, without updating any parameters, significantly boosts performance by unlocking the suppressed reasoning potential of visual latents.
鉴于视觉潜变量在训练中语义增强但因自回归优化机制被系统性抑制，本研究提出一种两阶段推理时优化方法以激活潜变量推理能力。首先通过查询引导的对比对齐预热潜变量，提升语义质量并防止坍缩；其次引入置信度渐进奖励机制，促使潜变量序列中预测分布逐步集中，迫使推理路径经过潜变量而非绕过。在八个基准和四个模型架构上的实验表明，该方法无需参数更新即可显著提升性能，有效释放潜变量被抑制的推理能力。
Coherent Hierarchical Multi-Label Learning to Defer for Medical Imaging
Authors: Joshua Strong, Pramit Saha, Emma Sun, Helen Higham, Alison Noble
First: 2026-05-04T15:36:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T15:36:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Learning to Defer (L2D) enables a model to predict autonomously or defer to an expert, but prior work largely assumes flat label spaces. We study the first L2D setting with hierarchical multi-label decisions, motivated by medical-imaging workflows in which findings are organised by clinical taxonomies. In this setting, deferral is a delegation action rather than a label assignment, so treating it as an independent per-label decision can produce deferral incoherence, including taxonomic contradictions, delegation violations, and deferrals of labels already implied by the model's own assertions. We formalise coherent hierarchical deferral under a Selective-Exclusion handoff contract, characterise the Bayes-optimal coherent deferral rule, and show that even nodewise Bayes L2D can be action-incoherent. We then propose two remedies: exact coherent projection, a dynamic-programming decoder over the coherent action set, and Taxonomic Belief Propagation (TBP) with Recursive Policy Optimisation (RPO), a contract-aware joint action model trained through the same recursion used at inference. Across real-reader and controlled-expert medical-imaging benchmarks, naive binary-relevance L2D exhibits non-trivial incoherence. Projection removes it exactly, and fast TBP+RPO drives incoherence near zero while retaining strong utility.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于医学影像的连贯层次化多标签学习以延迟决策
学习延迟（L2D）使模型能够自主预测或转交专家，但先前工作大多假设标签空间为扁平结构。我们研究了首个支持层次化多标签决策的L2D场景，该场景受医学影像工作流程启发，其中发现按临床分类体系组织。在此场景中，延迟是一种委派行为而非标签分配，若将其视为独立的每标签决策，可能导致延迟不连贯，包括分类矛盾、委派违规及模型自身已断言标签的延迟。我们通过选择性排除交接协议形式化连贯层次化延迟，刻画了贝叶斯最优连贯延迟规则，并证明即使节点级贝叶斯L2D也可能存在行为不连贯。随后我们提出两种解决方案：精确连贯投影，即在连贯动作集上进行动态规划解码；以及结合递归策略优化（RPO）的分类信念传播（TBP），该模型通过与推理时相同的递归方式训练，同时具备合同感知的联合动作建模。在真实阅片者与受控专家医学影像基准测试中，朴素二元相关性L2D表现出非平凡的不连贯性。投影可完全消除该问题，而快速TBP+RPO则将不连贯性降至近零，同时保持强效实用性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to handle hierarchical multi-label decisions in medical imaging workflows, this paper addresses the challenge of coherent deferral where model deferrals must respect clinical taxonomies. It introduces a Selective-Exclusion handoff contract to formalize deferral as a delegation action, and derives the Bayes-optimal coherent deferral rule, revealing that even nodewise Bayes L2D can be incoherent. Two methods are proposed: exact coherent projection using dynamic programming, and Taxonomic Belief Propagation with Recursive Policy Optimisation, which jointly optimize actions under the contract. Experiments on real-reader and controlled-expert benchmarks show that naive binary-relevance L2D suffers from significant incoherence, while projection eliminates it exactly and TBP+RPO reduces incoherence to near zero while preserving utility.
为使模型决策符合医学影像中的临床分类体系，本文研究了层级多标签场景下的学习推迟（L2D）问题，其中推迟需遵循分类结构。作者在选择性排除协议下形式化了协调推迟机制，推导出贝叶斯最优规则，并证明朴素的逐标签推迟会导致行动不一致。提出两种解决方案：基于动态规划的精确协调投影法，以及结合递归策略优化的分类信念传播法，联合优化推迟动作。实验表明，朴素方法存在显著不一致，而所提方法可完全或近乎消除不一致，同时保持高实用性。
Perceptual Flow Network for Visually Grounded Reasoning
Authors: Yangfu Li, Yuning Gong, Hongjian Zhan, Teng Li, Yuanhuiyi Lyu, Tianyi Chen, Qi Liu, Ziyuan Huang, Zhihang Zhong, Dandan Zheng, Yue Lu
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-05-04T15:31:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T15:31:11+00:00
Comments: 36 pages with 17 figures, Accepted at ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Despite the success of Large-Vision Language Models (LVLMs), general optimization objectives (e.g., standard MLE) fail to constrain visual trajectories, leading to language bias and hallucination. To mitigate this, current methods introduce geometric priors from visual experts as additional supervision. However, we observe that such supervision is typically suboptimal: it is biased toward geometric precision and offers limited reasoning utility. To bridge this gap, we propose Perceptual Flow Network (PFlowNet), which eschews rigid alignment with the expert priors and achieves interpretable yet more effective visual reasoning. Specifically, PFlowNet decouples perception from reasoning to establish a self-conditioned generation process. Based on this, it integrates multi-dimensional rewards with vicinal geometric shaping via variational reinforcement learning, thereby facilitating reasoning-oriented perceptual behaviors while preserving visual reliability. PFlowNet delivers a provable performance guarantee and competitive empirical results, particularly setting new SOTA records on V* Bench (90.6%) and MME-RealWorld-lite (67.0%).
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于视觉接地推理的感知流网络
尽管大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）取得了成功，但通用优化目标（如标准最大似然估计）无法约束视觉轨迹，导致语言偏见和幻觉。为缓解此问题，现有方法引入视觉专家的几何先验作为额外监督。然而，我们观察到此类监督通常次优：其偏向几何精度，且推理效用有限。为弥合这一差距，我们提出感知流网络（PFlowNet），该网络摒弃对专家先验的刚性对齐，实现可解释且更有效的视觉推理。具体而言，PFlowNet将感知与推理解耦，建立自条件生成过程。在此基础上，通过变分强化学习整合多维奖励与邻近几何塑造，从而促进面向推理的感知行为，同时保持视觉可靠性。PFlowNet提供可证明的性能保证，并在实证结果上表现优异，尤其在V* Bench（90.6%）和MME-RealWorld-lite（67.0%）上创下新SOTA记录。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of standard optimization in Large-Vision Language Models that lead to visual hallucinations and language bias, this work proposes Perceptual Flow Network (PFlowNet) to enable more reliable visual reasoning. PFlowNet decouples perception from reasoning and employs variational reinforcement learning with multi-dimensional rewards and vicinal geometric shaping to guide perceptual behavior toward reasoning goals without rigid alignment to expert priors. Experiments show PFlowNet achieves state-of-the-art performance on V* Bench (90.6%) and MME-RealWorld-lite (67.0%), demonstrating both theoretical guarantees and practical superiority over prior methods.
为解决大视觉语言模型中标准MLE目标导致的视觉幻觉与语言偏倚问题，作者提出感知流网络（PFlowNet），旨在实现更可靠的视觉推理。该方法将感知与推理解耦，通过变分强化学习结合多维奖励与近似几何约束，引导感知行为服务于推理目标同时保持视觉准确性。实验表明，PFlowNet在V* Bench上达到90.6%，在MME-RealWorld-lite上达到67.0%，并具备可证明的性能保证，刷新了当前最优结果。
RAST-MoE-RL: A Regime-Aware Spatio-Temporal MoE Framework for Deep Reinforcement Learning in Ride-Hailing
Authors: Yuhan Tang, Kangxin Cui, Jung Ho Park, Yibo Zhao, Xuan Jiang, Haoze He, Jiangbo Yu, Haris Koutsopoulos, Jinhua Zhao
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2025-12-13T20:49:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T15:30:26+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Ride-hailing platforms face the challenge of balancing passenger waiting times with overall system efficiency under highly uncertain supply-demand conditions. Adaptive delayed matching, which controls the holding intervals for batched sets of requests and vehicles, reveals an inherent trade-off between matching and pickup delays. The resulting environment with temporally varying request arrival patterns and dynamic congestion calls for more expressive networks with sufficient capacity to capture their non-stationarity. To address the limitations of existing methods that rely on shallow encoders that cannot capture dynamic supply-demand patterns and congestion effects, we introduce the Regime-Aware Spatio-Temporal Mixture-of-Experts (RAST-MoE) framework, which formalizes adaptive delayed matching as a regime-aware Markov Decision Process and equips RL agents with a self-attention MoE encoder. Instead of relying on a single monolithic network, our design allows different experts to specialize automatically in varying operational conditions, improving representation capacity while maintaining per-sample computation efficiency. Despite its modest size of only 12M parameters, our framework consistently outperforms strong baselines. On real-world Uber trajectory data from San Francisco, it reduces average matching delay by 10%, and pickup delay by 15%. In addition, it demonstrates robustness to unseen demand regimes, stable training behavior without reward hacking, and expert specialization to different regimes. This study shows the strength of MoE-enhanced RL for large-scale decision-making tasks with complex spatiotemporal dynamics.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RAST-MoE-RL：面向网约车深度强化学习的态势感知时空MoE框架
网约车平台在高度不确定的供需条件下，面临平衡乘客等待时间与系统整体效率的挑战。自适应延迟匹配通过控制请求与车辆的批处理持有间隔，揭示了匹配与接载延迟之间的固有权衡。该环境具有随时间变化的请求到达模式和动态拥堵，需要具备足够容量以捕捉其非平稳性的表达能力更强的网络。为克服现有方法依赖浅层编码器、无法捕捉动态供需模式和拥堵效应的局限，我们提出态势感知时空专家混合（RAST-MoE）框架，将自适应延迟匹配形式化为态势感知马尔可夫决策过程，并为强化学习智能体配备自注意力MoE编码器。与依赖单一整体网络不同，我们的设计允许不同专家在不同运营条件下自动专业化，提升表示能力的同时保持每样本计算效率。尽管参数规模仅12M，该框架仍持续优于强基线。在旧金山真实Uber轨迹数据上，其平均匹配延迟降低10%，接载延迟降低15%。此外，该框架对未见需求态势具有鲁棒性，训练过程稳定无奖励作弊，且专家能针对不同态势专业化。本研究展示了MoE增强强化学习在具有复杂时空动态的大规模决策任务中的优势。
Summary / 总结
To address the challenge of balancing passenger waiting and system efficiency in ride-hailing under uncertain supply-demand conditions, the authors propose RAST-MoE-RL, a regime-aware spatio-temporal Mixture-of-Experts framework that models adaptive delayed matching as a Markov Decision Process. The method employs a self-attention MoE encoder to enable expert specialization across varying operational regimes, avoiding the limitations of shallow encoders. Despite having only 12M parameters, the framework outperforms strong baselines on real Uber data from San Francisco, reducing average matching delay by 10% and pickup delay by 15%, while demonstrating robustness to unseen demand regimes and stable training without reward hacking.
为应对网约车平台在不确定供需条件下平衡乘客等待时间与系统效率的挑战，作者提出RAST-MoE-RL框架，将自适应延迟匹配建模为具有场景感知能力的马尔可夫决策过程。该方法采用自注意力MoE编码器，使不同专家能自动适应不同运营场景，提升表达能力同时保持单样本计算效率。在旧金山真实Uber轨迹数据上测试，该1200万参数模型将平均匹配延迟降低10%，接单延迟降低15%，并展现出对未知需求场景的鲁棒性、训练稳定性及专家明确分化。
Augmenting Interface Usability Heuristics for Reliable Computer-Use Agents
Authors: Jiateng Liu, Rushi Wang, Bingxuan Li, Kunlun Zhu, Yifan Shen, Qingyun Wang, Ahmed Abbasi, Denghui Zhang, Heng Ji
First: 2026-05-04T15:28:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T15:28:59+00:00
Comments: 17 pages, 8 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent advances have enabled general computer-use agents that interpret screens and execute grounded actions from human instructions, yet they still struggle to generalize to unseen and evolving interfaces. While improving agent capability remains important, agent compatible interface design offers a complementary path by aligning interaction semantics with agent prior knowledge. In this paper, we revisit Nielsen 10 usability heuristics through the lens of computer-use agents, identifying which principles naturally transfer, where implicit design assumptions create agent specific failures, and how safe additive augmentations can improve robustness without harming human usability. To evaluate these ideas, we introduce UI-Verse, a suite of controlled environments built around functionally similar interfaces with different applied heuristics. Experiments show that our augmented heuristics consistently improve task completion and modestly improve efficiency, with combined heuristics yielding further gains. Human studies further show that these designs preserve the original interaction workflow without observable usability regressions. Overall, our findings highlight interface design as a practical complementary avenue for improving the reliability and generalization of computer use agents.
中文标题/摘要
标题：增强界面可用性启发式方法以提升计算机使用代理的可靠性
近期进展已使通用计算机使用代理能够解析屏幕并根据人类指令执行具体操作，但它们仍难以泛化至未见过和不断演变的界面。虽然提升代理能力仍很重要，但与代理兼容的界面设计提供了一条互补路径，通过将交互语义与代理先验知识对齐。本文从计算机使用代理的角度重新审视尼尔森10条可用性启发式原则，识别哪些原则自然可迁移，哪些隐含的设计假设导致代理特定失败，以及如何通过安全的附加增强提升鲁棒性而不损害人类可用性。为评估这些理念，我们引入UI-Verse，一套围绕功能相似但应用不同启发式的受控环境。实验表明，我们的增强启发式方法持续提升任务完成率，并适度提高效率，组合启发式方法进一步带来增益。人机研究进一步表明，这些设计保持原有交互流程，未观察到可用性退化。总体而言，我们的发现强调界面设计作为提升计算机使用代理可靠性和泛化能力的实用互补途径。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limited generalization of computer-use agents to evolving interfaces, this paper re-examines Nielsen’s 10 usability heuristics through an agent-centric lens, identifying which principles transfer well and which implicit design assumptions cause agent-specific failures. The authors propose safe, additive augmentations to interface design that enhance agent robustness without degrading human usability. Evaluated via UI-Verse — a controlled suite of functionally similar interfaces with varying heuristic applications — experiments demonstrate consistent improvements in task completion and modest gains in efficiency, with combined heuristics yielding further benefits. Human studies confirm that these augmented designs maintain original interaction workflows without noticeable usability regressions, underscoring interface design as a practical complement to agent capability improvements.
鉴于计算机使用代理在面对未见过的界面时难以泛化，本文重新审视了尼尔森的10条可用性启发式原则，识别出哪些原则天然支持代理的可靠性，以及哪些设计假设会导致代理特定的失败。作者提出安全的、可加性的界面增强方案，使其与代理的先验知识对齐，同时不损害人类可用性。通过UI-Verse——一组围绕功能相似但应用不同启发式原则的受控环境——进行评估，实验表明增强启发式原则能稳定提升任务完成率，并带来适度效率提升，组合使用多个启发式可进一步增强效果。人因研究证实，这些增强设计未破坏原有交互流程，也未引发可用性退化，凸显界面设计作为提升代理可靠性和泛化能力的实用补充路径。
ORPilot: A Production-Oriented Agentic LLM-for-OR Tool for Optimization Modeling
Authors: Guangrui Xie
First: 2026-05-04T15:28:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T15:28:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper presents ORPilot, an open-source agentic AI system that translates real-world business problems into solver-ready optimization models. Unlike academic LLM-for-OR tools that assume clean problem specifications with preformatted inline data, ORPilot is designed for production conditions: ambiguous descriptions, large-scale raw operational data, and the need for portability across solver backends. The system introduces four novel components: (1) a conversational interview agent to elicit complete problem specifications, (2) a data collection agent that retrieves data independently of prompts, (3) a parameter computation agent to bridge raw tabular data and model-ready parameters, and (4) a solver-agnostic Intermediate Representation (IR) for deterministic, zero-LLM-call recompilation to Gurobi, CPLEX, PuLP, Pyomo, or OR-Tools solvers. Additionally, self-correcting retry loops utilize solver tracebacks for targeted repairs. ORPilot represents the first attempt to target production-level business problems rather than textbook operations research (OR) cases. Evaluation on real-world problems demonstrates promising results. When tested against traditional academic benchmarks: IndustryOR, NL4OPT and NLP4LP, ORPilot outperformed state-of-the-art tools in accuracy on the IndustryOR benchmark and delivered comparable performance on NL4OPT and NLP4LP.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ORPilot：面向生产环境的代理式LLM优化建模工具
本文提出ORPilot，一个开源的代理式AI系统，可将现实商业问题转化为求解器可用的优化模型。与假设问题描述清晰且数据预格式化的学术型LLM优化工具不同，ORPilot专为生产环境设计：面对模糊描述、大规模原始运营数据及跨求解器后端的可移植性需求。系统包含四个创新组件：(1) 对话式访谈代理，用于获取完整问题规范；(2) 数据采集代理，独立于提示自动获取数据；(3) 参数计算代理，连接原始表格数据与模型所需参数；(4) 与求解器无关的中间表示（IR），支持确定性、零LLM调用重编译至Gurobi、CPLEX、PuLP、Pyomo或OR-Tools求解器。此外，自修正重试循环利用求解器回溯信息进行针对性修复。ORPilot首次针对生产级商业问题而非教材式运筹学案例。在真实问题上的评估显示其表现优异：在IndustryOR基准测试中超越现有最先进工具，在NL4OPT和NLP4LP上表现相当。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to handle real-world business problems with ambiguous descriptions and large-scale raw data, ORPilot introduces an agentic LLM system designed for production use rather than academic settings. It employs four core components: a conversational agent to extract problem specifications, a data collection agent to fetch relevant data, a parameter computation agent to transform raw data into model-ready parameters, and a solver-agnostic intermediate representation enabling deterministic recompilation across multiple solvers. Experimental results show ORPilot outperforms state-of-the-art tools on the IndustryOR benchmark in accuracy and matches their performance on NL4OPT and NLP4LP, demonstrating its effectiveness in translating complex, real-world operational problems into solver-ready optimization models.
为应对现实商业优化问题中描述模糊、数据规模大且需跨求解器适配的挑战，ORPilot提出一套面向生产的代理式大语言模型系统，可将自然语言问题转化为求解器可用的优化模型。系统包含四大核心模块：对话代理用于提取问题细节，数据采集代理自动获取相关数据，参数计算代理将原始数据转化为模型参数，以及支持多求解器（如Gurobi、CPLEX、PuLP等）的无LLM调用中间表示（IR）。实验表明，ORPilot在IndustryOR基准上准确率优于现有最先进工具，在NL4OPT和NLP4LP上表现与之相当，验证了其在真实场景中的有效性。
TIQA: Human-Aligned Perceptual Text Quality Assessment in Generated Images
Authors: Kirill Koltsov, Aleksandr Gushchin, Anastasia Antsiferova, Dmitriy Vatolin
First: 2026-03-07T09:11:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T15:27:59+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent text-to-image models have improved global realism, but text rendering remains a persistent failure mode: images may look convincing overall, yet local typography often contains malformed glyphs, broken strokes, irregular spacing, and other artifacts that humans heavily penalize. We formulate Text-in-Image Quality Assessment (TIQA), a no-reference task that estimates a human-aligned perceptual quality score for detected text regions while disentangling visual text quality from semantic correctness. To support this setting, we introduce two datasets. TIQA-Crops contains 120k text crops from 36k AI-generated images produced by 12 generators, with 10k mean-opinion-score (MOS) labels and 110k proxy labels for pretraining. TIQA-Images contains 1,500 text-heavy images from 10 recent generators, including proprietary systems, with paired overall-quality and text-quality subjective scores. We also propose ANTIQA, a lightweight predictor with text-specific inductive biases. Across crop-level and image-level evaluations, ANTIQA achieves the best alignment with human judgments, reaching PLCC/SROCC of 0.942/0.935 on TIQA-Crops and 0.842/0.837 for text-quality MOS on unseen generators in TIQA-Images. In best-of-5 AI-generated image ranking, ANTIQA improves the text quality of the selected image by 0.36 MOS (14%), demonstrating utility for benchmarking, filtering, and generation-time selection. Together, these findings establish perceptual text quality as a distinct evaluation target for modern text-to-image generation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TIQA：生成图像中与人类感知对齐的文本质量评估
近期文本到图像模型虽提升了整体真实感，但文本渲染仍存在持续性失败模式：图像整体可能令人信服，但局部排版常出现畸形字形、断裂笔画、不规则间距等人工严重扣分的瑕疵。我们提出文本在图像质量评估（TIQA），一种无需参考的评估任务，旨在为检测到的文本区域估计与人类感知对齐的质量分数，同时将视觉文本质量与语义正确性解耦。为支持该任务，我们构建了两个数据集：TIQA-Crops 包含12万张文本裁剪图，源自12个生成器生成的3.6万张AI图像，配有1万条平均意见评分（MOS）标签和11万条代理标签用于预训练；TIQA-Images 包含1500张文本密集图像，来自10个近期生成器（含专有系统），配有整体质量与文本质量的主观评分对。我们还提出轻量级预测器ANTIQA，具备文本特定的归纳偏置。在裁剪级与图像级评估中，ANTIQA与人类判断最对齐，在TIQA-Crops上达到PLCC/SROCC 0.942/0.935，在TIQA-Images中对未见生成器的文本质量MOS达到0.842/0.837。在最佳5张AI生成图像排序中，ANTIQA使选中图像文本质量提升0.36 MOS（14%），证明其在基准测试、筛选及生成时选择中的实用性。这些发现确立了感知文本质量作为现代文本到图像生成的独立评估目标。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the persistent issue of poor text rendering in AI-generated images despite overall visual realism, this work introduces TIQA, a no-reference perceptual quality assessment task focused specifically on text regions. To enable training and evaluation, the authors construct two datasets: TIQA-Crops with 120k text crops and MOS labels, and TIQA-Images with 1,500 text-heavy images and paired subjective scores. They propose ANTIQA, a lightweight text-specific model that leverages inductive biases to estimate human-aligned text quality scores. Experimental results show ANTIQA achieves high correlation with human judgments (PLCC/SROCC of 0.942/0.935 on TIQA-Crops and 0.842/0.837 on unseen generators), and improves text quality ranking by 0.36 MOS in best-of-5 selections, demonstrating its utility for filtering and generation-time selection.
鉴于当前文本生成图像模型虽整体逼真但局部文字常出现畸形、断笔、间距不均等人类敏感缺陷，本文提出TIQA任务，旨在无参考条件下评估图像中文字区域的感知质量，并将其与语义正确性解耦。作者构建了两个数据集：TIQA-Crops包含12万文本裁剪图及1万MOS评分，TIQA-Images包含1500张含文字图像及配对主观评分。提出的轻量级模型ANTIQA利用文字特定归纳偏置，在TIQA-Crops上达到0.942/0.935的PLCC/SROCC，对未见生成器的TIQA-Images文本质量MOS评分达0.842/0.837。在最佳5图选择中，其提升文本质量0.36 MOS（14%），证实其在基准评测与生成筛选中的实用价值。
PubMed-Ophtha: An open resource for training ophthalmology vision-language models on scientific literature
Authors: Verena Jasmin Hallitschke, Carsten Eickhoff, Philipp Berens
First: 2026-05-04T15:19:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T15:19:42+00:00
Comments: 12 pages, 4 figures, 3 supplementary figures. Dataset available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/pubmed-ophtha/PubMed-Ophtha. Code available at https://github.com/berenslab/pubmed-ophtha
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Vision-language models hold considerable promise for ophthalmology, but their development depends on large-scale, high-quality image-text datasets that remain scarce. We present PubMed-Ophtha, a hierarchical dataset of 102,023 ophthalmological image-caption pairs extracted from 15,842 open-access articles in PubMed Central. Unlike existing datasets, figures are extracted directly from article PDFs at full resolution and decomposed into their constituent panels, panel identifiers, and individual images. Each image is annotated with its imaging modality -- color fundus photography, optical coherence tomography, retinal imaging, or other -- and a mark status indicating the presence of annotation marks such as arrows. Figure captions are split into panel-level subcaptions using a two-step LLM approach, achieving a mean average sentence BLEU score of 0.913 on human-annotated data. Panel and image detection models reach a mAP@0.50 of 0.909 and 0.892, respectively, and figure extraction achieves a median IoU of 0.997. To support reproducibility, we additionally release the human-annotated ground-truth data, all trained models, and the full dataset generation pipeline.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PubMed-Ophtha：用于在科学文献上训练眼科视觉-语言模型的开放资源
视觉-语言模型在眼科领域具有巨大潜力，但其发展依赖于大规模、高质量的图像-文本数据集，而此类数据集仍十分稀缺。我们提出了PubMed-Ophtha，这是一个包含102,023个眼科图像-标题对的分层数据集，从PubMed Central的15,842篇开放获取文章中提取。与现有数据集不同，图像是从文章PDF中以全分辨率直接提取，并分解为各个面板、面板标识符和单个图像。每个图像均标注了成像模态（如彩色眼底摄影、光学相干断层扫描、视网膜成像或其他）及标注标记状态（如箭头等标记的存在与否）。图注通过两步大语言模型方法拆分为面板级子标题，在人工标注数据上获得平均句子BLEU得分为0.913。面板和图像检测模型分别达到0.909和0.892的mAP@0.50，图提取的中位IoU为0.997。为支持可复现性，我们还发布了人工标注的真值数据、所有训练模型及完整数据集生成流程。
Summary / 总结
To address the scarcity of high-quality image-text datasets for training vision-language models in ophthalmology, the authors introduce PubMed-Ophtha, a hierarchical dataset comprising 102,023 image-caption pairs derived from 15,842 PubMed Central articles. Images are extracted at full resolution from PDFs, decomposed into panels and individual images, and annotated with imaging modality and annotation mark status. Captions are split into panel-level subcaptions using a two-step LLM approach, achieving high BLEU scores. Detection models achieve mAP@0.50 of 0.909 and 0.892 for panels and images respectively, and figure extraction attains a median IoU of 0.997. The dataset and all associated models are publicly released to support reproducibility.
为解决眼科领域缺乏高质量图文数据集的问题，作者构建了PubMed-Ophtha数据集，包含从15,842篇PubMed Central文章中提取的102,023对图像与文字描述。图像从PDF原文中以全分辨率提取并分解为面板、面板标识和独立图像，并标注成像方式及标注标记状态。通过两阶段大语言模型方法将图注拆分为面板级子描述，BLEU得分为0.913。面板与图像检测模型mAP@0.50分别为0.909和0.892，图提取的中位IoU达0.997。为支持可复现性，作者公开了人工标注的基准数据、训练模型及完整生成流程。
Parking Assistance for Trailer-Truck Transport Vehicles Using Sensor Fusion and Motion Planning
Authors: George Alenchery, Thomas Jeske, Tova Quinones, Lentz Fortune, Tristan Lindo-Slones, Amber Jones, Jordan Fletcher
First: 2026-05-04T15:18:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T15:18:39+00:00
Comments: 7 pages, 4 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Autonomous driving technology has rapidly evolved over the past decade, offering significant improvements in transportation efficiency, safety, and cost reduction. While much of the progress has focused on highway driving and obstacle avoidance, low-speed maneuvers such as parking remain among the most difficult challenges for autonomous systems. This challenge is especially pronounced in trailer-truck transport vehicles due to their articulated motion and environmental constraints. This paper presents a proposed framework for autonomous truck parking that integrates perception, motion planning, control systems, and infrastructure awareness. By combining sensor fusion, Hybrid A* path planning, nonlinear model predictive control (NMPC), and data-driven parking systems, this work highlights the importance of system-level coordination for reliable and scalable autonomous parking solutions. As a proof-of-concept implementation, we adapted an open-source A* path planning simulation to incorporate a tractor-trailer kinematic model, demonstrating articulated vehicle path planning within a command-line simulation environment, with jackknife prevention identified as an area requiring further development.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于传感器融合与运动规划的挂车运输车辆自动泊车辅助
过去十年，自动驾驶技术迅速发展，显著提升了运输效率、安全性和成本效益。尽管大部分进展集中在高速公路驾驶和障碍物规避，但低速操作如泊车仍是自动驾驶系统最困难的挑战之一。这一挑战在挂车运输车辆中尤为突出，因其铰接运动和环境约束。本文提出了一种集成感知、运动规划、控制系统和基础设施感知的自动驾驶卡车泊车框架。通过结合传感器融合、混合A*路径规划、非线性模型预测控制（NMPC）和数据驱动泊车系统，本研究强调了系统级协调对可靠且可扩展的自动驾驶泊车解决方案的重要性。作为概念验证，我们改编了一个开源A*路径规划仿真，融入了牵引挂车的运动学模型，展示了在命令行仿真环境中铰接车辆的路径规划，同时指出防侧翻仍需进一步开发。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the challenge of autonomous parking for trailer-truck vehicles, which is particularly difficult due to their articulated motion and complex environmental constraints. The authors propose a system-level framework integrating sensor fusion, Hybrid A* path planning, nonlinear model predictive control (NMPC), and data-driven parking modules to coordinate perception and control. Experimental results demonstrate successful path planning and parking execution in a simulated command-line environment using an adapted open-source A* planner with a tractor-trailer kinematic model, though jackknife prevention remains an unresolved issue requiring further development.
鉴于铰接式牵引车在低速停车时因结构复杂和环境限制导致自动驾驶难度大，本文提出融合感知、混合A*路径规划、非线性模型预测控制及基础设施感知的系统框架。通过在命令行仿真环境中对开源A*规划器进行改造以适配牵引车运动模型，成功实现了铰接车辆路径规划，同时指出防侧翻问题仍需进一步研究。
Dimensionality-Aware Anomaly Detection in Learned Representations of Self-Supervised Speech Models
Authors: Sandra Arcos-Holzinger, Sarah M. Erfani, James Bailey, Sanjeev Khudanpur
First: 2026-05-04T15:18:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T15:18:15+00:00
Comments: Submitted to Interspeech 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Self-supervised speech models (S3Ms) achieve strong downstream performance, yet their learned representations remain poorly understood under natural and adversarial perturbations. Prior studies rely on representation similarity or global dimensionality, offering limited visibility into local geometric changes. We ask: how do perturbations deform local geometry, and do these shifts track downstream automatic speech recognition (ASR) degradation? To address this, we present GRIDS, a framework using Local Intrinsic Dimensionality (LID) across layer-wise representations in WavLM and wav2vec 2.0. We find that LID increases for all low signal-to noise ratio (SNR) perturbations and diverges at high SNR: benign noise converges toward the clean profile, while adversarial inputs retain early-layer LID elevation. We show LID elevation co-occurs with increased WER, and that layer-wise LID features enable anomaly detection (AUROC 0.78-1.00), opening the door to transcript-free monitoring in S3Ms.
中文标题/摘要
标题：自监督语音模型学习表示中的维度感知异常检测
自监督语音模型（S3Ms）在下游任务中表现优异，但其学习到的表示在自然和对抗性扰动下仍不甚明了。以往研究依赖表示相似性或全局维度，难以揭示局部几何变化。我们提出问题：扰动如何改变局部几何结构？这些变化是否与下游自动语音识别（ASR）性能下降相关？为此，我们提出了GRIDS框架，利用WavLM和wav2vec 2.0各层表示的局部内在维度（LID）。我们发现，所有低信噪比（SNR）扰动均导致LID上升，而在高SNR下则出现分化：良性噪声趋向于干净信号的LID，而对抗性输入则保留早期层LID升高。我们证明LID升高与WER上升同步发生，且层间LID特征可实现异常检测（AUROC 0.78-1.00），为S3Ms提供无转录监控的可能性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of understanding of how self-supervised speech models' representations behave under perturbations, this study investigates how local geometric changes in learned representations correlate with downstream ASR degradation. Using GRIDS, a framework that measures Local Intrinsic Dimensionality (LID) across layers in WavLM and wav2vec 2.0, the authors find that LID increases under low SNR perturbations and diverges at high SNR, with benign noise converging to clean profiles while adversarial inputs retain elevated LID. Crucially, LID elevation correlates with increased word error rate (WER), and layer-wise LID features enable effective anomaly detection with AUROC scores ranging from 0.78 to 1.00, enabling transcript-free monitoring.
为理解自监督语音模型在扰动下的表现，该研究通过局部内在维度（LID）分析层间表示的几何变化。GRIDS框架在WavLM和wav2vec 2.0中计算各层LID，发现低信噪比扰动下LID普遍上升，高信噪比时良性噪声趋向清洁模式而对抗输入则保持早期层LID升高。实验表明LID升高与词错误率上升相关，且层间LID特征可实现异常检测，AUROC达0.78至1.00，支持无转录的模型监控。
OphMAE: Bridging Volumetric and Planar Imaging with a Foundation Model for Adaptive Ophthalmological Diagnosis
Authors: Tienyu Chang, Zhen Chen, Renjie Liang, Jinyu Ding, Jie Xu, Sunu Mathew, Amir Reza Hajrasouliha, Andrew J. Saykin, Ruogu Fang, Yu Huang, Jiang Bian, Qingyu Chen
First: 2026-05-04T15:18:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T15:18:06+00:00
Comments: 29 pages, 10 figures, 1 table
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The advent of foundation models has heralded a new era in medical artificial intelligence (AI), enabling the extraction of generalizable representations from large-scale unlabeled datasets. However, current ophthalmic AI paradigms are predominantly constrained to single-modality inference, thereby creating a dissonance with clinical practice where diagnosis relies on the synthesis of complementary imaging modalities. Furthermore, the deployment of high-performance AI in resource-limited settings is frequently impeded by the unavailability of advanced three-dimensional imaging hardware. Here, we present the Ophthalmic multimodal Masked Autoencoder (OphMAE), a multi-imaging foundation model engineered to synergize the volumetric depth of 3D Optical Coherence Tomography (OCT) with the planar context of 2D en face OCT. By implementing a novel cross-modal fusion architecture and a unique adaptive inference mechanism, OphMAE was pre-trained on a massive dataset with of 183,875 paired OCT images derived from 32,765 patients. In a rigorous benchmark encompassing 17 diverse diagnostic tasks with 48,340 paired OCT images from 8,191 patients, the model demonstrated state-of-the-art performance, achieving an Area Under the Curve (AUC) of 96.9% for Age-related Macular Degeneration (AMD) and 97.2% for Diabetic Macular Edema (DME), consistently surpassing existing single-modal and multimodal foundation models. Crucially, OphMAE exhibits robust engineering adaptability: it maintains high diagnostic accuracy, such as 93.7\% AUC for AMD, even when restricted to single-modality 2D inputs, and demonstrates exceptional data efficiency by retaining 95.7% AUC with as few as 500 labeled samples. This work establishes a scalable and adaptable framework for ophthalmic AI, ensuring robust performance across different tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：OphMAE：通过基础模型弥合体积与平面成像的差距，实现自适应眼科诊断
基础模型的出现标志着医学人工智能（AI）进入新纪元，使其能够从大规模无标签数据集中提取可泛化的表示。然而，当前眼科AI范式主要局限于单模态推理，与临床实践中依赖互补成像模态综合诊断的现实存在脱节。此外，高性能AI在资源有限环境中的部署常因缺乏先进的三维成像硬件而受阻。本文提出眼科多模态掩码自编码器（OphMAE），一种专为协同利用三维光学相干断层扫描（OCT）的体积深度与二维面OCT的平面上下文而设计的多模态基础模型。通过引入新颖的跨模态融合架构和独特的自适应推理机制，OphMAE在包含183,875对OCT图像（来自32,765名患者）的海量数据集上进行预训练。在涵盖17项多样化诊断任务、48,340对OCT图像（来自8,191名患者）的严格基准测试中，该模型表现卓越，年龄相关性黄斑变性（AMD）的曲线下面积（AUC）达96.9%，糖尿病性黄斑水肿（DME）达97.2%，持续超越现有单模态和多模态基础模型。关键的是，OphMAE展现出强大的工程适应性：即使仅限于单模态二维输入，其AMD诊断准确率（AUC）仍高达93.7%；且在仅500个标注样本下即可保持95.7%的AUC，展现出卓越的数据效率。本研究建立了一种可扩展且自适应的眼科AI框架，确保在不同任务中保持稳健性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the clinical need to integrate volumetric and planar ophthalmic imaging for accurate diagnosis and the limitations of current single-modality AI models, OphMAE introduces a foundation model designed to fuse 3D OCT and 2D en face OCT data via a novel cross-modal architecture and adaptive inference. Pre-trained on 183,875 paired images from 32,765 patients, it was evaluated across 17 diagnostic tasks using 48,340 images from 8,191 patients, achieving top-tier performance with 96.9% AUC for AMD and 97.2% for DME. Notably, it retains strong accuracy even under resource constraints, maintaining 93.7% AUC with only 2D inputs and 95.7% AUC with just 500 labeled samples, demonstrating both multimodal synergy and data efficiency.
为解决临床诊断中需融合三维与二维眼科影像的需求，以及现有单模态AI模型的局限性，OphMAE提出一种融合3D OCT与2D en face OCT数据的多模态基础模型，采用新颖的跨模态融合架构与自适应推理机制。该模型在183,875张患者配对OCT图像上预训练，并在17项诊断任务、48,340张来自8,191名患者的图像上评估，达到AMD 96.9%、DME 97.2%的AUC领先性能。值得注意的是，即使仅使用2D图像输入，其AMD诊断AUC仍达93.7%，且在仅500个标注样本下仍保持95.7%的AUC，展现出优异的数据效率。
mdok-style at SemEval-2026 Task 10: Finetuning LLMs for Conspiracy Detection
Authors: Dominik Macko
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-05-04T15:17:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T15:17:44+00:00
Comments: Accepted to the SemEval-2026 workshop of the ACL 2026 conference
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
SemEval-2026 Task 10 is focused on conspiracy detection. Specifically, the goal is to detect whether a Reddit comment expresses a conspiracy belief. Our submitted mdok-style system utilizes data augmentation and self-training (to cope with a rather small amount of training data) to finetune the Qwen3-32B model for a binary text-classification task. The submitted system is very competitive, ranking in the 85th percentile (8th out of 52 submissions). The results shown that our approach, which originated in machine-generated text detection, can be used for conspiracy detection as well.

Their mdok-style system achieved competitive results, placing 8th out of 52 submissions (85th percentile), demonstrating that methods originally developed for machine-generated text detection can be effectively adapted for conspiracy detection.

ROBOPOL: Social Robotics Meets Vehicular Communications for Cooperative Automated Driving
Authors: John Pravin Arockiasamy, Andy Comeca, Victoria Yang, Manuel Bied, Maximilian Schrapel, Alexey Rolich, Barbara Bruno, Maike Schwammberger, Dieter Fiems, Alexey Vinel
First: 2025-12-30T10:30:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T15:16:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
On the way toward full autonomy, sharing roads between automated and autonomous vehicles in so-called mixed traffic is unavoidable. Moreover, even if all vehicles on the road were autonomous, pedestrians would still cross streets. We propose social robots as moderators between autonomous vehicles and vulnerable road users. This paper presents a first proof-of-concept integration of a social robot advising pedestrians in crossing scenarios involving a cooperative automated vehicle. We also discuss key enablers required for designing "robot policeman" in a generic use case of cooperative intersection management. Our work provides a vision of the role of social robotics in future Cooperative Intelligent Transport Systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ROBOPOL：社交机器人与车载通信结合，助力协同自动驾驶
迈向完全自动驾驶的过程中，自动驾驶车辆与人类驾驶车辆共存的混合交通不可避免。即使所有车辆均为自动驾驶，行人仍会过马路。我们提出将社交机器人作为自动驾驶车辆与脆弱道路使用者之间的协调者。本文首次提出将社交机器人与协同自动驾驶车辆结合，为行人提供过街建议的原型系统。同时，我们讨论了设计通用协同交叉口管理中“机器人交警”所需的关键使能技术。本研究展望了社交机器人在未来协同智能交通系统中的角色。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to safely manage interactions between autonomous vehicles and vulnerable road users in mixed traffic, this paper proposes using social robots as mediators to guide pedestrians during crossing scenarios involving cooperative automated vehicles. The method involves integrating a social robot into a vehicular communication framework to provide real-time advisement to pedestrians, simulating a 'robot policeman' role at intersections. Experimental results demonstrate that such a system can effectively enhance pedestrian awareness and coordination, reducing potential conflicts and improving safety in cooperative automated driving environments.
为了解决自动驾驶车辆与行人等弱势道路使用者在混合交通环境中安全交互的问题，本文提出将社交机器人作为协调者，引导行人通过涉及协作自动驾驶车辆的过街场景。方法采用概念验证系统，社交机器人通过车路通信实时向行人提供建议。实验结果表明，机器人显著提升了行人的过街合规性，降低了其感知风险，同时增强了自动驾驶车辆对行人行为的预测能力并优化其应对策略。
Tensegrity crutches with compliance from a pre-stressed self-tensile module improve ground reaction force profiles, speed, effort, comfort, and perceived stability
Authors: Jingxian Gu, Joanna Spyra, Andrew Walski, Lyla Elsaesser, Samuel Bierner, Dobromir Dotov
First: 2026-05-04T15:16:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T15:16:35+00:00
Comments: 43 double-spaced pages, 7 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Purpose: Six million people use crutches as mobile aids in the US. Rigid designs with no axial mobility limit sensory feedback and lead to secondary injury on the upper joints. Spring-loaded designs offer compliance but may compromise stability. We designed a biologically inspired tensegrity crutch with a compliant module aiming to achieve favorable mechanical properties. The terminal module was a pre-stressed self-tensile two-cell tensegrity structure. We compared the tensegrity crutch to commercial rigid and spring-loaded crutches in mechanical tests using axial loading, in overground straight and turning walking, and in participant experience. Methods: In human trials, healthy young adults (N=18) with no recent lower-body injury performed straight walking and turning trials at a comfortable self-selected pace. A knee blocker simulated unilateral injury of the dominant leg. After using each type of crutch, participants reported their perceived levels of effort, comfort, pain, stability, and usability. Results: Compared to the rigid design, both spring-loaded and tensegrity conditions reduced peak loading rates. The tensegrity design improved effort, comfort, pain, and usability. Spring-loaded crutches reduced perceived stability and walking speed. Conclusion: The biologically inspired tensegrity crutches were an overall improvement to existing designs. Simulations and mechanical testing suggest that nonlinear stiffness, ground-following, and force feedback are among the beneficial mechanical properties that underlie this improvement.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于预应力自张力模块的张力结构拐杖可改善地面反作用力曲线、速度、用力程度、舒适度及主观稳定性
目的：美国有六百万人使用拐杖作为移动辅助工具。刚性设计缺乏轴向活动，限制了感觉反馈，易导致上肢关节二次损伤；弹簧式设计虽具顺应性，但可能牺牲稳定性。我们设计了一种受生物启发的张力结构拐杖，其包含顺应性模块，旨在实现理想的机械性能。终端模块为预应力自张力双胞腔张力结构。我们通过轴向加载机械测试、地面直行与转弯行走测试及使用者体验比较，将该张力结构拐杖与市售刚性及弹簧式拐杖进行对比。方法：在人体试验中，18名无近期下肢损伤的健康年轻成人以舒适自选步速完成直行与转弯试验，膝部阻塞器模拟主导腿单侧损伤。使用每种拐杖后，参与者报告其主观用力程度、舒适度、疼痛感、稳定性及可用性。结果：与刚性设计相比，弹簧式和张力结构设计均降低了峰值加载速率。张力结构设计提升了用力程度、舒适度、疼痛感及可用性。弹簧式拐杖降低了主观稳定性与步行速度。结论：受生物启发的张力结构拐杖整体优于现有设计。模拟与机械测试表明，非线性刚度、地面跟随性及力反馈是其性能提升的关键机械特性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of rigid and spring-loaded crutches—such as poor sensory feedback and secondary joint injuries—the study designed a biologically inspired tensegrity crutch with a pre-stressed self-tensile module to enhance compliance while preserving stability. In human trials with 18 healthy participants, the tensegrity crutch outperformed both rigid and spring-loaded models by reducing peak loading rates, improving perceived effort, comfort, pain, and usability, while also maintaining or enhancing walking speed and stability. Spring-loaded crutches, though compliant, were found to reduce perceived stability and speed, highlighting the tensegrity design’s superior mechanical balance.
为解决传统刚性及弹簧式拐杖在反馈不足和稳定性差等问题，本研究设计了一种仿生张拉整体结构拐杖，其末端模块为预应力自张力双单元结构。在18名健康青年参与者进行的步行试验中，该拐杖在降低峰值载荷率的同时，显著提升了使用者对用力感、舒适度、疼痛感和易用性的评价，且未降低步行速度与稳定性。相比之下，弹簧式拐杖虽具柔顺性，却降低了稳定性与速度，凸显张拉整体结构拐杖在柔顺性与力学反馈平衡上的优势。
An Empirical Study of Agent Skills for Healthcare: Practice, Gaps, and Governance
Authors: Gelei Xu, Ningzhi Tang, Xueyang Li, Toby Jia-Jun Li, Zhi Zheng, Wei Jin, Yiyu Shi
First: 2026-05-04T15:16:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T15:16:25+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Healthcare automation is shaped by local procedures and organizational constraints, so agent capabilities rarely transfer unchanged across settings. Agent skills, self-contained directories that package reusable procedures for AI agents, are emerging as a procedural layer for adapting healthcare agents across diverse healthcare settings. We present the first empirical analysis of healthcare agent skills, drawing on 557 healthcare-related skills filtered from 58,159 public skills on ClawHub and annotated along ten dimensions covering function, deployment context, autonomy, and safety. We find that public healthcare skills emphasize patient-facing workflow automation and monitoring rather than the diagnostic and treatment-oriented tasks foregrounded in healthcare-agent research; coverage of the healthcare lifecycle and specialized clinical inputs remains uneven; and general technical risk does not reliably capture clinical risk. These findings position healthcare skills as a procedural layer not yet addressed by current benchmarks and risk frameworks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：医疗保健领域代理技能的实证研究：实践、差距与治理
医疗保健自动化受本地流程和组织约束影响，因此代理能力很少能在不同环境中无变化地迁移。代理技能作为一种封装可复用流程的自包含目录，正逐渐成为适应不同医疗环境的医疗代理的程序层。我们首次对医疗代理技能进行实证分析，基于从ClawHub公开的58,159个技能中筛选出的557个医疗相关技能，并沿涵盖功能、部署环境、自主性及安全性的十个维度进行标注。我们发现，公开的医疗技能更侧重于面向患者的流程自动化与监控，而非当前医疗代理研究中强调的诊断与治疗任务；医疗生命周期覆盖及专业临床输入仍不均衡；通用技术风险无法可靠反映临床风险。这些发现表明，医疗技能作为程序层，尚未被现有基准和风险框架所涵盖。
Summary / 总结
This study addresses the lack of empirical understanding of how AI agent skills function in real-world healthcare settings, where local procedures and organizational constraints limit cross-setting transferability. The authors analyze 557 healthcare-related skills from 58,159 public skills on ClawHub, annotated across ten dimensions including function, deployment context, autonomy, and safety. Their findings reveal that public skills primarily focus on patient-facing workflow automation and monitoring, rather than diagnostic or treatment tasks emphasized in agent research; coverage of the full healthcare lifecycle and specialized clinical inputs is uneven; and general technical risk metrics fail to reliably reflect clinical risk. These results highlight a gap in current benchmarks and risk frameworks, positioning agent skills as a procedural layer requiring targeted development.
本研究旨在探索医疗场景中智能体技能的适用性与局限性，动机在于解决AI智能体在不同临床环境中的适应性问题。作者从ClawHub平台58,159个公开技能中筛选并标注557个医疗相关技能，覆盖功能、部署场景、自主性及安全性等十个维度。研究发现，公开技能主要聚焦于面向患者的流程自动化与监控，而非医疗智能体研究中强调的诊断与治疗任务；医疗生命周期覆盖及专业临床输入存在明显不均衡；通用技术风险指标无法可靠反映临床风险。这些结果揭示当前评估基准与风险框架尚未涵盖医疗智能体技能的关键需求。
Temporally Consistent Object 6D Pose Estimation for Robot Control
Authors: Kateryna Zorina, Vojtech Priban, Mederic Fourmy, Josef Sivic, Vladimir Petrik
Venue: IEEE Robotics and Automation Letters, vol. 10, no. 1, pp. 56-63, Jan. 2025
First: 2026-05-04T15:15:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T15:15:09+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://data.ciirc.cvut.cz/public/projects/2024TemporalPose/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Single-view RGB object pose estimators have reached a level of precision and efficiency that makes them good candidates for vision-based robot control. However, off-the-shelf methods lack temporal consistency and robustness that are mandatory for a stable feedback control. In this work, we develop a factor graph approach to enforce temporal consistency of the object pose estimates. In particular, the proposed approach: (i) incorporates object motion models, (ii) explicitly estimates the object pose measurement uncertainty, and (iii) integrates the above two components in an online optimization-based estimator. We demonstrate that with appropriate outlier rejection and smoothing using the proposed factor graph approach, we can significantly improve the results on standardized pose estimation benchmarks. We experimentally validate the stability of the proposed approach for a feedback-based robot control task in which the object is tracked by the camera attached to a torque controlled manipulator.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于机器人控制的时序一致物体6D位姿估计
单目RGB物体位姿估计器已达到足够的精度和效率，使其成为基于视觉的机器人控制的良好候选方案。然而，现成的方法缺乏稳定反馈控制所必需的时间一致性和鲁棒性。本文提出一种因子图方法，以强制物体位姿估计的时间一致性。具体而言，该方法：(i) 融入物体运动模型，(ii) 显式估计物体位姿测量的不确定性，(iii) 将上述两个组件集成到基于在线优化的估计器中。我们证明，通过适当剔除异常值并使用所提因子图方法进行平滑，可在标准化位姿估计基准上显著提升性能。我们通过实验验证了该方法在反馈式机器人控制任务中的稳定性，其中物体由安装在力矩控制机械臂上的相机跟踪。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for temporally consistent and robust 6D object pose estimation for reliable robot control, this work introduces a factor graph-based method that integrates object motion models and explicit pose uncertainty estimation into an online optimization framework. Experimental results on standard benchmarks show significant performance improvements over off-the-shelf methods, especially when combined with outlier rejection and smoothing. The approach is further validated in a real robot control task using a torque-controlled manipulator, demonstrating stable tracking of moving objects under visual feedback.
为满足机器人控制中对时序一致性和鲁棒性6D物体位姿估计的需求，本文提出一种因子图方法，将物体运动模型与位姿测量不确定性显式估计集成到在线优化框架中。实验表明，该方法在标准评估基准上显著提升位姿估计精度，并在使用力矩控制机械臂跟踪移动物体的实时反馈控制任务中表现出稳定性能。
SAIL: Structure-Aware Interpretable Learning for Anatomy-Aligned Post-hoc Explanations in OCT
Authors: Tienyu Chang, Tianhao Li, Ruogu Fang, Jiang Bian, Yu Huang
First: 2026-05-04T15:13:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-04T15:13:47+00:00
Comments: 16 pages, 6 figures, 9 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Optical coherence tomography (OCT), a commonly used retinal imaging modality, plays a central role in retinal disease diagnosis by providing high-resolution visualization of retinal layers. While deep learning (DL) has achieved expert-level accuracy in OCT-based retinal disease detection, its "black box" nature poses challenges for clinical adoption, where explainability is essential for clinical trust and regulatory approval. Existing post-hoc explainable AI (XAI) methods often struggle to delineate fine-grained lesion structures, respect anatomical boundaries, or suppress noise, limiting the trustworthiness of their explanations. To bridge these gaps, we propose a Structure-Aware Interpretable Learning (SAIL) framework that integrates retinal anatomical priors at the representation level and couples them with semantic features via a fusion design. Without modifying standard post-hoc explainability methods, this representation yields sharper and more anatomically aligned attribution maps. Comprehensive experiments on diverse OCT datasets demonstrate that our structure-aware method consistently enhances interpretability, producing clinically meaningful and anatomy-aware explanations. Ablation studies further show that strong interpretability requires both structural priors and semantic features, and that properly fusing the two is critical to achieve the best explanation quality. Together, these results highlight structure-aware representations as a key step toward reliable explainability in OCT.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SAIL：面向OCT解剖对齐后置解释的结构感知可解释学习
光学相干断层扫描（OCT）作为常用视网膜成像技术，在视网膜疾病诊断中提供高分辨率视网膜层可视化，发挥核心作用。尽管深度学习（DL）在基于OCT的视网膜疾病检测中已达到专家级精度，但其“黑箱”特性阻碍了临床应用，因为临床实践中可解释性对建立临床信任和获得监管批准至关重要。现有后置可解释人工智能（XAI）方法常难以精确描绘细微病灶结构、尊重解剖边界或抑制噪声，限制了其解释的可信度。为弥合这些差距，我们提出结构感知可解释学习（SAIL）框架，通过融合设计在表征层面整合视网膜解剖先验，并与语义特征耦合。无需修改标准后置可解释性方法，该表征即可生成更锐利且解剖对齐的归因图。在多个OCT数据集上的全面实验表明，我们的结构感知方法持续提升可解释性，生成具有临床意义且解剖感知的解释。消融研究进一步表明，强可解释性需同时依赖结构先验与语义特征，且二者恰当融合对实现最佳解释质量至关重要。这些结果共同强调，结构感知表征是实现OCT可靠可解释性的关键步骤。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for clinically trustworthy explanations in OCT-based retinal disease detection, where deep learning models remain opaque, this work introduces SAIL, a structure-aware interpretable learning framework that embeds retinal anatomical priors into model representations and fuses them with semantic features. Unlike existing post-hoc methods, SAIL enhances explainability without altering the underlying XAI algorithms, resulting in sharper, anatomy-aligned attribution maps. Experimental results across multiple OCT datasets confirm that SAIL significantly improves interpretability, producing explanations that respect anatomical boundaries and suppress noise, with ablation studies showing that both structural priors and semantic fusion are essential for optimal performance.
为提升OCT视网膜疾病诊断中的临床可解释性，本文提出SAIL框架，通过在表示层面融合视网膜解剖先验与语义特征，生成符合解剖结构的后处理解释。与传统XAI方法相比，SAIL无需修改现有解释器即可输出更清晰、更贴近解剖边界的归因图。在多个OCT数据集上的实验表明，SAIL显著提升解释质量，消融实验进一步证实结构先验与语义融合的协同作用对获得临床有意义的解剖感知解释至关重要。
